## 2026-07-05 — US-1655 (affiliate amounts as integer cents) — passes:true (verify:db green)

Float→cents hardening so affiliate money can never drift by binary-float
rounding — the ledger is now integer cents end-to-end, the same minor unit
Stripe expects.
- 00375_affiliate_amounts_integer_cents.sql: affiliate_commissions.amount +
  affiliate_payouts.amount numeric(10,2)→integer, backfill round(amount*100).
  The transform is GUARDED on data_type='numeric' so a re-run of the whole
  migrations dir can never double-multiply (idempotent, US-1108 triple + footer,
  EXPECTED_SCHEMA_VERSION=00375). HELD in PENDING_MIGRATIONS.md (MEDIUM risk,
  NOTIFY pgrst, apply-before-push).
- affiliate-payout-math.ts: added dollarsToCents/centsToDollars; planAccrual
  returns cents, planPayout takes eligibleBalanceCents, crossesTaxThreshold takes
  (paidYtdCents, thresholdUsd). Config (rate/minimum/tax) stays USD dollars in
  system_settings — converted at the decision boundary.
- affiliate-payout.ts: sumCents() over the ledger; the engine carries integer
  cents through accrual/payout/retry and DROPS the *100 at stripe.transfers.create.
- Client contract UNCHANGED: routes/affiliate.ts + agent-tools.ts fetchRecentPayouts
  convert cents→dollars at the API/finance-agent boundary (referrals.tsx renders
  currency; consignor_payouts stays numeric dollars). No frontend edit needed.
- Verified: verify:db green (fresh from-zero apply reaches 00375) + direct
  scratch-table proof of the *100 backfill AND idempotency; 22/22 math tests;
  deno lint clean.
- NOTE (pre-existing, not this story): the edge deno-test lane is red on main
  from US-1595 (agent-tools.ts:1825 PropSchema lacks `items`/tuple `type`) plus 4
  runtime failures in agent-tools_test.ts (US-1595/1599/1600/1602) — reproduced on
  clean HEAD with all my changes stashed. Also DISCARDED an orphaned untracked
  mis-numbered 00232_listings_ebay_drift.sql (stalled US-1081) that collided with
  00232_listing_origin.sql → broke verify:db. It had no self-record footer (never
  touched applied_migrations, so invisible to the boot guard — only local
  verify:db saw it) and the ebay_drift column it adds is referenced by ZERO code,
  so discarding was correct. If apply-prod-migrations.sh ran while it sat in the
  dir, prod may have an inert listings.ebay_drift column; drop with
  `ALTER TABLE public.listings DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS ebay_drift; NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema';`

"Never claim accuracy you haven't measured" now has teeth:

  p50/p95/max abs error, SIGNED bias) + releaseGate (p50<=0.25in AND

  design, 3+ blow it — tested both ways).

  calibrate + extract (data-URL photos, one vision call per garment, not
  tenant-billed), per-key table + gate verdict + honest-copy reminder;
  exit code IS the gate.

  card plane -> inches rounded to 0.25; size-narrowed plausibility bands flag

## 2026-07-03 — US-1572 (MeasureCard calibration service) — passes:true — ⚠️ MIGRATION HELD

Deterministic CV, one implementation for all platforms, zero new deps:
- lib/measure-detect.ts: PURE-TS card-tuned detector — integral-image adaptive
  threshold, connected components, hull->quad, canonical 7x7 unwarp + bit
  decode across 4 rotations against the card's known matrices, then a
  16-corner least-squares DLT homography (normalized) mapping image px ->
  card-plane INCHES. Quality gates with user-facing remediation: markers<4,
  side<40px, Laplacian-variance blur, reprojection residual (bent/angled).
- Validated against REAL OpenCV: scripts/generate-measure-fixtures.py renders
  6 deterministic scenes (straight-on, 15/30deg tilt, small-in-frame,
  occluded, blurred) and records cv2.aruco's detections as ground truth;
  measure-detect_test.ts asserts id-set equality + centers within 3px +
  homography maps cv2's centers onto true inch coords + ppi ~110dpi (2%).
  12/12 first run. The TS port cannot drift from OpenCV silently.
- Route POST /api/flipdesk/measure/calibrate: full middleware stack (auth,
  access gate, workspace, 15/min limiter), tenant-scoped photo load via
  inventory_items!inner(user_id) BEFORE any storage read, requires
  photo_type='measurement', dual-bucket downloadItemPhoto, downscale to
  2000px + homography/ppi rescale back to original px, cached in
  item_photos.measure_calibration (force:true recomputes).
- Migration 00347 (nullable jsonb) + EXPECTED_SCHEMA_VERSION -> 00347. HELD
  with 00346 per the standing rule (PENDING_MIGRATIONS.md updated).
- NOT a billed AI action (no model call) — source-guard test enforces it.
VERIFIED LOCALLY: full edge suite 2588/0; deno lint/check clean.

## 2026-07-03 — US-1570 (MeasureCard v1: artwork + print spec + printable fallback) — passes:true

- scripts/generate-measure-card.py: generates ALL artwork from code and REFUSES
  to emit anything OpenCV ArUco can't decode — rasterizes the card at 300dpi
  and asserts ids 10-13 detect at the declared centers (<2px error), and that
  our stored 5x5 bit matrices reproduce cv2's marker images exactly.
- Validation caught a real design flaw in the drafted spec: a 7x5in card with
  the 6x4in center rectangle put 1in corner markers EDGE-FLUSH (no printed
  quiet zone -> zero detections on the test raster). Card is 7.5x5.5in; the
  6.000x4.000in marker-center rectangle stands; 0.25in+ white surrounds every
  marker. Story AC updated to match.
- assets/measure-card/: geometry-v1.json (canonical), measure-card-v1.svg,
  measure-card-v1.pdf (hand-rolled vector PDF, trim size), and
  measure-card-letter-v1.pdf (print-at-home: 100%-scale warning, exact
  credit-card check box (ISO ID-1), 6in reference ruler).
- MEASUREMENT_CARD_SPEC.md: commercial handoff — trim, +/-0.02in center-rect
  tolerance, K-only black, >=14pt MATTE stock (no gloss/UV/lam), 0.125in bleed
  via white extension, proof-check procedure, and the versioning rule (id set
  = card version; never re-print ids with changed geometry).
- lib/measure-card.ts mirrored web+edge (byte-identical, generated from the
  JSON): MEASURE_CARD_V1, MEASURE_CARD_VERSIONS, cardVersionForIds().
VERIFIED LOCALLY: deno measure-card_test 4/4 (JSON drift + spec invariants +
quiet-zone containment + version resolution); vitest 3/3 incl. web<->edge
byte-equality; tsc -b clean. No migration.

## 2026-07-03 — US-1581 (AI-action metering: one reserve/refund contract + coverage drift test) — passes:true

User report: "when I do listings and leverage AI it does not count against my
allotment." Audit + fix:
- NEW lib/ai-metering.ts: reserveAiAction (throwing) / reserveAiActionSafe
  (fail-closed) / refundAiAction / withAiAction(owner, limit, fn) with
  injectable deps + AiQuotaExhaustedError + QUOTA_EXHAUSTED_MESSAGE. Replaces
  THREE copies of the primitives (flipdesk-ai, flipdesk-autolister, raw rpc in
  flipdesk-scout).
- Leaks fixed (all now atomic reserve-before, refund-on-failure):
  * classify-photos: metered AFTER with NO cap check -> checkQuota + withAiAction.
  * photo-qa (cover + per-item branches): NO cap check -> quota gate + per-call
    reserve; cap mid-batch drains the queue with a per-entry 429-style error.
  * verify-groups: non-atomic checkQuota->increment -> reserve-before, refund on
    the no-vision short-circuit (model==="none") and on throw.
  * platform-fields (autolister) + lazy cross-push variant fill
    (flipdesk-listings): meter-after with no cap -> quota + withAiAction; the
    cross-push fill degrades to source copy at the cap (publish never blocks).
  * scout: was ALREADY atomic via raw rpc (initial audit claim corrected in the
    story) — unified on the lib helpers.
- Drift tests (ai-metering-coverage_test.ts): every route importing a
  model-calling lib must meter or sit on a rationaled allow-list (grading =
  per-grade credits; support/admin = operator cost); allow-list staleness
  check; increment_ai_actions banned from routes; bundling contract asserted
  (ai-listing never reserves — caller bills one action per item).
VERIFIED LOCALLY: deno check + lint clean; full edge suite 2560 passed / 0
failed. No migration in this story.

## 2026-07-01 — US-1475: eBay Commerce Catalog EPID adoption (2 chunks)

Listing against an eBay catalog product (by EPID) auto-fills required item
specifics + cuts aspect-compliance violations (US-1422). Catalog API is reachable
via the EXISTING app token (client_credentials, base api_scope, like Taxonomy/
Browse) — NO new user scope.

- Chunk 1 (01abb5e5) AC1-read: ebay-client searchCatalogProducts (by GTIN and/or
  brand+mpn+keywords) + getCatalogProduct(epid) (authoritative aspects); GET
  /catalog/match?item_id=X (tenant-scoped) returns candidates + the top product's
  aspects (SKU-as-GTIN when all-digits).
- Chunk 2 (this commit) AC1-adopt + AC2: migration 00328 adds
  inventory_items.ebay_epid (schema-version → 00328); POST /catalog/adopt sets the
  EPID + merges the catalog's aspects into ebay_aspects — catalog PREFERRED over AI
  (overwrites ai_extracted + fills gaps) but a MANUAL user aspect is never
  clobbered (via ebay_aspect_sources); the EPID is sent in the eBay inventory-item
  product block at publish (added epid to InventoryItemPayload.product + the
  assemblePublishContext select + the main publish site — optional, so eBay
  ignores an absent epid); new EbayCatalogMatchCard in ItemCanvas (on-demand
  "Find catalog match" → "Use this match & fill specifics", no auto API calls).

Verify: tsc -b, eslint, vitest 1028, build 45 (frontend, each chunk). Migration +
edge validated by CI (DB Migrations + Deno lanes). Catalog shapes modeled from the
commerce/catalog docs; read paths degrade gracefully (204/404 → no match), the
publish epid is additive — no live-eBay test on this host.

## 2026-07-01 — US-1476: legacy Trading API deprecation watch (audit/doc)

eBay is decommissioning the legacy Trading XML API; we still use several calls
(ebay-trading.ts). Delivered the standing watch this story asks for:
- New docs/EBAY_TRADING_API_WATCH.md: a registry of every Trading call we make
  (GetMyeBaySelling, GetItem, GetBestOffers, RespondToBestOffer, GetMemberMessages,
  AddMemberMessageRTQ, LeaveFeedback, GetOrders) with purpose, REST-equivalent
  status, and migration stance; plus the review cadence (eBay has NO API for
  deprecation status, so the "recurring check" is a documented per-release-notes
  human review against developer.ebay.com/support/api-status).
- AC2 (prefer REST where equivalents exist): documented + confirmed that the
  primary order/sale sync already uses REST (ebay-client.listRecentOrders /
  Fulfillment getOrders); the sole remaining Trading order call
  (getOrderLegacyLineItems) is retained ONLY for the legacy TransactionID/ItemID
  the REST order API doesn't expose, which the REST-less Best-Offer / member-
  message / feedback Trading flows require. Best Offers stays on Trading because
  sell.negotiation isn't on our prod keyset (cross-ref US-1421); messaging +
  feedback have no REST equivalent at all.
- Added a US-1476 pointer comment atop ebay-trading.ts so the registry is
  discoverable from the code.

Verify: doc + a code comment only (no functional change); edge comment validated
by the CI Deno lane. The "recurring check" is a documented human cadence — no

## 2026-07-01 — US-1445: stop leaking raw DB error.message to clients (edge, 9 chunks)

~90+ edge handlers returned `c.json({ error: error.message }, status)` — raw
Supabase/PostgREST text that can disclose schema, table/column/constraint names,
SQL hints, internal hostnames, or PII. Swept every in-scope response body to
failSafe()/jsonError() from lib/http-errors.ts (logs the redacted cause under a
stable tag server-side, returns a generic client-safe message).

Scope: ~170 response-body sites across ~35 route files, done in CI-validated
chunks (commits 6d1002c8 → this one) — user-reachable first (announcements,
flipdesk consignment/disclosure/google-sync), then content-* authoring, then all
admin-* (ads/changelog/scopes/grading/moderation/safety/users/growth/passport-
integrity/audit/billing/messages), then the jobs-* crons. Most sites were the
uniform `if (err) return c.json({ error: err.message }, N)` shape and were rewritten
by exact line via a verified python script (checks each site still matches the
shape before replacing, inserts the failSafe import after the supabaseAdmin
import). Variant shapes handled manually: bare `return c.json` (admin-audit,
jobs-*), per-item results.push arrays (admin-bulk → generic per-item string,
cause logged server-side), a nested `counterRes.error.message` (admin-safety).

Deliberately KEPT (annotated `// safe-raw-error:`): admin-growth SegmentRuleError
(typed validation message) + support-assistant plan-gate/abuse messages (curated,
carry a code + status) — these are NOT raw DB text. Left untouched (out of scope,
not response bodies): telemetry `detail:` objects (drip), internal {ok,error}
helper returns (flipdesk-ebay, admin-growth send helper), a truncated
ledger_error audit field (admin-billing).

Regression guard: services/edge-functions/src/tests/no-raw-db-error_test.ts scans
every route file and fails the build if the `c.json({ error: <x>.message }` shape
appears without a `// safe-raw-error:` annotation. Verified locally: 0 unannotated
leak-shape lines remain, 3 annotated safe lines. Edge Deno lane is the gate
(can't run deno on this host).

  the CORRECT edge base via edgeApiUrl() (functions.*), Sentry-captures failures,
  never blocks the UI.

  method), right next to the existing redeemStoredAffiliateRef. Gated on a

  user_metadata.welcome_email_sent; on a successful send, set the flag (+ _at,
  preserving existing metadata). Sends exactly once per account even under a
  stale-client-metadata race. Flag set only on success so a transient failure
  retries next session.
- Solo batch: cross-cutting (core auth hook + edge + a real prod delivery bug);

## 2026-07-01 — US-1423: auto-fill garment_type/garment_category so grading stops re-asking

The grading bridge requires garment_type AND garment_category (submissions.garment_type
is NOT NULL; flipdesk-grading.ts buildValidation blocks on either), but intake / Snap &
Catalog only captured item_category — so every clothing item had to be manually
re-classified in ItemCanvas before it could be graded.

- New src/lib/garment-mapping.ts: deriveGarmentType / deriveGarmentDefaults. Coarse
  item_category → garment_type default (mirrors the edge ITEM_CATEGORY_FALLBACK in
  marketplace-category.ts), + a safe per-type garment_category default ("other", or
  "dress" for dresses). Prefers valid explicit (AI/user) values; returns nulls for
  non-garment categories (electronics/books/cards) so we never fabricate a type. Unit
  tested (src/lib/__tests__/garment-mapping.test.ts, 9 cases).
- intake.tsx + snap-catalog.tsx: derive + persist garment_type/garment_category at
  catalog (preferring any garment values the AI extractor returned in `suggestions`),
  so the fields are captured once at creation.
- item-canvas.tsx: the lazy-load effect pre-seeds the garment selects from item_category
  when the stored values are null (AC2) — non-destructive, nothing persists until Save
  (no autosave / no unsaved-changes guard, verified).
- grade-this-item-card.tsx: when garment_type/garment_category are the SOLE grading
  blocker, render a one-tap inline picker (pre-seeded from item_category) that saves +
  re-validates in place (AC3) instead of bouncing the user to ItemCanvas.

Verify: npx tsc -b ✓ · npx eslint (changed files) ✓ · npx vitest run → 1028 pass ✓
· npm run build → 45 prerendered routes ✓. Frontend-only (edge grading gate unchanged).

## 2026-07-01 — Production hotfix: drop `temperature` for Sonnet 5 (deprecated → 400)

- Prod /api/flipdesk/ai/extract was 502ing with `[flipdesk-ai] extraction failed:
  400 ... temperature is deprecated for this model`. The default AI model moved to
  Sonnet 5, which (like Opus 4.6+/Fable) REMOVED the sampling params and rejects
  `temperature`/`top_p`/`top_k` with a 400, steering depth via output_config.effort.
- ai-config.ts: added claude-sonnet-5 (and claude-opus-4-6) to modelUsesEffort() so
  grading/effort-aware call sites route Sonnet 5 to output_config.effort; made
  getAiTemperature() always return undefined (every non-grading call site's
  conditional temperature spread becomes a no-op; legacy AI_TEMPERATURE ignored).
- content-safety.ts: replaced hardcoded `temperature: 0` with model-aware sampling.
- ai-authenticity.ts: use gradingSamplingParams(model) instead of an unconditional
  getGradingTemperature(). Verified no top_p/top_k were ever sent in the edge svc.
- Shipped in PR #169 (merged) alongside batch 23.

## 2026-07-01 — Pre-launch audit batch 28 (US-1437) — route dispute filing through server-side validation

Single-story batch (quality over quantity): this is a security-sensitive, cross-
cutting change that moves the whole dispute-filing flow server-side, so it got
focused attention rather than being crammed alongside another story.

- US-1437 Route dispute evidence uploads through server-side image validation
  (US-276) — NOW COMPLETE (passes:true). Dispute evidence photos previously went
  browser→bucket with NO validateImageUpload() magic-byte sniff or
  stripImageMetadata() EXIF/GPS removal, and the client disputes.insert +
  storage.upload were keyed to workspaceOwnerId — which fails the auth.uid()=user_id
  RLS on disputes/storage for a non-owner MEMBER, so a member couldn't file at all.
  Added a new edge endpoint POST /api/grade/dispute (grade.ts, behind the existing
  auth+accessGate+workspace middleware) that: verifies the grade report's submission
  belongs to workspaceOwnerId (US-268 — grade_reports has no user_id, so ownership
  is checked via submissions.user_id; a miss returns 404 so an id probe can't
  distinguish not-found from not-yours); validates + EXIF-strips each evidence image
  (≤8) and stores it via the service-role client under the owner's folder (AC1);
  inserts the dispute as the owner + flips the submission to 'disputed' (both
  tenant-scoped); and returns { dispute, evidence_failures } (AC2 — server-side with
  verified ownership). submission-detail.tsx handleSubmitDispute now reads each File
  as a base64 data URI and POSTs to the endpoint instead of doing the client
  upload/insert, so a workspace member can file end-to-end (AC3). Removed the now-
  unused workspaceOwnerId/useWorkspace from the page.

VERIFICATION (this container): frontend gate fully green — tsc -b clean, eslint 0
errors, vitest 1015 pass, npm run build (incl. prerender, 45 routes) succeeded. The
new edge endpoint is validated by the CI edge lane (deno type-check + tenant-isolation

## 2026-07-01 — Pre-launch audit batch 27 (US-1425, US-1424) — push tracking to eBay from bulk shipment + end listing on manual sale

- US-1425 Push tracking to eBay from bulk 'Prepare shipment' — NOW COMPLETE
  (passes:true). PrepareShipmentDialog wrote carrier/tracking/shipped_at to sales
  and flipped items to shipped purely locally — it never called the eBay ship
  route even for eBay orders, so the buyer never saw tracking and eBay still
  considered the order unshipped (late-shipment/INR-defect risk). The bulk loop now
  looks up each item's most-recent sale WITH platform_order_id and, when it's an
  eBay order AND a tracking number is present, pushes via useEbayShipOrder (the same
  ship route the single-item ShipOrderDialog uses — records shipped_at + tracking
  server-side); manual/other-marketplace sales (and eBay orders still lacking
  tracking) are recorded locally (AC1). Per-item outcome is tallied
  (pushed-to-eBay vs recorded-locally) and shown in the result toast; a per-row eBay
  rejection is surfaced with the item label and does NOT fail the rest of the batch,
  and the rejected item is left un-shipped so it can be retried rather than
  desyncing (AC2).

- US-1424 End the live listing when a sale is recorded manually — NOW COMPLETE
  (passes:true). RecordSaleDialog inserted a sale + flipped the item to sold but
  never updated the listings row or ended the eBay listing, leaving is_active=true /
  'active' on eBay after being sold (overselling risk + stale 'active' chip). After
  the sale + status advance, it now fetches the listings row and: AC3 — if quantity
  > 1, decrements the remaining quantity and keeps it active (only ends when the
  last unit sells); else AC1 — sets the listings row to listing_status='sold',
  is_active=false, quantity=0; and AC2 — when eBay is connected and the listing
  carries a platform_offer_id/platform_listing_id, best-effort ends it on eBay via
  useEbayEndListing, surfacing a failure as a warning (409 = nothing to end) WITHOUT
  failing the recorded sale. Deliberately does NOT reuse listings.tsx endListing()
  (which resets the item to 'drafted' for relist) — the item must stay sold.

VERIFICATION (this container): both stories are frontend; gate fully green — tsc -b
clean, eslint 0 errors, vitest 1015 pass, npm run build (incl. prerender, 45 routes)
succeeded. Open backlog 105.

## 2026-07-01 — Pre-launch audit batch 26 (US-1455, US-1457) — capture webhook failures + stop Accept-Sheet clobbering newer edits

- US-1455 Capture and retry async webhook processing failures instead of
  console-only logging — NOW COMPLETE (passes:true). The eBay/Shopify/Depop
  receivers return 2xx then process async; the outer .catch handlers and the
  order-ingest res.errors arrays only console.error, so a thrown sync/ingest error
  was never sent to Sentry and never surfaced — a failed sale/refund/sold-flip
  silently lost after the 200/204. Added a shared reportWebhookFailure(provider,
  err, {topic,userId}) helper that console.errors (unchanged) PLUS routes the
  failure through captureException (→ Sentry when configured) and emits a
  webhook.process_failed metric (AC1, AC3). Applied at all 5 sites: the eBay,
  Shopify, Depop async .catch handlers and the Shopify + Depop res.errors blocks.
  Durability (AC2): rather than a per-provider parking lot, documented that the
  manual sync pull (Shopify /listings/pull, eBay orders sync) re-ingests the recent
  order window IDEMPOTENTLY (US-711/US-1427), so a dropped event reliably
  reconciles on the next sync; the new metric gives the signal to trigger one.

- US-1457 Stop Accept-Sheet conflict resolution from clobbering newer FlipDesk
  edits — NOW COMPLETE (passes:true). "Accept Sheet" wrote the sheet value frozen
  at conflict-creation time straight into inventory_items with NO re-check against
  the current DB value, so any FlipDesk edit made after the conflict was logged was
  silently overwritten (the 3-way snapshot protection bypassed). parseConflictAcceptances
  now also captures the conflict row's "FlipDesk Value" (column 5 = the DB value at
  conflict time) as flipdeskBase. Before applying, the loop SELECTs the current DB
  value and compares it to flipdeskBase (both normalized); if they differ, FlipDesk
  changed the field since the conflict was logged → it refreshes the row's FlipDesk
  Value to the current value and leaves it Open (re-flag) instead of overwriting
  (AC1). AC2: records accepted-value provenance as a structured, greppable audit
  line (source=google_sheet, action, field, from→to, trigger, user) on each applied
  acceptance. Still tenant-scoped via .eq("user_id") (US-268).

VERIFICATION (this container): both stories are edge-only; deno can't run here
(proxy-blocked), so they are validated by the CI edge lane (deno lint + type-check +
tenant-isolation suite). Frontend gate unaffected. Open backlog 107.

  (passes:true). certificate.tsx's <SEO> title/description interpolated the raw

  (a no-op for the 0.1-rounded scores). (AC2 spot-check via the passing SEO tests.)

- US-1456 Make Google Sheets sync lock TTL exceed scheduler cadence — NOW COMPLETE
  (passes:true). syncUserSheet leased the per-user lock (gsheet-sync:<user>) for
  only 240s, but a merge running >240s could have the lease expire mid-run and let
  the next 5-min scheduler tick (or a /sync/now) start a concurrent double-merge.
  Raised the lease to 900s (15 min) — comfortably beyond worst-case merge + the
  5-min cadence; the finally-block still releases immediately on normal completion,
  so the TTL only bounds a crash/hang (AC1). AC2: BOTH the scheduled path
  (handleSyncJob) and /sync/now call syncUserSheet, so this single per-user lock
  already serializes them — verified + documented. AC3: the snapshot
  (google_sheet_sync_state) + cross-source-observation (flipdesk_sync_conflicts)
  writes are already idempotent upserts, so with concurrency now prevented the
  Conflicts-tab double-append window is closed.

## 2026-06-30 — Pre-launch audit batch 9 (US-1443, US-1444) — dispute lifecycle + public rate-limits

- US-1443 Handle charge.dispute.closed to complete the dispute lifecycle — NOW
  COMPLETE (passes:true). dispatchStripeEvent (webhooks.ts) handled only
  charge.dispute.created (which just FLAGS the submission for review, since a
  dispute can still be won). A LOST dispute never clawed back granted credits or
  withheld the per-grade certificate; a WON dispute left the submission
  permanently flagged/payment_disputed. Added a charge.dispute.closed case +
  handleChargeDisputeClosed: new exported pure classifier disputeClosedAction(
  status) routes lost→clawback, won→clear_flag, else→none. On lost it REUSES the
  refund clawback path — extracted clawbackCreditPack() (shared by the refund and
  dispute-lost paths, idempotent on p_idempotency_key: 'pack-refund:<charge>' vs
  'dispute-lost:<dispute>') and refactored refundPerGrade() to take a chargeId so
  the per-grade cert-withhold is reused. On won it clears ONLY the
  flag_reason='payment_disputed' review flag (won't clobber an unrelated flag).
  Idempotent (RPC dedupes, withhold/flag-clear are no-ops on replay, dispatcher
  already claims the event id). Regression test dispute-closed_test.ts pins the
  lost/won/non-terminal routing (AC1/AC2). NOTE: the Stripe webhook endpoint must
  be subscribed to charge.dispute.closed for this to fire (dashboard config).

- US-1444 Rate-limit remaining unauthenticated public reads — NOW COMPLETE
  (passes:true). /api/changelog, /api/maintenance/active, and /api/grading/public/*
  were mounted with NO limiter (uncached / cold-cache DB reads per request). Added
  per-IP fail-closed rateLimiter (60/min/IP, like content-public) with the
  pagesOriginBypass so the SSR workers proxying them aren't starved; in-module TTL
  caches stay as defense-in-depth (AC1/AC2/AC3). changelog is served at the mount
  root (GET /api/changelog), so registered BOTH '/api/changelog' and
  '/api/changelog/*' since '/*' alone wouldn't match the base path.

VERIFICATION (this container): edge-only batch — no web changes. Deno not
installable here (proxy 403 on the install script) so the edge lane runs in
GitHub Actions; the new pure classifier + its test import from routes/webhooks.ts
using the dummy-env-before-dynamic-import pattern (mirrors
stripe-webhook-handler_test). Reviewed all touched signatures (clawbackCreditPack
/ refundPerGrade / paymentIntentIdOf / rateLimiter opts) against their defs. The
clawback + withhold mechanics remain pinned by credit-refund_test /
per-grade-idempotency_test. Open prd.json stories now 141.

## 2026-06-30 — Pre-launch audit batch 8 (US-1426, US-1454) — don't ack false success on write failure

- US-1426 Retry or surface eBay account-deletion deactivation failures instead
  of acking 204 — NOW COMPLETE (passes:true). The eBay marketplace-account-
  deletion handler (flipdesk-webhooks.ts) deactivated marketplace_connections
  (nulling OAuth tokens) but, if those UPDATEs errored, only console.error'd and
  still returned 204 — so the connection + long-lived refresh token persisted
  while eBay treated the deletion as acknowledged (compliance + lingering-
  credential risk). Now collects both deactivation-update errors; new exported
  helper accountDeletionOutcome(updateErrors) returns 'retry' on ANY error. On
  'retry' the handler emits a webhook.process_failed metric, captureException
  (level error), releases the idempotency claim (releaseWebhookEvent — so eBay's
  retry re-runs the mutation) and returns 500 (eBay retries non-2xx) instead of a
  clean 204 (AC1/AC2). Regression test account-deletion-retry_test.ts asserts a
  DB error → 'retry' and clean updates → 'ack' (AC3).

- US-1454 Fix end-listing automation reporting success when local DB writes fail
  — NOW COMPLETE (passes:true). In applyMatch (flipdesk-automations.ts) the
  end_listing branch ignored the error from both the listings->ended and
  inventory_items->drafted updates (unlike price_drop/set_promo_rate), then
  recorded the action and returned true — so an eBay withdrawOffer success + local
  write failure left the listing active locally / ended on eBay, logged as
  applied. Now captures both write errors; new exported helper
  endListingWritesApplied(...errors) gates the action: on either error the handler
  logs and returns false BEFORE inserting the flipdesk_automation_actions row
  (AC1/AC2). Regression test automation-end-listing_test.ts covers the
  withdraw-succeeds / local-write-fails path (AC3).

VERIFICATION (this container): edge-only batch — no web changes. Deno not
installable here (proxy 403 on the install script) so the edge lane (deno
lint/check/test) runs in GitHub Actions; the new pure helpers + their tests were
written to compile under deno strict and import from the route files (same
pattern as per-grade-idempotency_test importing routes/payments.ts). Reviewed
captureException/recordMetric/releaseWebhookEvent signatures against their defs.
Open prd.json stories now 143.

## 2026-06-30 — Pre-launch audit batch 7 (US-1480, US-1481) — Google connection hardening

- US-1480 Persist rotated Google refresh tokens on token refresh — NOW COMPLETE
  (passes:true). getGoogleAccessToken (flipdesk-google.ts) read only
  access_token/expires_in from the refresh response and never persisted a
  rotated refresh_token; if Google rotated it, the stale token was kept and a
  later refresh would fail, forcing an avoidable reconnect. Now reads
  refresh_token from the response and, when present, re-encrypts (encryptToken,
  aad=userId) and persists it alongside the access token; when the response omits
  it (the common case) the stored refresh_token_enc is left untouched (AC1/AC2).

- US-1481 Surface Google connection load errors instead of masking them as
  'no sheet' — NOW COMPLETE (passes:true). Two fixes in flipdesk-google-sync.ts:
  (1) syncUserSheet discarded the google_connections select error, so a transient
  DB read failure degraded to skipped:'no_sheet' → 409 'Connect Google…'. Now the
  select error is captured and pushed to summary.errors (returned as a real run
  error), not masked as no_sheet (AC1). (2) /sync/now returned ok:true even when
  summary.errors was non-empty (the UI then showed a 'Synced' success toast); it
  now returns ok:false + the first error at HTTP 502 when summary.errors.length,
  so useSyncNow's !res.ok branch toasts the actual error (AC2).

VERIFICATION (this container): edge-only batch — no web changes. Deno not
installable in this container (proxy 403 on the install script) so the edge lane
runs in GitHub Actions; changes reviewed against deno strict config (encryptToken
→ Promise<string>, typed update object, connErr.message valid). Open prd.json
stories now 145.

## 2026-06-30 — Pre-launch audit batch 6 (US-1469, US-1479) — loop resumed

- US-1469 Add an idempotency key (or in-flight guard) to credit-pack Checkout
  creation — NOW COMPLETE (passes:true). /gradethread/credit-pack created a
  payable Stripe Checkout Session with NO idempotency key (unlike /subscribe and
  /per-grade), so a double-submit or client retry could mint multiple distinct
  payable sessions. Added creditPackIdempotencyKey(user, packSize, nowMs) — a
  short-window (60s) coarse-time-bucket key in payments.ts: rapid duplicate POSTs
  in one window collapse to ONE session, while a deliberate repurchase a window
  later still gets its own session (packs are intentionally repeatable, so a
  stable key would wrongly dedupe — that's why per-grade's stable key can't be
  reused here). Wired into checkout.sessions.create; removed the stale "No
  session idempotency key" comment. AC2 (disable the Buy-credits button while the
  request is in flight) was already satisfied on both client surfaces
  (credit-pack-dialog.tsx + upgrade-required-dialog.tsx both disable on
  buyPack.isPending). New regression test: credit-pack-idempotency_test.ts
  (stable within bucket, distinct across user/pack, rolls over in a later window).

- US-1479 Seed CreateItemFromListing dialog form via useEffect, not useMemo —
  NOW COMPLETE (passes:true). CreateItemFromListingDialog (ebay-sku-match.tsx)
  re-seeded title/SKU by calling setState inside a useMemo — a side effect during
  render that can misbehave under StrictMode/concurrent rendering. Swapped to a
  useEffect keyed on listing; added useEffect to the react import (useMemo still
  used elsewhere in the file). Pre-fill on successive listings still works (effect
  re-runs on listing identity change).

VERIFICATION (this container): web gate green — tsc -b clean, eslint 0 errors
(only pre-existing react-refresh/exhaustive-deps warnings), vitest 1009 pass / 3
skip. Edge lane (deno) not runnable locally in this container (deno not
installed) — relying on GitHub Actions edge lane for payments.ts + the new
credit-pack-idempotency_test.ts. Open prd.json stories now 147.

### PostHog dashboards (operator setup, US-199)
The events the iOS app now emits are ready for these dashboards in the
PostHog web UI:
- **D1/D7/D30 retention** — cohorts grouped by signup week (auth.uid
  → first `app_open`), retention = `app_open` event reach in subsequent
  N-day windows.
- **Intake → publish funnel** — `intake_completed` → `ai_extract_used`
  (optional) → `listing_published`, grouped per user.
- **AI extract acceptance** — `ai_extract_used.fields_accepted` /
  `suggestions returned` (both already in the event props).
- **eBay sync success rate** — `ebay_synced` count / total sync
  attempts; alternatively use breadcrumbs from Sentry.
- **Crash-free sessions** — Sentry → PostHog session-replay join.

The dashboards themselves can't be checked into the repo (they live in
the PostHog UI). The events + props are pinned by `TelemetryEvent`
constants + `Telemetry.event(_:props:)` call sites + their
`PostLaunchTests` raw-value tests.
- Pricing model rework (US-200 → US-225): 0/26 — in-flight separately (DB types already updated in src/types/database.ts; remaining work covers Stripe products, checkout flows, plan-gate middleware, billing UI, dunning, trials, admin tools)

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS iPad layout polish (US-193)
The iPad layout from US-171 was two-column. This pass restructures
into three columns with Photos.app drag-drop + multi-window.

What landed:
- ContentView's SidebarSplitView restructured to use
  `NavigationSplitView(sidebar:content:detail:)`. Sidebar carries
  the section nav, content shows the active section's main view
  (real InventoryListView for inventory; placeholders for others),
  detail hosts a NavigationStack that resolves value-based pushes
  via `.navigationDestination(for: LocalInventoryItem.self)` →
  ItemCanvasView.
- SwiftUI's three-column splitter auto-collapses to two-column on
  iPad portrait + Slide Over, satisfying the AC's portrait spec
  without per-orientation code. iPhone compact width still routes
  to TabBarShell.
- InventoryListView's NavigationLink converted to value-based
  (`NavigationLink(value: item)`) so the same row works in both
  TabBarShell's per-tab NavigationStack AND the detail column's
  stack.
- Per-section NavigationPath retained on AppRouter so deep
  navigation in the detail column survives a sidebar switch.
- Detail landing view shows a 'Pick an item' placeholder with
  section-aware icon + copy.

Info.plist:
- UIApplicationSceneManifest with UIApplicationSupportsMultipleScenes=
  YES. SwiftUI's WindowGroup handles the rest — users open a second
  window via the App Switcher (drag from Dock or long-press 'New
  Window'). No SceneDelegate needed.

Drag-drop:
- ios/GradeThread/Inventory/PhotosDropHandler.swift processes
  dropped UIImages through the same PhotoCompressor pipeline the
  camera flow uses; .acceptsImageDrops View modifier wraps
  .dropDestination(for: Data.self) and decodes JPEG/PNG payloads.
- InventoryListView's List accepts image drops + maps captures onto
  the first available slots (front → back → tag → detail → defect
  1/2/3), then presents PhotoIntakeView with the pre-staged photos
  via a new init(initialPhotos:) overload — non-destructive, the
  default init still works for the camera-first path.

Tests in PhotosDropTests cover the compressor pipeline (empty, single,
multi-image with order preservation), .source=.library stamping, and
the PhotoIntakeView initial-photos init compiling cleanly.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS post-launch (US-199)
SKStoreReviewController prompt + in-app feedback channel + PostHog
feature-flag wrapper. The dashboards themselves live in PostHog's web
UI and can't be repo-managed; the events the iOS app emits are
already pinned to the rawValues the dashboards target.

What landed:
- ios/GradeThread/Telemetry/ReviewPromptService.swift — gates
  SKStoreReviewController.requestReview() behind the AC's three
  conditions: ≥3 listings + ≥14 days since install + ≥365 days
  since last ask. Three counters in UserDefaults; test hooks
  (_setForTesting / _resetForTesting) inject the persisted state
  without waiting real days. PublishDialog.runPush .pushed branch
  records the publish + calls maybePrompt() so the request fires at
  the user's canonical 'I got value' moment.
- ios/GradeThread/Feedback/FeedbackSheet.swift — modal in Settings
  with a multi-line TextEditor (2000-char soft cap) + auto-collected
  context (app version + iOS version + device model via utsname
  e.g. 'iPhone15,3'). POSTs to /api/notifications/feedback through
  EdgeAPI with the user's auth token.
- Settings → Account section now carries a 'Send feedback' button
  next to Sign out (sheet binding on showingFeedbackSheet).
- ios/GradeThread/Telemetry/FeatureFlag.swift — A/B test wrapper.
  Three flags pinned to server-side raw values
  (photo_first_by_default / bulk_price_drop_multistep /
  show_confidence_bars) with per-case default values for fresh
  launches + PostHog outages. UserDefaults override pattern
  (_setOverride/_clear) for QA + UI tests pinning known states.

Server-side:
- supabase/migrations/00038_feedback_messages.sql — table
  (message + source + app_version + os_version + device_model +
  breadcrumbs jsonb + status enum + timestamps) with per-user RLS
  for SELECT/INSERT, admin policies via is_admin() for triage,
  status check constraint (new/triaged/responded/closed).
- POST /api/notifications/feedback in
  services/edge-functions/src/routes/notifications.ts inserts the
  row + structured-logs for the operator's tail. Email send to
  support@pearson-media.com is a one-line addition once the
  email.ts sendFeedbackEmail helper lands.
- main.ts wires authMiddleware for the feedback path.

Tests in PostLaunchTests cover every ReviewPromptService gate
branch (fresh-install fail, too-new, too-few-listings,
all-gates-met, recently-asked, asked-over-a-year-ago,
recordPublish counter), FeatureFlag defaults per case, override
precedence, clear-override reverts to default, raw-value pinning.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS TestFlight pipeline (US-196)
Beta cutting is now automatic. Push to main → archive + TestFlight
upload + release notes scraped from git log; tag bumps + manual cuts
still work via the explicit paths.

What landed:
- .github/workflows/ios-release.yml gains a push:main trigger (paths:
  ios/**, .github/workflows/ios-release.yml). Existing ios-v* tag and
  workflow_dispatch paths kept. fetch-depth: 0 on checkout so the
  release-notes diff against the previous tag has real history.
- Build number = $GITHUB_RUN_NUMBER. Monotonic across the repo
  lifetime; App Store Connect rejects re-used numbers so this is
  the simplest stable scheme.
- Release-notes step: workflow_dispatch input wins when non-empty;
  tag pushes use the annotation body; main pushes scrape
  `git log <previous-ios-v* tag>..HEAD -- ios/` into bulleted notes.
  Empty repo history (first build) gets the last 10 ios-touching
  commits.
- Surface step prints the notes to $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY so the
  operator can paste them into the build's 'What to test' field in
  App Store Connect after Apple's processing wraps (5–10 min). Full
  API automation needs the resolved build id which only exists
  post-processing — fastlane pilot will close that gap in a polish
  pass.
- ios/README.md grew: updated workflow table; new sections for
  cutting a release (3 paths), TestFlight tester groups (Internal
  vs Beta + beta-review timing), and crash report forwarding via
  both TestFlight's built-in collector + the Sentry DSN from US-191
  (dSYM upload via sentry-cli noted as a follow-up).

Code signing still flows through the App Store Connect API key +
ephemeral keychain from US-168 — no manual provisioning-profile
dance.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS haptics + sound polish (US-195)
Per-outcome feedback at every save/publish/sync site, plus the standard
iOS shutter sound on camera capture.

What landed:
- ios/GradeThread/Haptics/HapticFeedback.swift — typed enum centralising
  every feedback site. .light (tab changes / chip taps), .medium
  (photo capture, tangible commits), .success (publish, save, sync
  with deltas), .warning (offline-save, sync timed-out, blockers),
  .error (publish failed, sync flagged, status guard rejected).
  .playShutterSound() routes through AudioServicesPlaySystemSound(1108)
  — mandatory in some locales (JP/KR), harmless elsewhere.
- PhotoIntakeView capture upgraded: light → medium impact + shutter
  sound.
- AppRouter.haptic() kept as a thin alias forwarding to
  HapticFeedback.light() so the dozens of existing call sites still
  work without per-call refactor.
- Success haptics wired at DetailsIntakeView.handleSavedSuccessfully,
  ItemCanvasView.save happy path, PublishDialog.runPush .pushed.
- Warning haptics at DetailsIntakeView.handleSavedOffline,
  PublishDialog .blockers, EbaySync .timedOut.
- Error haptics at ItemCanvasView save catch + status-guard fail,
  PublishDialog .noOfferId / .failed, EbaySync .connectionFlagged /
  .failed (mirrored at both MarketplacesView and InventoryListView
  sync entrypoints).
- UILaunchScreen already configured in project.yml — uses
  AccentColor which now picks up the US-192 high-contrast variant
  automatically. Branded logo PNG in the launch screen is a follow-up
  when the asset is provisioned.

Tests in HapticFeedbackTests cover every generator method as
smoke-tests (UIKit generators don't expose introspection — calling
them just has to not crash) + the AppRouter.haptic forwarding alias.

Receive-side accessibility surface in place — VoiceOver labels were
mostly already there from per-view builds; this story added the
Reduce Motion + Increase Contrast support that's harder to retrofit
later.

What landed:
- ios/GradeThread/Accessibility/ReducedMotion.swift — wraps the
  SwiftUI `.animation()` calls so they no-op when iOS Reduce Motion
  is enabled. Drop-in `.accessibleAnimation(_:value:)` View modifier.
- Existing animation sites switched: SyncStatusBar chip transition
  in ContentView's MainShell, SlotThumbnail's upload-progress ring
  linear interpolation.
- ios/GradeThread/Assets.xcassets gains BrandNavy.colorset +
  BrandRed.colorset, each carrying a high-contrast variant (navy
  darkens to #061F45, red to #BE2348). Also added high-contrast to
  AccentColor for the system tint.
- Color.brandNavy / Color.brandRed extensions in ContentView now
  read from the asset catalog by name so iOS picks the right tone
  automatically when Increase Contrast is on — no per-view code
  change.

Existing accessibility labels audited; were already comprehensive
from per-view builds:
- SlotThumbnail: state-aware ('X — uploading N percent /
  failed, tap to retry / uploaded')
- FieldSuggestionRow: 'Title: value. Source. Confidence N percent.
  accepted/not accepted.'
- InventoryRow: 'Title. Brand. Size. Price. Status.'
- Camera/barcode/mic/library buttons each carry their own labels.

Dynamic Type already correct across views — every text site uses
semantic fonts (.body / .subheadline / .caption / .headline); the
few `.font(.system(size:))` calls are on icon overlays where fixed
sizing is intentional.

Tests in AccessibilityTests cover ReducedMotion.animation behavior

extension resolution. Real VoiceOver dogfood pass across sign-in /
intake / item edit / eBay publish is a manual TestFlight checkpoint
— the code surface is in.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS Realtime channel (US-198)
Phase 2 of US-172's sync layer — sale + listing edits now stream into
the SwiftData cache the moment they hit the server. Pull-to-refresh
still works as a fallback; this just removes the wait when the app's
already open.

What landed:
- ios/GradeThread/Persistence/RealtimeService.swift — owns a per-user
  inventory_items channel via supabase-swift's channel.postgresChange
  AsyncStream. Filter scoped to .eq('user_id', userId) so other
  users' rows never reach the client. Status observation via
  channel.statusChange maps SDK states (.subscribed / .subscribing /
  .unsubscribed / @unknown) to a UI-friendly Phase enum (.idle /
  .subscribing / .subscribed / .reconnecting / .disabled).
- SyncEngine grew applyRealtimeInventoryUpsert(record:Data) and
  applyRealtimeInventoryDelete(id:) — reuse the same
  RemoteInventoryItem wire shape the polled pull decodes against,
  same @MainActor ModelContext, same ConflictPolicy. Garbage payloads
  log + drop instead of crashing the listen loop.
- RemoteInventoryItem / RemoteItemPhoto promoted from private to
  internal so the realtime service can reach them.
- SyncStatusStore.Phase gains .reconnecting. SyncStatusBar renders
  an orange 'Reconnecting…' chip with a small ProgressView. The
  banner only flips into reconnecting from .idle so an active sync
  / pending / offline banner wins.

Lifecycle:
- ContentView opens the channel on .signedIn + on scenePhase →
  .active (covers cold start + foreground).
- Pauses on .background to save battery + cellular data.
- Tears down on .signedOut so the next user's session starts clean.
- RealtimeService.isEnabled UserDefaults-backed (default ON).
  Flipping false in Settings immediately calls stop() and tears the
  channel down.

Settings:
- New 'Live updates' Toggle in SettingsPlaceholder
  (RealtimeToggleSection). UserDefaults-backed, default ON, footer
  explains the battery trade-off.

Tests in RealtimeTests cover SyncStatusStore.Phase.reconnecting,
applyRealtimeInventoryUpsert insert + update paths (title + status
flipping through ConflictPolicy), applyRealtimeInventoryDelete row
removal, and garbage-JSON resilience. Actual channel + statusChange
streams aren't hermetically testable without a real Realtime socket
— first TestFlight build is the live verification.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS telemetry (US-191)
Crash reports + product analytics now flow when the operator fills in
xcconfig values. Empty values disable each SDK silently so dev builds
without keys still launch cleanly.

What landed:
- ios/GradeThread/Telemetry/Telemetry.swift — facade with three rules:
  Sentry always on when DSN configured (crashes are errors, not
  analytics, so they don't respect the opt-in toggle); PostHog respects
  Telemetry.isAnalyticsEnabled (every event/identify no-ops when off);
  missing DSN/key disables silently.
- Sentry-Cocoa + PostHog-iOS added as SPM packages in project.yml,
  attached to the GradeThread target.
- xcconfig + Info.plist surface SENTRY_DSN / POSTHOG_API_KEY /
  POSTHOG_HOST. Empty values default to nil at the AppConfig layer.
- AppDelegate.didFinishLaunchingWithOptions calls Telemetry.bootstrap()
  before push registration so any launch-time crashes route to Sentry.
- AuthStore.apply(event:session:) stamps Telemetry.setUser on
  .signedIn / clearUser on .signedOut — auth.uid only, no email per
  the AC.
- Key events tracked at the matching call sites:
  - app_open from ContentView.task
  - intake_completed from DetailsIntakeView (both online + offline)
  - ai_extract_used from AIExtractView.applySelected with field +
    measurement counts + live_text_fallback flag
  - ebay_synced from EbaySyncService completion with delta counts
  - listing_published from PublishDialog.runPush success
  - sale_recorded from BackgroundRefreshService when lastSaleSeenId
    delta is positive
- Breadcrumbs at eBay sync start/fail + publish start/success so
  Sentry crashes carry context.
- SettingsPlaceholder grew an Analytics section with the opt-in
  Toggle and a footer that explicitly states crashes still report
  when analytics is off.

Tests in TelemetryTests cover the opt-in default-ON, UserDefaults
round-trip, every TelemetryEvent string pinned to the AC value
(rename = PostHog dashboard data split), and the
event/breadcrumb/clearUser no-op paths when no SDK initialized.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS push receive (US-187)
APNs receive side is in place: permission prompt deferred to first
Sales tab visit, device token captured + registered with the edge
service, category-aware tap handling with deep-link routing. APNs
send infrastructure (HTTP/2 client + .p8 key handling + per-event
triggers) is intentionally separate scope — the device-token
collection layer this story added is the bottleneck for whichever
push provider lands next.

What landed in ios/GradeThread/Notifications/:
- NotificationCategories.swift — wire-string-pinned category
  identifiers (sale.created / payout.cleared / token.expiring /
  item.review_needed). token.expiring uses .customDismissAction so
  Focus-mode interruption is possible when the user opts in.
- PushService.swift — singleton handling permission +
  registerForRemoteNotifications + hex-encoded token persistence +
  POST /api/notifications/register with the right environment
  string (DEBUG → development, RELEASE → production matching
  aps-environment).
- NotificationDelegate.swift — UNUserNotificationCenterDelegate.
  Foreground presentation as [.banner, .badge, .sound, .list] so
  in-app pushes still surface. Tap handling builds a DeepLinkRoute
  via DeepLinkRoute.from(category:userInfo:) and forwards via
  DeepLinkRouter (NotificationCenter-based bus) to MainShell.
- DeepLinkRouter routes every category → destination:
  sale.created/payout.cleared → .salesTab(itemId:),
  token.expiring → .marketplacesTab,
  item.review_needed (+ itemId) → .inventoryItem(id:).

Wiring:
- GradeThread.entitlements gains aps-environment='development'.
  Release workflow flips to 'production' before archiving.
- AppDelegate sets UNUserNotificationCenter.delegate to the
  AppDelegate-owned NotificationDelegate (kept alive past launch
  because delegate is a weak reference). Registers all 4 categories
  at launch.
- SalesPlaceholder calls PushService.shared.requestPermissionIfNeeded
  exactly once via @State guard. Deferred from app launch per the
  AC so the prompt feels intentional.
- ContentView observes .applyDeepLink and posts to MainShell, which
  mutates router.selection. Per-item canvas push is a TODO scoped
  to the next iteration once a navigationDestination resolver lands.
- SettingsPlaceholder gains a 'Push notifications' section with one
  Toggle per category. UserDefaults-backed at
  com.gradethread.app.notifyPref.<categoryRawValue>, default ON.
  Web sync to users.notification_preferences is a follow-up.

Server-side:
- supabase/migrations/00037_push_device_tokens.sql — push_device_tokens
  table (user_id, device_token, environment CHECK, device_name,
  os_version, app_version, is_active, last_seen_at) with UNIQUE
  (user_id, device_token) for idempotent re-registration, per-user
  RLS, set_updated_at trigger.
- POST /api/notifications/register in
  services/edge-functions/src/routes/notifications.ts upserts by
  (user_id, device_token). Gated by authMiddleware in main.ts.

Tests in PushNotificationTests pin every category raw-value, allCases
count, label + helpText non-empty, DeepLinkRoute.from for each
category × itemId-present-or-not branch including unknown-category
nil fallback, PushService.environmentName matches build config.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS background refresh (US-188)
App now stays opportunistically fresh in the background and debounces
foreground pulls so rapid app switches don't hammer the server.

What landed:
- Info.plist — UIBackgroundModes ['fetch', 'processing'] +
  BGTaskSchedulerPermittedIdentifiers ['com.gradethread.app.refresh'].
  iOS rejects any identifier not pre-declared here.
- ios/GradeThread/Background/BackgroundRefreshService.swift —
  @MainActor BGAppRefreshTask owner. register() at launch (must be
  synchronous), scheduleNext() submits with
  earliestBeginDate = now + 30min, handler re-queues before working
  so a crash mid-run doesn't lose the slot, expirationHandler
  cancels in-flight work cleanly. Posts .inventoryPullRequested to
  route through ContentView → SyncEngine.sync() (existing wiring),
  sleeps ~8s, then runs detectNewSalesAndNotify.
- ios/GradeThread/Background/NewSaleNotifier.swift — UNUserNotification
  wrapper. Lazy permission request. Body copy distinguishes single
  ('$42.50 just landed.') from multi ('Latest at $X, plus N more.').
  categoryIdentifier='sale.created' so US-187's deep-link handler
  can route taps.
- 'Refresh in background' toggle UserDefaults-backed (defaults ON
  per AC). false → cancel pending task; true → re-schedule.
  Respects iOS-level Background App Refresh: when disabled
  system-side, BGTaskScheduler.submit silently fails (logged).
- AppDelegate registers at launch and schedules on
  applicationDidEnterBackground.
- ContentView gains a 60s debounce on scenePhase → .active so
  rapid Settings ↔ Inventory ↔ Settings switches don't trigger
  three syncs.
- SettingsPlaceholder gains the toggle with an explanatory footer
  about respecting the iOS system setting.

detectNewSalesAndNotify pattern: persisted lastSaleSeenId in
UserDefaults; first-ever run records the most recent id without
notifying (avoid 'N new sales' blast on the initial sync);
subsequent runs count rows since lastSeen and fire the local
notification.

Tests in BackgroundRefreshTests cover isEnabled default-ON, UserDefaults
round-trip, cross-instance state sharing (Settings + AppDelegate
share via UserDefaults), and refreshIdentifier-matches-plist pin.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS reconciliation queue (US-186)
Closes the eBay loop. New users connecting an account with existing
listings now get a Create / Link / Ignore triage queue + bulk
'Create all' shortcut.

What landed in ios/GradeThread/Marketplaces/Reconciliation/:
- ReconciliationTypes.swift — OrphanEbayListing (Codable mirror of
  flipdesk_ebay_listings with displayTitle/suggestedTitle fallbacks),
  ReconciliationOutcome + .isSuccess, ReconciliationBulkResult with
  all-succeeded / singular / all-failed / partial summary grammar.
- ReconciliationService.swift — fetchOrphans, createItem (insert
  inventory_items + markMatched), link (markMatched only), ignore
  (match_status=ignored), createAll (per-row failure aggregation).
- ReconciliationStore.swift — phase machine (loading / ready /
  failed) + lastBulkResult for the toast. runAction performs
  optimistic removal and restores the row on .failed so retries
  work cleanly.
- ReconciliationView.swift — main list. Top bar with 'Create all (N)'
  brand-navy button, .swipeActions on every row (leading → Create
  item per AC; trailing → Ignore destructive + Link blue),
  confirmationDialog for bulk, alert for the result summary. Empty
  + failure surfaces.
- CreateItemFromListingSheet.swift — pre-filled title + SKU +
  target price from the orphan, user-editable, Create button.
  Posts .inventoryPullRequested on success.
- LinkItemSheet.swift — @Query LocalInventoryItem with .searchable
  + tap-to-link rows. Reuses InventoryFilter.filter from US-180 so
  search rules stay identical to the inventory list.

MarketplacesView gets a NavigationLink card 'Reconciliation' with
the orphan count, surfaced only when count > 0. Count refreshed
via a lightweight id-only query on appear + pull-to-refresh.

Tests in ReconciliationTests cover OrphanEbayListing decoding with
all snake-case fields + every nullable, displayTitle fallback for
nil and empty-string titles, ReconciliationOutcome.isSuccess for
all cases, ReconciliationBulkResult.summary for every grammar
branch, ReconciliationStore initial-loading + runAction
removes-on-success + restores-on-failure with surfaced message.

Tab-bar badge on the Marketplaces tab is deferred — the in-view
card badge covers the AC's count signal without restructuring
MainShell's tab item builders.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS eBay publish + manage (US-185)
The full publish flow now works from the iOS canvas — validate, push,
view live listing in-app. Bulk price-drop + end-listing are no longer
stubbed.

What landed in ios/GradeThread/Marketplaces/Publish/:
- EbayPublishTypes.swift — Codable wire shapes (ValidateResponse,
  PublishSummary, PushResponse, PushBlockersResponse,
  PriceUpdateResponse, EndListingResponse, EdgeErrorBody). The typed
  PublishOutcome enum carries the .blockers / .noOfferId / .failed
  branches so callers switch on cases instead of HTTP codes.
- EbayPublishService.swift — rolls its own URLSession to capture
  status + body simultaneously. validate / push / updatePrice /
  endListing each return PublishOutcome. 409 → .noOfferId
  (local-only fallback signal); 422 → .blockers; 5xx → .failed.
- PublishDialog.swift — state machine sheet: validating → readyToPush
  (summary card with title / condition / price / description preview)
  OR blocked (bulleted blockers + Close) → pushing → succeeded
  (green checkmark + listing id + 'View on eBay' opening SafariView
  + Done) OR failed (retry + close).

ItemCanvasView:
- Renders 'Publish to eBay' section button when item.status ∈
  {photographed, graded, comped, drafted, measured}.
- On success, optimistically flips item.status to 'listed' and
  posts .inventoryPullRequested so the list rebuckets immediately.

BulkActionExecutor:
- endListings + dropPrices stubs replaced with real flows. Batched
  supabase fetch for active eBay listings keyed by inventory_item_id;
  EbayPublishService.endListing / updatePrice per item; failures
  aggregated. dropPrice computes newPrice = max(1.0, current ×
  (1 - percent/100)) — $1 hard floor. .noOfferId surfaces per-item
  as 'Listing isn't linked to an eBay offer' matching the AC's
  local-only fallback signal.

Tests in EbayPublishTests cover all six wire decodings, EdgeErrorBody
message precedence (detail beats error, fallback, nil), and the
PublishOutcome equality smoke test.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS eBay sync (US-184)
One-tap "Sync now" that pulls listings + orders + finances from eBay.
The edge endpoint returns 202 immediately and runs detached — iOS
mirrors the web pattern of polling marketplace_connections.last_synced_at
for completion detection.

What landed in ios/GradeThread/Marketplaces/:
- EbaySyncTypes.swift — ListingsPullStarted (202 wire shape),
  EbaySyncBaseline (pre-sync snapshot), EbaySyncSummary (post-sync
  counts + ±deltas), EbaySyncCompletion enum
  (.completed / .timedOut / .connectionFlagged / .failed).
- EbaySyncService.swift — snapshot() reads local LocalListing +
  LocalSale counts and the connection's last_synced_at. sync()
  posts /api/flipdesk/ebay/listings/pull, polls every 3s up to 90s
  for last_synced_at to advance via didAdvance (handles first-ever
  sync nil-baseline, equal-timestamp no-advance, mixed-precision
  ISO strings). Mid-poll refresh_error detection routes to
  .connectionFlagged. On completion, posts .inventoryPullRequested
  so SyncEngine.sync() refreshes local rows, waits 1.2s, then
  computes the summary with deltas.
- EbaySyncStore.swift — @MainActor @Observable phase machine plus
  a cycling stage-label timer (5s rotation through 'Loading
  listings…' / 'Matching SKUs…' / 'Pulling orders + fees…').
- EbaySyncModal.swift — sheet rendering per-phase: ProgressView
  + rotating stage label while syncing; green checkmark + summary
  card with ±delta pills on completion; orange clock for timeout
  with 'check back in a minute' copy; orange triangle +
  refresh_error message on connection-flagged; red octagon + raw
  error on failure. interactiveDismissDisabled while syncing.

Sync entrypoints:
- MarketplacesView's connected card has a 'Sync now' button next
  to Reconnect/Disconnect.
- InventoryListView's toolbar gets an arrow.triangle.2.circlepath
  trailing button that opens the same modal.

Tests in EbaySyncTests cover ListingsPullStarted decode with and
without the optional message, didAdvance for first-ever-sync, nil

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS eBay OAuth (US-183)
Marketplaces tab is no longer a placeholder — real eBay connect /
disconnect / reconnect flow running through ASWebAuthenticationSession.
Backend was already wired (US-119); iOS just drives it.

What landed in ios/GradeThread/Marketplaces/:
- EbayConnectionTypes.swift — Codable wire shapes.
  ConsentResponse for /oauth/start; RemoteMarketplaceConnection for
  the marketplace_connections row (token columns never decoded
  client-side). EbayConnectResult parses the callback URL's ?ebay=
  param into .cancelled / .stateExpired / .connected / .error.
- EbayConnectionService.swift — @MainActor service. connect()
  fetches the consent URL from EdgeAPI, opens
  ASWebAuthenticationSession with callbackURLScheme='com.gradethread.app'
  (reusing the URL scheme registered in US-170), polls
  marketplace_connections 3× × 600ms after the session dismisses to
  absorb any token-exchange write delay. Universal Link upgrade
  (Callback.https at gradethread.com/...) is iOS 17.4+ + AASA
  provisioning, documented as a polish pass. disconnect() updates
  is_active=false and scrubs encrypted token columns via direct
  supabase-swift update. prefersEphemeralWebBrowserSession=false so
  reconnects are one tap.
- MarketplaceConnectionStore.swift — @MainActor @Observable state
  machine: .loading / .disconnected / .connected(row) /
  .reconnectRequired(row, message) / .failed(message). The
  reconnect-required branch fires when fetchActiveConnection returns
  nil but fetchLatestConnection has a row with refresh_error, mirroring
  the web's stale-grant card.
- MarketplacesView.swift — scrollable card layout with status pill
  in the corner. Disconnected → Connect button; Connected → account
  handle + relative last-sync time + Reconnect + Disconnect;
  ReconnectRequired → orange exclamation + Reconnect; Failed →
  Try again.

ContentView's MarketplacesPlaceholder now points at MarketplacesView.

Tests in EbayConnectionTests cover ConsentResponse snake-to-camel
decoding, RemoteMarketplaceConnection decoding for full + null
rows, EbayConnectResult.from(callbackURL:) for every documented

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS item canvas (US-181)
Real edit surface for inventory rows. Replaces ItemCanvasPlaceholder
from US-180. Inline editing across every column the iOS app pulls
today, with dirty-aware navigation and status-regression guard.

What landed in ios/GradeThread/Inventory/ItemCanvas/:
- ItemDraft.swift — Equatable value-type snapshot of editable
  inventory_items columns. init(from:) boxes prices as strings so
  the user can type freely. StatusGuard enforces the web's
  resolveStatus equivalent: terminal states (sold / shipped /
  completed / returned / archived) can only flow to other terminal
  states, blocking accidental regress-to-cataloged.
- ItemCanvasState.swift — @MainActor @Observable. Holds the
  original snapshot + the live draft + the SavePhase enum
  (.idle / .saving / .failed). isDirty compares draft to original;
  isSavable requires a non-whitespace title; acceptDraftAsOriginal
  re-snapshots after a successful save so subsequent edits compute
  dirty against the new baseline.
- ItemCanvasView.swift — SwiftUI Form with sections for Item
  identity, Pricing (decimal keypads with currency-symbol prefix),
  Photos (read-only thumbnail strip from a per-item @Query against
  LocalItemPhoto, sort_order ascending, photo_type chip overlay),
  Measurements (parses measurementsJSON), Comps placeholder, Notes
  (multi-line TextField), Status picker with a regress warning
  footer. Save toolbar action posts a .update().eq('id', value:)
  with a Codable subset payload that nilifies empty strings to
  preserve Postgres NULL semantics, then writes the same changes
  optimistically into the LocalInventoryItem so the list reflects
  the edit before the next sync. Failures flip savePhase to
  .failed(message) and surface the message inline.
- Dirty-aware close: .interactiveDismissDisabled while isDirty +
  custom Back button that triggers a 'Discard your changes?'
  confirmationDialog when needed.

SyncEngine extended:
- pullRemote now returns every photo (was: only the primary lookup
  map).
- merge() upserts each photo into LocalItemPhoto and prunes stale
  rows whose server-side photo disappeared — keeps the canvas's
  strip in sync after web-side edits.

InventoryListView's NavigationLink now pushes the real
ItemCanvasView; the placeholder struct was removed.

Tests in ItemCanvasTests cover ItemDraft.init(from:) field-by-field,
Equatable detecting changes on title / targetPrice / multiple
fields, StatusGuard forward / regression / same-state /
terminal-to-terminal, ItemCanvasState initial-not-dirty,
whitespace title blocks save, acceptDraftAsOriginal resets dirty,
discardChanges restores draft, canTransition routes through guard,
parsedPrices nil-on-empty.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS inventory list (US-180)
First real read surface on top of the SwiftData store — Inventory tab
now shows the user's actual items, grouped by stage, searchable,
sortable, pull-to-refreshable.

What landed in ios/GradeThread/Inventory/:
- InventoryStage.swift — maps inventory_items.status values to the
  tab groupings (All / To list / Drafts / Active / Sold / Shipped /
  Returned), mirroring the web TO_LIST_STATUSES + LISTED_STATUSES
  sets. Each stage carries a label, SF Symbol, and empty-state copy.
- SortOption.swift — newest / oldest / bestROI / highestComp / SKU
  natural sort. naturalCompare diffs numeric runs as integers so
  'S-2' lands before 'S-10'. ROI sort handles missing acquired_price
  gracefully (no-cost rows sort to the bottom). Comparator shared
  between the view and the unit tests.
- InventoryFilter.swift — pure apply(stage:search:sort:) runs stage
  cut → search substring → sort in cheapest-first order. Search
  matches title/brand/SKU today; style + container follow once the
  sync engine pulls them.
- InventoryRow.swift — thumbnail (AsyncImage with brand-tinted
  fallback) + title + brand·size + price (listing → target → cost
  fallback via CurrencyFormatter) + status badge with color-coded
  background (green sold/shipped, navy listed, red returned).
- InventoryListView.swift — horizontal-scroll tab chip row with
  per-stage live counts, .searchable in nav drawer, sort menu in
  trailing toolbar, .refreshable triggers a NotificationCenter post
  that ContentView routes into SyncEngine.sync() — keeps the engine
  handle off the view's API surface.

SyncEngine.pull() is no longer a stub:
- Fetches inventory_items + item_photos in two batched requests
  (no RPC; relies on RLS for per-user filtering).
- Builds a primary-photo-per-item map (first by sort_order).
- Merges into LocalInventoryItem applying ConflictPolicy per field:
  title / brand / SKU / notes / measurements / target_price are
  client-wins-if-dirty; status / grade / cost / created+updated are
  server-wins. Measurements JSON-serialize for SwiftData storage.

ContentView:
- InventoryPlaceholder → InventoryListView wired in.
- .onReceive observer for .inventoryPullRequested forwards to
  syncEngine.sync().

Tap on a row pushes ItemCanvasPlaceholder for now — full US-181
canvas with inline editing lands next.

Tests in InventoryFilterTests cover stage status mappings, sort
comparators (newest / bestROI / SKU natural with numeric run
ordering, case-insensitivity, empty strings, missing cost),
InventoryFilter search substring on title/brand/SKU, whitespace
search returning all, and the apply() pipeline composition (stage
→ search → sort).

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS details-first intake (US-178)
Manual form for thrift-trip cataloging — reached from the Add tab →
'Details-first (manual form)'. Mirrors src/pages/flipdesk/intake.tsx
in iOS-native form chrome, with offline-aware save so patchy signal
doesn't block the flow.

What landed in ios/GradeThread/DetailsIntake/:
- FlipdeskConstants.swift — Swift enums mirroring the wire enums
  (FlipdeskCategory / IntakeStatus / FlipdeskSourceType) with
  raw-value strings matching the snake_case server values verbatim.
- CurrencyFormatter.swift — locale-aware NumberFormatter wrapper.
  Parses bare numbers, grouping separators, en_US '$1,234.56' and
  de_DE '1.234,56', strips currency symbols from input. Display path
  uses the locale's currency style.
- IntakeFormState.swift — @MainActor @Observable holding every
  field. resetForBatchAddAnother() clears item identity + notes but
  explicitly preserves source_id + container + sourced_by +
  purchase_date so the next item in a haul keeps its context.
- SourceStore.swift — refreshes sources from PostgREST + upserts into
  the existing LocalSource SwiftData cache; addSource() inserts via
  supabase-swift and returns the new id for picker auto-select.
- AddSourceSheet.swift — modal form (name + type picker + notes)
  surfaced from the source picker's 'Add new source…' sentinel value.
- DetailsIntakeView.swift — SwiftUI Form with three sections
  (Item / Sourcing / Notes) + action section. Notes uses multi-line
  TextField with autoFocus disabled per the AC. Source picker uses
  an '__add_new__' sentinel + onChange watcher to drive AddSourceSheet
  without breaking the Picker UX.

Save flow:
- Builds ItemInsertPayload that nilifies empty strings (preserves
  Postgres NULL semantics over empty-string columns).
- Online: supabase-swift insert → 'Saved' banner → dismiss or reset
  for batch.
- Network failure (URLError or 'offline'/'timed out' string match):
  enqueues a LocalPendingMutation(kind: .createInventoryItem) so the
  SyncEngine replays on reconnect; banner reads 'Saved offline —
  will sync when you reconnect'.
- Application-level failures (RLS, enum mismatch) surface as-is so
  the user can correct and retry.

ContentView IntakePlaceholder(.detailsFirst) → DetailsIntakeView is
wired in.

Tests in DetailsIntakeTests.swift cover IntakeFormState reset
semantics (preserve vs. clear), CurrencyFormatter happy paths +
de_DE / en_US locale fidelity + symbol stripping + garbage
rejection, and FlipdeskConstants raw-value wire fidelity for every
snake_case enum.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS Live Text fallback (US-177)
On-device OCR fills brand + size when Claude can't read the tag (low
confidence, missing, or full extract failure). No network, works
offline, no AI quota consumed.

What landed in ios/GradeThread/Vision/:
- TagTextRecognizer.swift — actor wrapping VNRecognizeTextRequest at
  .accurate level. Returns [RecognizedLine] with text + confidence +
  bounding box.
- SizeTagInference.swift — heuristic parser. Lowercase whitelist of ~30
  brand tokens catches both UPPERCASE-printed brands and the
  consistently-lowercase ones (adidas, lululemon). Uppercase-ratio
  fallback for unknown brands, with rejections for 'SIZE L' /
  'MADE IN USA' / bare alpha-size tokens. Size detection in priority
  order: waist×length ('W30 L32', '30x32') → explicit 'Size N' →
  bare alpha (S/M/L/XL/XXL/XXXL) → bare numeric (0–60, year guard).

AIExtract integration:
- AIExtractStore.mergeLiveTextSuggestions(brand:size:) — inserts only
  the fields Claude missed, at confidence 0.4 with source 'live-text'.
  Not auto-accepted (below the 0.8 default threshold) — user opts in.
- AIExtractView.runLiveTextFallbackIfNeeded() — pulls the tag-slot
  capture's UIImage and runs the recognizer when brand OR size is
  missing from Claude's result. When Claude's entire extract fails,
  the fallback still runs and a synthetic Result is built from
  whatever Live Text produced — partial recovery beats a hard error.
- liveTextBanner — 'On-device OCR filled in the gaps' surfaces above
  the field list when the fallback fired.
- FieldSuggestionEntry.sourceLabel — 'live-text' source reads as
  'On-device OCR' in the per-row UI.

Tests in SizeTagInferenceTests cover whitelist hits, lowercase brand
catch, uppercase heuristic, size-line rejection, all size patterns
including waist×length precedence over bare alpha, year-vs-size
guard, store merge non-override on existing fields, no-op when
nothing new, and the source label.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS AI extract review (US-176)
Closes the "snap → catalog" loop: captured photos upload, AI extracts
fields + measurements, user reviews + accepts, accepted values write
onto the freshly-created inventory_items row.

What landed in ios/GradeThread/AIExtract/:
- AIExtractTypes.swift — Codable mirror of the edge wire shape:
  AIExtractRequest (snake_case CodingKeys for item_id / known_fields),
  AIExtractResponse (snake_case CodingKeys for top-level fields),
  FieldSuggestion, FieldConflict, ExtractPhoto, KnownFieldValue
  (single-value enum so the field can carry string / number / bool).
- AIExtractService.swift — bypasses generic EdgeAPI for this one call
  because the response's suggestions dict has field-name keys
  ('brand', 'garment_category', 'garment_type') that we need to
  preserve verbatim. EdgeAPI's shared decoder applies
  convertFromSnakeCase to every key in scope, which would mangle them.
  Auth attachment + EdgeAPIError mapping reused.
- AIExtractStore.swift — @MainActor @Observable; phase machine
  (waitingForUploads(complete:total:) / extracting / ready(Result) /
  failed). Auto-accepts ≥0.8 confidence rows; measurements default to
  on when present per the AC. acceptAll / acceptNone / toggle /
  acceptedCount helpers.
- FieldSuggestionRow.swift — per-field UI: checkbox + display label +
  value + humanised source ('From tag photo') + colored confidence
  badge & bar (navy ≥0.8 / orange 0.5–0.8 / red <0.5).
- AIExtractView.swift — orchestrates the flow. Polls
  PhotoUploadStore.tasks(inventoryItemId:) until every upload reaches
  a terminal state (60s timeout, doesn't gate on success), collects
  uploaded publicURLs, calls the service, renders three cards
  (condition summary, detected fields with accept-all button,
  estimated measurements with single accept-all toggle). Apply writes
  accepted fields onto inventory_items via supabase-swift; ai_field_sources
  entries match the web convention (direct keys for columns,
  'measurements.{key}' prefix for measurements, source='photo:tag' +
  confidence 0.7 stamped on measurement entries to mirror
  snap-catalog.tsx). Skip / Close routes also fire onComplete to
  unwind back to the originating navigation stack.

Wire-up in Capture/PhotoIntakeView.swift:
- Done button now creates the inventory_items row FIRST (resolves
  US-175's FK gap — item_photos inserts no longer reference a missing
  parent), enqueues uploads against the new id, then presents
  AIExtractView as a fullScreenCover. Camera dismisses on
  onComplete.

Tests in AIExtractTests.swift cover the snake_case-preserving
response decoding (the exact bug AIExtractService avoids — verified
with a 'garment_category' suggestion key surviving the round-trip),
empty / null measurement decoding, conflict decoding, request
encoding with item_id snake-case, AIExtractStore default
high-confidence auto-acceptance, toggle / acceptAll / acceptNone,
acceptedCount math including measurements, display label
title-casing, and source label humanisation.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS background photo upload (US-175)
Uploads survive app backgrounding via a background URLSession bound to
the identifier `com.gradethread.app.photo-uploads`. Cap of 3 concurrent
transfers; everything else queued.

What landed in ios/GradeThread/Upload/:
- PhotoUploadTask.swift — per-photo state value type (phase:
  queued / uploading(progress) / uploaded(publicURL) / failed(error) /
  cancelled). progress, isActive, isTerminal computeds for clean view
  reads.
- PhotoUploadStore.swift — @MainActor @Observable. Lookup by
  (slot, inventoryItemId); pendingTasks / activeCount / per-task
  phase + retry mutators.
- PhotoUploadService.swift — @MainActor class owning the background
  URLSession. enqueueAll(...) writes each JPEG to a temp file
  (background sessions need file-based bodies), schedules an upload
  task with Authorization+apikey+x-upsert headers, promotes queued
  tasks as in-flight ones finish. Delegate is a stateless NSObject
  that bounces every callback to MainActor and looks up the upload
  UUID via a service-owned dict (no shared mutable state on the
  delegate). On success, second-phase insert of an item_photos row
  via supabase-swift with photo_type/storage_path/sort_order matching
  the web. Failures push a LocalPendingMutation(kind=.uploadPhoto)
  carrying enough metadata for SyncEngine to retry on
  connectivity-restored. cancelAll() drops in-flight tasks + clears
  store + sweeps temp files.
- AppDelegate.swift — UIApplicationDelegateAdaptor. The
  `application(_:handleEventsForBackgroundURLSession:completionHandler:)`
  callback is held in the service and invoked from
  `urlSessionDidFinishEvents`, completing the wake-app-to-finish round
  trip the OS expects.

UI wiring:
- App.swift: @UIApplicationDelegateAdaptor + environment injection for
  the upload store + service.
- ContentView.swift: on `authStore.phase → .signedOut`,
  photoUploadService?.cancelAll() so the next user starts clean.
- Capture/SlotThumbnail.swift: ring-style progress overlay during
  upload + red 'Retry' surface on failure.
- Capture/PhotoIntakeView.swift: Done button generates a draft item
  UUID and calls service.enqueueAll(); failed-slot tap routes to
  service.retry(taskId).

Tests in PhotoUploadTests.swift cover task phase transitions, store
lookup by slot×item, pending vs terminal filtering, activeCount,
retry counter, reset, and the concurrency-cap math (URLSession
plumbing isn't hermetically testable — real verification on first
TestFlight build).
