## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS camera capture (US-173)
Custom AVFoundation camera with a 4-slot guided flow (front/back/tag/detail)
plus up to 3 optional defect slots. Custom UI throughout — no
UIImagePickerController, no system photo picker (that comes in US-174).

What landed in ios/GradeThread/Capture/:
- PhotoSlotType.swift — enum of 7 positions with label / hint /
  SF Symbol / serverPhotoType mapping (defects collapse to the wire enum
  'defect'; slot order preserved separately via sort_order on
  item_photos).
- PhotoCapture.swift — Identifiable struct holding the compressed JPEG +
  thumbnail UIImage + capturedAt + source (camera / library).
- PhotoCompressor.swift — aspect-preserving resize to 2048px max long
  edge, JPEG 0.8, 160px thumbnail; stripExifPreservingOrientation()
  whitelists only the orientation tag for the library-import path.
- PhotoIntakeStore.swift — @MainActor @Observable; photos dict by slot,
  activeSlot cursor, defectSlotsVisible (0–3), recordCapture auto-
  advances to nextEmptySlot.
- CameraSession.swift — @MainActor wrapper around AVCaptureSession + back
  camera input + AVCapturePhotoOutput. Configuration runs on a private
  serial queue (per Apple guidance — touching session config from main
  can stutter startup). Permission flow handles every
  AVAuthorizationStatus branch and surfaces a clear "open Settings"
  screen on denial.
- CameraPreview.swift — UIViewRepresentable; the hosted UIView's
  layerClass IS AVCaptureVideoPreviewLayer (cheaper than a sublayer +
  frame tracking).
- SlotThumbnail.swift — thumbnail / empty-state / active-ring / checkmark
  badge. Accessibility labels reflect captured / active / empty.
- PhotoPreview.swift — fullScreenCover with retake / delete buttons.
- PhotoIntakeView.swift — full-bleed preview, exit-confirmation dialog
  when hasUnsavedShots, active-slot hint, horizontal scroll strip with
  tap-to-focus + long-press-to-delete, plus tile to reveal one more
  defect slot, large shutter button with haptic + ProgressView during
  capture.

Wired into the Add tab via IntakePlaceholder(.photoFirst) → PhotoIntakeView.
The "Library" button position is reserved in the capture row layout for
US-174's PHPickerViewController landing.

Tests in PhotoIntakeTests.swift cover PhotoCompressor resize math + small-
image short-circuit + size budget; PhotoIntakeStore cursor advance,
defect reveal cap + hidden-slot guard, clearPhoto re-focus; PhotoSlotType
required set + defect-collapse to wire enum. CameraSession itself isn't
unit-tested — it owns hardware resources that need a real device; CI
just verifies it compiles.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS persistence + tab polish (US-171, US-172)

### US-171 — tab bar polish
ContentView.swift's MainShell now adapts: TabView at compact horizontal
width, NavigationSplitView at regular (iPad full-screen). One AppRouter
state powers both layouts so selection + per-tab navigation paths survive
the switch.

The Add tab is special: tapping it never actually selects — a custom
Binding<AppSection> intercepts the .add value, fires
UIImpactFeedbackGenerator(.light), shows a confirmationDialog ('Photo-first
(Snap & Catalog)' / 'Details-first (manual form)'), and returns without
mutating selection. The picked IntakeRoute is appended to whichever tab's
NavigationPath was active. Real photo-first/details-first flows land in
US-173 / US-178; for now both route to a TabPlaceholder.

Haptics fire on every tab change (both compact + regular layouts). iPad
sidebar has a dedicated 'Add' toolbar button that drives the same dialog.

### US-172 — SwiftData + sync engine
**Deployment-target bump 16.0 → 17.0.** SwiftData is iOS 17+ and the Core
Data fallback path the AC suggested isn't worth the maintenance cost
given current iOS adoption. Documented as a deviation from US-168's
'iOS 16+' target.

Models in ios/GradeThread/Persistence/Models/: LocalInventoryItem,
LocalItemPhoto, LocalListing, LocalSale, LocalSource, LocalPendingMutation
— each @Model class mirrors its Supabase row plus a `hasLocalChanges`
flag for offline writes and `localBytesPath` on photos for queued
captures.

Persistence/SyncEngine.swift is an actor:
- pull() — pulls items_full/listings/sales from Supabase (stubbed at the
  IO boundary; merge step + conflict policy fully implemented and tested).
- flushPending() — drains LocalPendingMutation queue. Handlers stub to
  retryCount++ until US-175 (photo upload) and US-178 (intake form) land.
- Connectivity-driven loop: NetworkMonitor (NWPathMonitor wrapper)
  exposes an AsyncStream<Bool> that the engine subscribes to from
  start(); path-satisfied flushes the queue + pulls.

ConflictPolicy splits fields three ways — server-wins for
marketplace-owned (price, status, grade), client-wins-if-dirty for
user-edited (title, brand, notes, target_price, measurements),
newest-write-wins for neutral fields decided by `updatedAt`.

@MainActor @Observable SyncStatusStore drives SyncStatusBar:
'Syncing…' / 'N pending' / 'Offline' / hidden. Banner renders atop
MainShell with an ease-in-out transition.

App.swift wires the ModelContainer (cloudKitDatabase: .none — sync goes
through Supabase, not iCloud). ContentView starts the SyncEngine the
moment the user signs in, kicks a pull on every scenePhase → .active.

Tests in SyncTests.swift cover all three ConflictPolicy branches,
LocalPendingMutation round-trip through an in-memory ModelContainer,
unknown-kind detection (forward-compat for future mutation types), and
SyncStatusStore phase transitions including the "don't override
.syncing while pending updates arrive" edge.

syntax, so first CI run after merge is the real check.

## Session Notes (2026-05-27) — iOS Share Extension (US-189)
Users can now send 1-8 images from Photos.app (or any share sheet
vending public.image) straight into a FlipDesk intake.

What landed:
- ios/ShareExtension/ is its own XcodeGen target. Bundle id
  com.gradethread.app.share-extension. project.yml declares it as
  type: app-extension and the GradeThread target picks it up via
  dependencies.target with embed: true so CI bundles it.
- App Group group.com.gradethread.app on both targets' entitlements
  is the only handoff channel — no shared frameworks needed.
- ShareViewController loads attachments via NSItemProvider and
  hosts a SwiftUI ShareIntakeView through UIHostingController. The
  view shows a thumbnail + slot picker per image, defaults each
  image to the next required slot then spills into Defect 1-3.
- Tapping 'Add' JPEG-encodes at 0.8 quality and writes a batch via
  IntakeInbox.writeBatch — UUID directory + photo-N.jpg files +
  manifest.json (id / createdAt ISO-8601 / per-photo slot + size).
- ios/Shared/IntakeInbox.swift is compiled into both targets. The
  reader pendingBatches() sorts ascending by createdAt so multi-
  share sessions process in user order; subdirs without manifest
  are silently skipped so corrupted writes don't 404 the rest.
- ShareInboxConsumer is main-app-only: pulls the next batch,
  decodes JPEGs back to UIImage, builds 240x240 thumbnails, maps
  PhotoSlotType raw strings to typed cases (unknown slots drop),
  first-write-wins preserves order when a slot is doubled.
- MainShell drains on .task + every scenePhase=.active, presents
  PhotoIntakeView in a fullScreenCover (DrainedBatch is Identifiable
  via the batch UUID), tail-recurses after dismiss to walk pending
  batches.
- 'Sign in first' fallback is free: MainShell only mounts when
  authStore.phase = .signedIn. The inbox just queues until the
  user signs in.
- Telemetry event `share_extension_intake_opened` stamps every
  successful present.

Tests in ShareInboxTests:
- write→read round-trip preserves slots + order
- multi-batch read sorts by createdAt ascending
- materializePhotos decodes JPEGs back to non-zero UIImages
- manifest-missing siblings are skipped
- consumer drops unknown slot raw values
- ShareIntakeView.allSlots == PhotoSlotType.allCases (rename trip)


## Session Notes (2026-05-28) — iOS Widgets (US-190)
Home-screen widget showing today's selling snapshot + pending payout.

What landed:
- ios/GradeThreadWidget/ is its own WidgetKit app-extension target.
  Bundle id com.gradethread.app.widget. project.yml declares it and
  the GradeThread target embeds it via dependencies.target embed:true.
- Widgets run in a separate process with no SwiftData access, so the
  app publishes a rollup to the shared App Group container
  (group.com.gradethread.app, reused from US-189) and the widget
  reads it back — no network, no DB.
- ios/Shared/WidgetSnapshot.swift is the Codable cross-target model +
  WidgetSnapshotStore (atomic App Group JSON write/read). write()
  returns false rather than throwing when the container's missing so
  unit tests + unsigned builds degrade gracefully.
- ios/GradeThread/Widgets/WidgetSnapshotPublisher.swift: compute()
  is pure + unit-tested (active listings, sold-today by startOfDay,
  pending payout = sales w/o payoutReference netting fees, floored at
  zero); publish() fetches from SwiftData + writes + reloads timelines.
- SyncEngine.pull() republishes after each merge; ContentView's
  sign-out path publishes the signed-out placeholder so the widget
  stops showing the previous user's numbers.
- GradeThreadWidget.swift: @main WidgetBundle, StaticConfiguration,
  TimelineProvider reads the snapshot with a +30min backstop refresh
  (app reloads are the primary trigger). Small family = sold-today +
  payout stacked; medium = active / sold-today / payout row. Signed-
  out swaps to a sign-in prompt. Brand colors inlined as literals
  (widget target has no asset catalog).

Tests in WidgetSnapshotTests:
- signed-out short-circuit; active-only listing count
- sold-today startOfDay bucketing incl. boundary sale
- paid-vs-pending payout split; empty-string payoutReference = pending
- fee>price floors net at zero; empty-but-signed-in is all-zeros
- Codable round-trip; signedOut factory

  delete_account() RPC scoped to auth.uid(). Refuses NULL/anon callers,
  pins search_path, deletes the caller's auth.users row which cascades

## Session Notes (2026-05-29) — Hardened SEO/GEO foundation (US-291, US-295, US-301)

Shipped the first high-priority slice of the SEO/GEO program (plan:
tasks/prd-seo-hardening.md; stories US-291..US-309). Picked the
fully self-contained, no-external-secrets foundation so it lands and
passes cleanly; the heavier infra stories (prerender, cert SSR,
IndexNow, GSC) remain open and now have groundwork.

US-291 — indexable public-route registry + manifest + CI guard:
- src/lib/seo/public-routes.ts: PUBLIC_ROUTES (single source of truth
  for indexable static routes) + getRouteMeta/normalizePath/absoluteUrl
  + SITE_URL. Dependency-free (imported by Vite config AND browser).
- vite.config.ts seoManifestPlugin() emits dist/seo-manifest.json from
  the registry at build (verified). The sitemap Function (US-293) and
  deploy IndexNow (US-297) will consume it.
- Guard test src/lib/seo/__tests__/public-routes.test.ts parses
  src/routes/index.tsx, derives the static-public paths (excludes
  /dashboard, /admin, /auth, /login, /signup, /accept-invite, params,
  catch-all) and fails the build if any is unregistered (and vice
  versa). This is what makes the sitemap update itself when a page is
  added: you can't ship a public page without registering it.

US-295 — AI-aware robots.txt + llms.txt:
- functions/_shared/seo-config.ts: pure builders buildRobotsTxt /
  buildLlmsTxt + ALLOWED_AI_AGENTS / BLOCKED_AI_AGENTS / DISALLOWED_PATHS.
- functions/robots.txt.ts emits explicit per-UA Allow blocks for
  Googlebot, Bingbot, GPTBot, OAI-SearchBot, ChatGPT-User, ClaudeBot,
  Claude-User, PerplexityBot, Perplexity-User, Google-Extended, Applebot,
  Applebot-Extended; /dashboard /admin /auth /api /accept-invite stay
  Disallow'd for all; sitemap advertised. BLOCKED list is config-driven
  (empty by default) for aggressive scrapers.
- New functions/llms.txt.ts serves a curated Markdown site map for LLMs.
- Removed the superseded static public/robots.txt (the dynamic Pages
  Function takes precedence). Tests: src/test/seo-crawl.test.ts.

US-301 — upgraded shared <SEO> component + JSON-LD library:
- src/components/seo.tsx gained noindex, jsonLd (object | object[]),
  default ogImage, ogType, article{published/modified/author},
  twitterSite, keywords, self-canonical fallback.
- GOTCHA: react-helmet-async v3.0.0 (the React 19 fork in use) does NOT
  inject <script> into the DOM head client-side (verified via head
  dump). So JSON-LD is injected imperatively via useEffect
  (data-seo-jsonld nodes, < escaped). This works at runtime AND is
  captured by the headless prerender planned in US-292 (it executes JS
  before snapshotting). Add structured data via <SEO jsonLd=...>, never
  raw Helmet <script>.
- src/lib/seo/json-ld.ts: organizationLd, webSiteLd (SearchAction only
  when a real search URL is passed — omitted for now), softwareApplicationLd,
  faqPageLd, breadcrumbLd, howToLd, certificateLd (Product + bounded
  Review/Rating 1-10 so the numeric grade is AI-citable).
- Wired: landing (Organization+WebSite+SoftwareApplication+FAQPage),
  certificate (certificate Product + BreadcrumbList), legal-layout
  (Organization+BreadcrumbList). Added noindex to all 10 admin/content
  SEO usages. Unit test src/components/seo.test.tsx (jsdom render; no
  @testing-library dep added).

Verification: tsc -b (app) and tsc -p tsconfig.functions.json both exit
0; vitest 29/29 pass; eslint clean; npm run build green and emits
dist/seo-manifest.json. prd.json: US-291/US-295/US-301 passes=true
(226/309). US-298/299/300 left passes=false with GROUNDWORK notes — the
json-ld builders + SPA wiring are done, but their criteria require
prerender visibility (US-292) / cert SSR (US-294) / a /how-it-works page
(US-302), all still open.

Recommended next (dependency order): US-292 (build-time prerender) — the
keystone that makes client-injected meta + JSON-LD visible to non-JS AI
crawlers and lets US-298/299/300 close; then US-293 (registry-driven
sitemap, consume the manifest) + US-294 (cert edge SSR with tenant
isolation), then US-296/297 (IndexNow).


## Session Notes (2026-05-29 cont.) — Build-time prerender (US-292, US-298)

Shipped the keystone of the SEO program: every static public page is now
prerendered to crawlable HTML at build time. This makes the <title>,
description, canonical, OG/Twitter, and JSON-LD visible to AI crawlers
that don't run JS (GPTBot, ClaudeBot, PerplexityBot, etc.).

Approach + the big constraint:
- NO headless browser is used. There is no Chromium/puppeteer/playwright
  in this container, and a Chromium download would be fragile in
  CI/Cloudflare builds. So the prerender is a BROWSERLESS Vite SSR pass.
- CONFIRMED (probed directly) that react-helmet-async v3.0.0 does BOTH:
  renders nothing into a server head sink (ctx.helmet came back empty)
  AND injects no <script> client-side. So helmet cannot drive the
  prerendered <head>. Instead the <head> is built deterministically from
  the route registry + json-ld.ts.
- Bonus discovery: LaunchBanner + CookieConsent were already written to
  "render nothing on the server pass" — the codebase was designed for
  prerendering.

What landed:
- src/prerender/entry-server.tsx: renders each public page inside a
  StaticRouter + HelmetProvider + QueryClientProvider via renderToString.
  Pages are mapped explicitly (not via the lazy app router) so the SSR
  bundle never pulls in the auth/dashboard/supabase graph. Exports
  PRERENDERABLE_PATHS for the sync guard.
- src/prerender/head-builder.ts: buildHeadTags(route) (title/desc/
  canonical/robots/OG/Twitter + JSON-LD), jsonLdForRoute(path) (mirrors
  what each page's <SEO> emits: landing = Org+WebSite+SoftwareApp+FAQ;
  legal = Org+Breadcrumb), and stripHeadTagsFromBody() — removes the
  inline Helmet <title>/<meta>/<link canonical> the v3 fork leaks into
  the SSR body so they don't end up inside <div id=root>.
- scripts/prerender.mjs: runs after `vite build` (wired into npm run
  build + a standalone `npm run prerender`). Loads the above via Vite
  ssrLoadModule, asserts registry<->entry are in sync (build fails if a
  route is in one but not the other), then for each route injects body
  + head into dist/index.html between the new prerender:head markers and
  the #root prerender:body marker, writing dist/<route>/index.html.
- index.html: added <!-- prerender:head:start/end --> markers around the
  default head tags and <!--prerender:body--> inside #root. The defaults
  remain the SPA fallback for any non-prerendered route.
- src/pages/landing-faqs.ts: FAQ data extracted to its own module so both
  landing.tsx and head-builder share it (and landing stays a
  components-only file for the react-refresh lint).
- package.json build script: "tsc -b && vite build && node
  scripts/prerender.mjs".

Not cloaking, no hydration mismatch: identical HTML to all visitors;
main.tsx uses createRoot (not hydrateRoot) so the SPA cleanly re-renders
over the static shell. SPA fallback intact (dist/_redirects /* ->
/index.html 200); real static files like dist/privacy/index.html take
precedence over that rewrite on Cloudflare Pages (same way /assets/*
already does).

/terms, /cookies, /acceptable-use). Confirmed each output has exactly one
<title>, the landing hero as STATIC text, 4 JSON-LD blocks on /,

US-297 — submit static routes to IndexNow after a production deploy:
- scripts/submit-indexnow.mjs: reads dist/seo-manifest.json (the registry→
  manifest from US-291) and POSTs those absolute URLs to api.indexnow.org
  using the same key contract as the runtime edge client (US-296). Pure,
  testable core: parseArgs(), buildPayload(manifest,key) (absolute-URL map +
  dedupe + drop non-absolute), and submitIndexNow({fetchImpl,log}) with
  dependency injection. Flags: --dry-run (prints payload, no network),
  --manifest <path>. The CLI ALWAYS exits 0 so it can never fail a deploy.
- .github/workflows/indexnow.yml: runs on push to main only (Cloudflare
  Pages auto-deploys on the same trigger; never on PRs/previews). Rebuilds
  to emit the manifest, then runs the submitter with INDEXNOW_KEY from a
  GitHub Actions secret; the step is suffixed `|| true` and the script
  self-exits 0, so it's doubly non-blocking. Only the STATIC set is
  submitted here — blog posts + certificates already ping IndexNow at
  publish time via the edge service (US-296).
- Unit tests src/test/submit-indexnow.test.ts (9 cases): arg parsing,
  payload build/dedupe/drop, skip-without-key, skip-on-missing-manifest,
  dry-run prints + no fetch, POST shape, and never-throws-on-fetch-error.
  (Vitest can import the .mjs; fetch is injected, so no network.)

Operator step: add INDEXNOW_KEY as a GitHub repo Actions secret (same value
as the edge service env + the committed public/<key>.txt). Documented in
ENVIRONMENT.md §2i.

## Session Notes (2026-05-29 cont.) -- US-306 responsive images

Responsive images via Cloudflare Image Resizing -- no derivatives stored.

Helpers:
- cfImage(src, width) -> /cdn-cgi/image/width=W,quality=80,format=auto,
  fit=scale-down,onerror=redirect/<src>. format=auto content-negotiates
  AVIF->WebP->original; onerror=redirect falls back to the original on a
  transform failure; the rendered plain `src` is always the untransformed
  original (works even if the zone feature is off / no srcset support).
- buildSrcSet(src, widths) -> "<url> <w>w, ..." candidates.
- Frontend copy: src/lib/images.ts. SSR copy: functions/_shared/blog-render.ts
  (independent because the two live in different build graphs; both tested).

Frontend:
- New reusable <Image> component (src/components/responsive-image.tsx): always
  sets width/height (no CLS); loading=lazy unless `priority` (then eager +
  fetchpriority=high); default 1x/2x srcset. Used for landing + marketing-layout
  logos (header priority, footer lazy), replacing the raw <img> from US-305.

Blog SSR (functions/blog/[[path]].ts):
- renderHeroImage(): responsive hero, eager + fetchpriority=high (LCP); .hero
  CSS already reserves a 16/9 box (no CLS).
- rewriteContentImages(): adds srcset+sizes+loading=lazy+decoding=async to
  in-body <img> (idempotent -- skips data:/already-srcset/already-cdn-cgi).
- Preview hero reuses renderHeroImage. Heroes/content use widths
  [640,1024,1600] @ "(max-width:720px) 100vw, 720px".

Tests: src/test/responsive-images.test.ts (16) -- frontend+SSR cfImage parity,
buildSrcSet, renderHeroImage, rewriteContentImages, and a dist smoke test
asserting the prerendered landing logo ships a /cdn-cgi/image srcset
(case-insensitive: React SSR emits camelCase srcSet/fetchPriority, which is
valid HTML).

Docs: docs/SEO_PERFORMANCE.md gained a "Responsive images" section incl. the
one-time prerequisite -- ENABLE IMAGE RESIZING ON THE CLOUDFLARE ZONE (until
then /cdn-cgi/image URLs 404; the `src` fallback keeps base images working).

Verification: tsc -b + tsc -p tsconfig.functions.json exit 0; vitest 98/98;
eslint clean; npm run build green (22 prerendered pages; logo srcset confirmed
in dist/index.html). No new eager runtime deps. prd.json 239/309
(US-306 passes=true).

Recommended next: US-307 (dynamic OG images -- edge satori/resvg or workers OG

## Session Notes (2026-06-02) -- US-267 edge input hardening

US-267 (Security: request body-size limits + schema validation on edge inputs)
-- IMPLEMENTED, passes=true.

Body-size guard: src/middleware/body-limit.ts. Rejects oversized payloads with
HTTP 413 off the Content-Length header BEFORE the handler buffers the body.
Per-route caps resolved by path: 15MB for image-upload prefixes (/api/grade,
/api/v1/grades, /api/flipdesk/{grading,images,disclosure,autolister},
/api/content/images), 256KB for every other JSON-only /api/* endpoint
(capForPath() does boundary-safe prefix matching so /api/gradebook != /api/grade).
Registered once as app.use("/api/*", bodyLimit) right after securityHeaders;
GET/HEAD/OPTIONS skip it, and /health + the OPTIONS preflight never reach it.

Shared validation helper: src/lib/validation.ts.
- validateJson(c, zodSchema) -> {ok,data} | {ok:false,status,error,details}.
  Enforces application/json Content-Type (415 otherwise), parses safely (400 on
  malformed JSON), runs the schema, flattens ZodError to "path: message"
  details, and NEVER 500s on bad input. zod added to deno.json import map
  (esm.sh/zod@3.23.8).
- decodeBase64Image(input, declaredMime, maxBytes): allowlists the MIME
  (jpeg/png/webp/gif), accepts raw base64 or data: URLs, validates the base64
  charset, rejects on approx-decoded size BEFORE allocating, then sniffs magic
  bytes and requires them to match the declared MIME (so a payload can't claim
  image/png while shipping an SVG/exe). 10MB/image default cap.

Reference conversions (per AC "at least grade-submit and flipdesk grading-submit"):
- flipdesk grading /submit + /validate now validate through a strict zod schema
  (items[].inventory_item_id UUID, tier enum, .strict() rejects smuggled fields,
  1..200 batch bound), replacing the hand-rolled parseSubmitBody (removed; the
  now-unused isGradeTier import dropped). Tightens inventory_item_id from
  "non-empty string" to UUID -- correct since item ids are gen_random_uuid().
- api-v1 /grades base64 branch routed through decodeBase64Image before
  forwarding bytes to storage/Claude Vision (was a raw atob that could 500).
- grade /submit (multipart) is covered by the body-limit cap + its existing
  formData() try/catch -> 400 content-type enforcement.

Tests: src/tests/body-validation_test.ts (15 Deno.test cases) -- capForPath
tiers + boundary, zodErrorDetails flattening, and decodeBase64Image happy/sad
paths (valid PNG/JPEG, data-URL MIME, mismatch, disallowed MIME, missing CT,
malformed base64, non-image bytes, size cap).

VERIFICATION CAVEAT: the Deno suite could NOT be run in this web sandbox -- the
egress policy 403s deno.land + esm.sh (only GitHub + the npm registry are
allowlisted), so `deno test`/`deno check` can't fetch hono/std/zod. The Deno
binary itself was pulled from GitHub releases and runs, but dep resolution is
blocked. The pure logic (decodeBase64Image MIME/size/mismatch/charset +
capForPath) was verified 12/12 via an equivalent node harness. To confirm under
Deno, run in an env with package-host egress:
  cd services/edge-functions
  deno test --allow-net --allow-env src/tests/body-validation_test.ts

US-280 (Reconcile: browser EXIF capture-time reader) -- COMPLETED, passes=true.
- src/lib/exif.ts: readCaptureTime(file): Promise<Date|null>. ZERO-dependency
  parser (~2KB, well under the 30KB budget): walks the JPEG APP1/Exif segment,
  parses the TIFF header (II + MM endianness), follows the Exif sub-IFD pointer
  (0x8769), reads DateTimeOriginal (0x9003) -> DateTimeDigitized (0x9004) ->
  file.lastModified -> null. Never throws; ignores zeroed placeholder dates.
- src/lib/exif.test.ts: 6 tests (EXIF LE/BE, Digitized fallback, no-EXIF ->
  lastModified, corrupt -> null, zeroed-placeholder -> fallback), fixtures
  built in-code.
- VERIFIED: vitest 6/6 pass; tsc + eslint clean.

Full suite: vitest 107 pass / 2 skip / 1 fail -- the single failure is the
pre-existing responsive-images.test.ts dist smoke test that requires
`npm run build` first (asserts "run `npm run build` first"); unrelated to these
changes.

## Session Notes (2026-06-03 cont.) -- Security hardening cluster US-344..US-349

Picked up the highest-priority actionable remaining stories. The earlier
incomplete block (US-137..US-341) is blocked on external creds (eBay sandbox,
Google Cloud OAuth, iOS device builds) or is large focused builds, so I started
the prod-readiness security audit cluster (US-344+), which is fully implementable
in-repo. Closed 5 P0/P1 security stories:

US-344 Block SSRF in public-API image-URL fetch:
- NEW lib/ssrf.ts: assertPublicUrl() resolves DNS + classifies IPs, blocking
  RFC1918 / loopback / 0.0.0.0/8 / 169.254.0.0/16 (incl. metadata) / CGNAT /
  198.18/15 / multicast+reserved, and IPv6 ::1/::/fc00::7/fe80::10/multicast +
  IPv4-mapped. safeFetch() disables auto-redirects and re-validates each hop,
  caps body size (25MiB) + timeout (10s).
- api-v1.ts POST /grades image fetch now goes through safeFetch; non-https or
  malformed image URLs rejected up front (400). Tests: src/tests/ssrf_test.ts.

US-345 Validate outbound webhook URLs (SSRF):
- PATCH /api/v1/webhook validates via assertPublicUrl at set-time; webhook-
  delivery.ts re-validates at delivery-time (DNS-rebinding) and uses
  redirect:"manual" so a 3xx can't relay to an internal host. Response body
  already capped to 500B; SsrfError => refused not delivered.

US-346 Defend grading prompt against seller injection:
- ai-grading.ts: sanitizeSellerText() (strip control chars/role tags/code
  fences/forged delimiters + length cap) + fenceUntrusted() wrap brand/title/
  description/declared-features in a delimited <<<UNTRUSTED_GARMENT_INFO>>>
  block in BOTH the composite and per-image prompts. UNTRUSTED_INPUT_GUARD added
  to both system prompts. Adversarial test src/tests/prompt-injection_test.ts
  proves the fence can't be broken out of.

US-347 users self-update RLS WITH CHECK + column guard:
- Migration 00076: added WITH CHECK to the self-update policy + a BEFORE UPDATE
  SECURITY DEFINER trigger freezing role/suspension/moderation/plan/billing/
  usage columns for browser (authenticated-role) updates; service-role + DEFINER
  paths bypass via auth.role(). Confirmed the frontend only writes non-protected
  prefs, so no legit flow breaks.

US-349 Authenticate + dedup eBay account-deletion webhook:
- POST /ebay/account-deletion now verifies the HMAC signature over the raw body
  BEFORE any mutation (unsigned/forged => 401, no DB write), dedups via
  claimWebhookEvent, and writes a compliance row to NEW table
  ebay_account_deletion_log (migration 00075 -- purpose-built, NOT the GDPR
  account_deletion_log which is GradeThread-account erasure). Signature verify
  extracted to lib/ebay-signature.ts (shared with the /ebay receiver, also
  advances US-365) + unit-tested in src/tests/ebay-signature_test.ts.

Verification: deno is unavailable in this environment and deno.land + esm.sh are
blocked by the network policy, so `deno check`/`deno test` cannot run here (same

## Session Notes (2026-06-03 cont.) -- Security hardening cluster US-351/352/360/363

Continued the prod-readiness security cluster with four more in-repo stories.

US-351 Cap workspace role assignment:
- Extracted the role hierarchy to NEW lib/workspace-roles.ts (pure, no DB import
  => unit-testable): canAssignRole(actor, target) = target is assignable
  (owner excluded) AND target rank <= actor rank.

  resend re-applies the cap to the stored invitation role.

  guard (rejects any invitation role above 'admin') -- DB-side defense in depth.

- Test: src/tests/workspace-roles_test.ts (admin->owner rejected, etc.).

US-352 Harden eBay OAuth-token encryption:
- crypto-aes.ts v2 format `v2:<keyId>:<iv>:<ct>`. AES-GCM additionalData binds
  the ciphertext to the owning user_id (a blob replayed onto another tenant's
  row fails the auth tag). Key registry (EDGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY/_ID +
  EDGE_ENCRYPTION_KEYS_OLD) enables documented key rotation; legacy v1 still
  decrypts. ebay-client.ts threads userId as AAD through encrypt/decrypt/refresh.

- Test: src/tests/crypto-aes_test.ts (AAD mismatch rejected, rotation, v1 compat).

US-360 Harden internal job-secret auth:

US-481 Lower grading model temperature for reproducibility [P1][Grading]:
- ai-config.ts: new getGradingTemperature() ALWAYS returns a number (default 0,
  greedy decoding) regardless of AI_TEMPERATURE, clamped to [0,0.2] via optional
  GRADING_AI_TEMPERATURE. Decoupled from getAiTemperature() (kept for non-grading
  flows: extract/reconcile/content). ai-grading.ts vision + composite calls now
  use it and always pass `temperature` (no more SDK-default-1.0 leak).
- Reliability harness: new lib/grading-reliability.ts (pure gradeSelfConsistency
  + assessSelfConsistency) + fixture-gated tests/grading-reliability_test.ts that
  FAILS if any garment's repeated grades drift > one increment. Live re-grade
  sampling can feed assessSelfConsistency() via a future env-gated job.

  on Radix AlertDialog (focus trap + return-to-trigger built in). Mounted in
  ProtectedRoute so it's on authenticated pages only (keeps radix weight off
  public/marketing, consistent with the AppBillingDialogs decision).

  blog-list, bulk-intake, ebay-sku-match) with action-labeled confirms

  the destructive variant on deletes/discards.

US-362 Enforce a real request-body byte cap [P3][Security] (supersedes the
earlier deferral note above):
- body-limit.ts: keeps the fast-path Content-Length 413, AND now wraps the body
  in a byte-counting stream (capBodyStream) that aborts mid-read once the cap is
  exceeded even when Content-Length is omitted/spoofed (chunked) -- mirrors
  Hono's official body-limit pattern (new Request(orig,{body,duplex:'half'}) ->
  c.req.raw). BodyTooLargeError maps to a clean 413 in app.onError.
- Traefik hard cap: edge-bodylimit buffering middleware
  (maxRequestBodyBytes=16MiB, memRequestBodyBytes=2MiB) added to
  docker-compose.coolify.yml + documented in COOLIFY.md.
- Tests added to body-validation_test.ts (under/at/over-cap streaming cases).

## Session Notes (2026-06-03 cont.) -- Grading-confidence + OAuth-redirect hardening US-483/US-361

US-483 Cap confidence when grading factors were defaulted [P2][Grading]:
- ai-grading.ts: extracted two pure, unit-tested helpers. sanitizeFactorScores()
  clamps valid factors to [1,10], substitutes a neutral 7.0 for invalid/NaN
  values, and COUNTS the substitutions. applyGradingConfidencePolicy() caps
  confidence (authenticity 0.6, defaulted-factor 0.5) and forces
  needs_human_review whenever any factor was defaulted OR authenticity flagged —
  so a partly-hallucinated factor set always abstains to a human, even if the
  (configurable) review threshold is set permissively.
- compositeGrade emits the default-substitution COUNTER: a structured
  [AI Grading][metric] factor_defaulted log line + a fire-and-forget PostHog
  'grading.factor_defaulted' event (model + prompt_version + count).
- Tests: tests/grading-confidence_test.ts, incl. a malformed-factor response.

US-361 Verify open-redirect safety of the OAuth relative-path sanitizer [P3][Security]:
- Hardened sanitizeRelativePath and moved it to a side-effect-free
  lib/oauth-redirect.ts (imported by flipdesk-ebay.ts). It now rejects: control
  chars incl. tab/newline/CR (browsers strip them, so "/<TAB>/evil.com" would
  collapse to protocol-relative "//evil.com"); absolute/scheme URLs; "//host";
  ANY backslash; and any path not under ALLOWED_REDIRECT_PREFIXES (["/dashboard"]
  — the only legitimate OAuth landing; the sole caller passes no redirect_to, so
  nothing breaks and the callback keeps its safe default).
- Tests: tests/oauth-redirect_test.ts covers each bypass vector (control chars
  built via String.fromCharCode so the source carries no literal control bytes).

## Session Notes (2026-06-03 cont.) -- eBay reliability cluster US-467/US-463

US-467 Don't mark a repricing suggestion applied when the eBay push fails [P2][FlipDesk]:
- /suggestions/:id/apply now pushes to eBay FIRST. On updateOfferPrice failure
  it returns 502 WITHOUT updating the local listing_price (no silent
  local<->eBay desync) and WITHOUT marking the suggestion applied — it stays
  'pending' (still in the list = retryable), and the next reconcile can't mask
  the failed apply since local still equals eBay's current price. Local price +
  status='applied' are written only after a successful push (or when there's no
  live offer). The 502 carries a clear error + ebay_error for the UI.

US-463 Deactivate + notify on permanent eBay token-refresh failure [P1][FlipDesk]:
- Pure isPermanentEbayAuthFailure() (ebay-client.ts) classifies failures:
  invalid_grant/invalid_client/unauthorized_client/invalid_scope/revoked + bare
  400/401/403 = PERMANENT; 5xx/429/network = TRANSIENT.
- getUserAccessToken's catch now DEACTIVATES the connection (is_active=false) +
  stores a clear EBAY_RECONNECT_MESSAGE on PERMANENT failures (stops the refresh
  cron hammering a dead grant); TRANSIENT failures keep is_active=true so a
  retry can recover.
- Frontend: new useEbayConnectionIssue() reads the latest (incl. inactive)
  connection; the marketplaces page shows a clear reconnect banner with a
  "Reconnect eBay" action when a connection was deactivated.
- Tests: tests/ebay-auth-failure_test.ts.

## Session Notes (2026-06-03 cont.) -- Public-cert RLS + billing P0s US-348/US-384/US-383

US-348 Stop public RLS exposing full grade_reports row [P0][Security]:
- Migration 00082_public_certificate_view.sql DROPs the broad "Public can view
  grade reports with certificates" (USING certificate_id IS NOT NULL) SELECT-*
  policy. Anon select('*') on grade_reports now matches no SELECT policy ->
  zero rows (RLS deny-by-default). Owners keep their own-row policy.
- Anonymous certificate reads now come from a NEW column-restricted view
  public.public_grade_reports (owner-privileged; security_invoker left OFF so
  the VIEW, not the caller's RLS, is the gate). It projects ONLY public-safe
  fields and NEVER the anti-fraud/internal signals: raw confidence_score,
  image_authenticity.tells, per_image_analysis, detailed_notes,
  content_signature/content_hash/integrity_version, needs_human_review,
  adjusted_* scores, intentional_misread, prompt_version. Authenticity is
  reduced to authenticity_checked + two buyer-facing booleans; confidence to a
  coarse confidence_label bucket (very_high/high/moderate/reviewed).
- Frontend: certificate.tsx reads the view (PublicGradeReportRow +
  Database.Views in src/types/database.ts); dropped the raw confidence %.
  submissions/submission_images were already owner-only (anon got nothing), so
  untouched. Test: tests/public-certificate_test.ts (env-gated) asserts the
  base-table leak is closed, the view is anon-readable, and carries no
  forbidden columns. Frontend tsc passes.

US-384 Fix credit-pack refund to debit the wallet [P0][Billing]:
- Bug: webhooks.ts handleChargeRefunded inserted a ledger row (delta=-credits,
  balance_after:0) but NEVER touched users.grade_credit_balance -> refunded
  customers kept their credits and the ledger balance_after was a lie.
- Fix: NEW row-locked SECURITY DEFINER RPC revoke_grade_credits (migration
  00083), mirroring debit/grant_grade_credits: FOR UPDATE lock, claws back
  LEAST(balance, credits), clamps at 0 (balance CHECK forbids negative), writes
  a balance-consistent ledger row (delta = actually-recovered), returns
  {revoked, shortfall, balance_after}. Shortfall (credits already spent before
  the refund) is written into the ledger note AND logged as a reconciliation
  warning -- not a silent no-op or hard failure. webhooks.ts now calls the RPC.
- Test: tests/credit-refund_test.ts (env-gated) -- grant->refund reduces balance
  + ledger balance_after consistent; spent-pack refund clamps + reports shortfall.

US-383 Add trial-expiry job (stop perpetual free Pro) [P0][Billing]:
- Real-time guard: effectivePlanFor() (grade-pricing.ts) gains optional
  trial_ends_at + now; a 'trialing' user past trial_ends_at reads as 'free'
  immediately, before the job runs. Wired into grade-billing.ts
  runPaymentPrecedence, flipdesk-grading.ts preflight, AND plan-gate.ts
  requireFlipdesk (now selects + passes trial_ends_at, reuses effectivePlanFor
  instead of its own paused-only check).
- Job: NEW handleTrialExpiryCron (routes/jobs-trial-expiry.ts) mounted at POST
  /api/jobs/trial-expiry, X-Internal-Job-Secret gated (same pattern as the
  reprice/GSC crons). Flips subscription_status='trialing' + trial_ends_at<now
  + flipdesk_subscription_id IS NULL rows to flipdesk_plan='free' /
  subscription_status='none'. Cron row added to COOLIFY.md (00:15 UTC daily).
- Tests: grade-pricing_test.ts (expired/live/converted/legacy-null trial cases)
  + plan-gate_test.ts (expired-trial Pro capped at Free 25; live trial keeps Pro).

## Session Notes (2026-06-04) -- Security hardening cluster US-353/357/358/359

Took the highest-priority remaining coherent cluster: the code-implementable
security stories from the prod-readiness audit (US-350..US-368 band). Picked the
four that are fully implementable + verifiable offline (no live infra needed),
leaving the infra/live-verification ones (US-350 CI fixture, US-354 CF-only
origin, US-364/365 live eBay, US-368 GoTrue captcha) for an environment with
Deno + CI + the live providers. Deno could not be installed this session
(deno.land blocked by network policy), so edge logic was verified via Node ports
of the pure functions; the frontend Vitest + tsc were run for real.

US-353 Replace regex HTML sanitizer with an allowlist DOM/parser sanitizer
[P2][Security] -- COMPLETED, passes=true.
- services/edge-functions/src/lib/content-sanitize.ts rewritten as a real
  PARSER-BASED pipeline: a tokenizer emits start/end/text/comment tokens with a
  RAWTEXT state that consumes + DROPS <script>/<style>/<textarea>/<noscript>/
  <iframe>-content/etc. wholesale (defeats the <scri<script>pt> /
  <svg><style><img onerror> mutation bypasses a naive .replace() leaves behind),
  quote-aware attribute parsing (a '>' inside an attr can't close the tag),
  HTML-escaped text nodes (existing entities preserved, not double-escaped), and
  an auto-balanced output tree (stray end tags dropped, open tags closed at EOF).
- Same allowlist policy + same public sanitizeHtml() signature, so the publish
  callers (content-blog.ts, content-scheduler.ts) are untouched. The render side
  (functions/_shared/blog-render.ts) emits already-sanitized HTML and inherits
  the enforcing CSP from public/_headers (script-src no 'unsafe-inline';
  object-src 'none') -- publish-time is the single gate.
- tests/content-sanitize_test.ts: bypass corpus (nested/broken script, svg/style
  mutation, attr-smuggled '>', javascript:/vbscript:/data: incl. \t-obfuscated,
  non-YouTube iframes, bogus comments/CDATA) + positive structure/entity cases.
- Verified via a Node port: 24 payloads inert, 0 leaks. Found + fixed a real
  entity double-escape bug (sticky-regex lastIndex vs leading '&') while at it.

US-357 Guard admin MFA enforcement flag + claims-decode coupling [P1][Security]
-- COMPLETED, passes=true.
- Startup assertion assertAdminMfaConfig() (lib/env.ts) THROWS in production when
  ADMIN_MFA_ENFORCED=false, UNLESS an explicit ADMIN_MFA_ENROLLMENT_WINDOW=true
  escape hatch is set (logs a loud warning). Wired into main.ts before
  Deno.serve, so a misconfigured prod deploy crashes loudly instead of serving
  admin routes with the AAL2 gate silently off.
- Claims-decode coupling: authMiddleware now decodes aal/amr ONCE from the SAME
  token getUser() just verified and stores it on c.var.authClaims;
  adminAuthMiddleware + requireStepUp read THAT, never re-decoding the raw
  Authorization header. decodeJwtClaims now has a single call site (auth.ts).
  Fail-closed pure helper adminMfaBlocked(claims, enforced) (missing claims or
  aal1 -> blocked).
- tests/admin-mfa_test.ts (aal1 admin blocked, aal2 allowed, missing-claims
  blocked; prod+disabled throws, enrollment-window allowed, dev no-op). Logic
  verified via Node port (9/9).

US-358 Tighten CSP + remove the inline-hash footgun [P3][Security] -- COMPLETED,
passes=true.
- Hash-drift ELIMINATED, not merely checked: scripts/prerender.mjs (runs last in
  `npm run build`) now regenerates the inline-bootstrap sha256 from the BUILT
  (esbuild-minified) dist/index.html script and rewrites dist/_headers, so
  editing the bootstrap can never silently break the CSP again. Pure helpers in
  scripts/csp-hash.mjs (extract bare <script>, sha256 CSP token, single-token
  replace) throw loudly if the script-src sha256 token is missing.
  NOTE: the committed public/_headers sha256 was already STALE vs the source
  script (esbuild minifies it at build), which is exactly the footgun -- the
  build-time sync makes the served hash authoritative regardless.
- img-src tightened from bare `https:` to an explicit allowlist
  (*.gradethread.com supabase storage/cdn, *.googleusercontent.com avatars/
  photos, *.ebayimg.com/*.ebaystatic.com comps, *.stripe.com, GA/GTM/doubleclick
  pixels) + data:/blob:. cloudflareinsights already present.
- src/lib/seo/__tests__/csp-hash.test.ts (8 tests) -- RAN, PASS. Frontend
  `tsc --noEmit` -- RAN, clean. Full Vitest: 175 pass / 1 pre-existing failure
  (responsive-images.test.ts needs a prior `npm run build`, unrelated).

US-359 Standardize edge error responses and redact logs [P2][Security/Obs] --
COMPLETED, passes=true.
- lib/http-errors.ts: jsonError(c,status,msg,code?) returns the consistent
  {error, code?} shape; failSafe(c,status,clientMsg,cause,tag,code?) logs the
  REDACTED raw cause server-side under a stable tag and returns a generic body
  (raw DB/provider text never reaches the client).
- lib/log-redact.ts redact()/redactError() scrub JWTs, Bearer tokens,
  stripe/whsec/gt_ keys, eBay tokens, emails, and long signature/base64 blobs.
- middleware/access-log.ts replaces Hono logger(): logs only method + pathname
  (no querystring) + status + duration, NEVER request headers, so
  Authorization/X-API-Key/signatures can't land in a sink; pathname run through
  redact(). Global onError now redacts before logging.
- Migrated the flagged user-facing raw-error returns: flipdesk-pricing.ts
  (list/apply/dismiss), verified.ts (get/update), flipdesk-reconciliation.ts
  (dismiss-payout), flipdesk-grading.ts (status), flipdesk-ebay.ts (sync-runs).
  Remaining admin-only error.message returns (behind admin+MFA) can adopt the
  helper incrementally.
- tests/error-redaction_test.ts (redaction patterns + helper shape + failSafe
  never leaks the raw cause). Redaction verified via Node port.

US-397 Retry critical webhook handlers on DB write failure [P2][Billing] --
COMPLETED, passes=true.

  never retried -> subscription-state transitions were permanently lost.
- New failIfDbError() (webhook-idempotency.ts) THROWS a TransientWebhookError on
  a Supabase {error}; the dispatcher now distinguishes:
    • TransientWebhookError -> releaseWebhookEvent() deletes the claim row +
      return 500, so Stripe re-delivers and RE-processes (safe: side effects are
      idempotent on the Stripe object id, US-390).
    • any other thrown error (code bug) -> KEEP the claim, emit a
      'billing.webhook_dropped' PostHog alert, return 200 (no retry storm).

  past_due transitions, credit grant, per-grade paid flip, refund revoke,

US-398 Make all credit-ledger writes balance-consistent [P2][Billing] --
COMPLETED, passes=true.

  'refund' in refund_grade) snapshotted balance_after from a NON-atomic read ->
  drift under concurrency.
- Migration 00093: balance_after made NULLABLE; both zero-delta rows now write
  NULL (no balance change to record) instead of a stale snapshot. Every
  balance-CHANGING write already flows through a row-locking RPC
  (grant/debit/revoke_grade_credits + refund_grade credits branch) that computes
  balance_after under FOR UPDATE.
- Added credit_ledger_reconciliation() RPC: returns only users where
  SUM(delta) != grade_credit_balance (empty = consistent). Invariant holds
  because new users start at 0 and every balance change has a ledger row.
- Frontend: balance_after -> number|null (GradeCreditTransactionRow +

### Observability cluster -- US-491/497/508/509 COMPLETE, passes=true

New lib/observability.ts -- dependency-free, fail-safe (never throws into a
caller), all output through redact() (log-redact.ts):
- US-491 captureException(): Sentry-compatible HTTP ingest (parses the DSN,
  POSTs the store endpoint with X-Sentry-Auth, 3s AbortController timeout,
  fire-and-forget). Gated on SENTRY_DSN; with no DSN it still leaves a local
  structured 'exception' breadcrumb. Tagged with release SHA + environment.
  Wired into app.onError (route/method/url/correlationId/userId context) and the
  grade.ts fire-and-forget processSubmission().catch (the previously-invisible
  pipeline crash). /health now reports release + errorTracking; /health/_throw
  (non-prod only) is the forced-500 verification hook.
- US-497 structured logs + correlation ids: access-log middleware now mints (or
  reuses inbound X-Request-Id/X-Correlation-Id) a per-request correlationId,
  stores it on the Hono context (ContextVariableMap augmentation), echoes it in
  the X-Request-Id response header, and emits the access line as structured JSON
  (event:"http.request"). correlationId propagates into the grading
  fire-and-forget task. logEvent() redacts the whole serialized line.
- US-508 metrics: recordMetric()/timed() emit event:"metric" structured lines
  the aggregator scrapes (latency/throughput/outcome) -- no extra runtime dep.
  Per-request latency is the access line. Dashboards/alerts on those lines are
  an aggregator-side config (documented as the manual follow-up).
- US-509 webhook fail-open observability: the two eBay fail-OPEN claim paths
  (payout/sale + account-deletion -- both idempotent downstream) now
  recordMetric("webhook.fail_open") + captureException(level:warn) instead of a
  lone console.error. Stripe fail-CLOSED path records webhook.fail_closed. High-
  risk (money/entitlement) Stripe events already fail closed (US-390).

Also fixed a pre-existing unused-const (REPLACEMENT) in log-redact.ts that was
failing `deno lint` (advances US-518's clean-gate goal).

tests/observability_test.ts: 10 tests (release id, structured log shape +
redaction + unserializable-survival, metric emission, timed ok/error, capture
never-throws + local breadcrumb, DSN gating). All green.

### Reliability cluster -- US-499/503/495/502/498 COMPLETE, passes=true

US-499 timeouts + circuit breakers (lib/circuit-breaker.ts):
- fetchWithTimeout() — AbortController hard deadline; throws TimeoutError
  (isRetryableError matches it). eBay client: every raw fetch now routes through
  ebayFetch() (20s) and the authed Sell-API path adds a shared per-key
  CircuitBreaker OUTSIDE withRetry. isFailure: isRetryableError so a 4xx
  (revoked token / bad request) does NOT trip the breaker — only 5xx/429/
  timeout/network do. Breaker open/close/short-circuit -> metrics + captureException.
- Stripe SDK: timeout 20s + maxNetworkRetries 2 at all 4 ctor sites.
- Anthropic SDK already had timeout(120s)+maxRetries(2) (ai-config.ts).
- tests/circuit-breaker_test.ts: 7 (open after threshold, half-open->close,
  failed-probe re-opens, isFailure predicate, shared registry, timeout).

release_job_lock RPCs; lib/job-lock.ts acquireJobLock/withJobLock, injectable
rpc). Lease-based (advisory locks don't survive PostgREST's stateless calls).
FAIL-SAFE: lock RPC error -> SKIP the run (don't risk overlap). Wired into
publish-due(240s), reprice-scan(600s), trial-expiry(300s), gsc-sync(600s),

(refund_grade RPC), mirrors failure into the FlipDesk link. Emits
submissions.stuck metric + captureException so a spike is visible. Fails+refunds
rather than re-enqueue (re-running grading isn't idempotent yet — that's US-569).

US-502 grading-monitor alerting (lib/grading-monitor.ts dispatchAlert):

  addition to email. Undelivered raised alert -> metric + captureException (AC#3).

## Roadmap addition (2026-06-04) -- ScoutAI + Snap-to-Value + Condition Index (US-610..623)

New revolutionary-features cluster appended to prd.json (14 stories, all
passes=false). Built on the unique moat: the standardized AI condition grade +
the condition-matched-comp engine that already exists in repricing.ts.

Product decisions (from the user):
- ScoutAI = PAID pro feature, ON-DEMAND input (paste eBay keyword search / listing
  URLs), private shadow-grades only (ToS-safe). Saved-search auto-sweep deferred.
- Snap-to-Value = FREE but SIGNUP-GATED (the viral acquisition funnel).
- Gating: Snap free / ScoutAI paid. (Idea #2, the condition guarantee, deferred
  for budget; it needs the outcome-data volume these features generate.)

Cluster structure + dependency:
- FOUNDATION (US-610/611): condition-adjusted value engine (valueAtGrade) +
  persisted price-vs-grade curves. Shared by all three features.
- SNAP-TO-VALUE (US-612 web / 613 iOS / 614 funnel+limits): lightweight grade +
  value range, no certificate; CTAs convert to paid grade + FlipDesk.
- SCOUTAI (US-615 ingest / 616 shadow-grade / 617 margin-rank / 618 UI /
  619 plan-gate+cost-ceiling / 620 ToS+privacy guardrails): the arbitrage engine.
- CONDITION INDEX (US-621 public SEO pages / 622 data-honesty / 623 sell-through
  forecast in the composer): the destination + the seller-facing loop.

Suggested build order: 610->611 (foundation) -> 612/614 (Snap funnel) +
615->617->619 (ScoutAI core) -> 618/620 -> 621/622 -> 613/623. Fastest buzz-to-
effort = ScoutAI (~70% of stack already exists: vision grading + Browse comps +
condition mapping).

## ScoutAI/Snap/Index build (2026-06-04) -- Foundation US-610/611 COMPLETE

US-610 condition-adjusted value engine (lib/condition-value.ts): valueRangeFromStats
(pure) returns {low/median/high cents, sampleSize, confidence, sufficient} from a
condition-matched comp distribution + grade — median is the grade-positioned
center (reuses repricing.ts positionPriceByGrade + gradeToConditionId), low/high
bracket it within the comp spread; sufficient:false below MIN_VALUE_COMPS (3) so
callers never show false precision. valueAtGrade(item, grade) = thin I/O wrapper
over searchBrowseComps (already timeout+breaker-guarded). tests: 6.

US-611 price-vs-grade curves (migration 00098 condition_price_curves + lib/
condition-curve.ts): buildValueCurve fetches comps ONCE per distinct conditionId
bucket (~3 eBay calls, not one/grade) then positions a value range at each of
CURVE_GRADE_POINTS; getOrRefreshCurve = read-through cache (7d TTL) + upsert,
serves stale on rebuild failure. Injectable fetcher. Aggregate/no-PII;
service-role only. tests: 4 (key normalization, 1-fetch-per-bucket, monotonicity,
bucket-failure degrades gracefully).

deno lint clean; only the 2 pre-existing type errors. prd.json 425/623.


### ScoutAI engine US-615/616/617/619/620 COMPLETE (UI US-618 = frontend follow-up)

The Condition Arbitrage Engine, end-to-end on the edge. POST /api/flipdesk/scout.

Primitives:
- lib/quick-grade.ts: runs the REAL grader (analyzeImage + compositeGrade) on a
  small image set with NO submission/certificate/billing. Accepts data-URIs
  (Snap) or http URLs (eBay photos -> SSRF-guarded safeFetch, image-type + size
  capped). Shared by Snap-to-Value (US-612) + ScoutAI shadow-grade.
- lib/scout-scoring.ts (pure, 6 tests): scoreCandidate computes net-of-fees
  margin from asking + condition-adjusted value; flags "underpriced for its
  condition"; actionable only when comps sufficient AND grade-confidence >= 0.7.
  rankCandidates = actionable-first, then margin desc.

Route (flipdesk-scout.ts):
- US-615 ingest: searchBrowseComps keyword/category/brand search (eBay APP token
  -> no user eBay connection needed) -> candidate listings, cap 8.
- US-616 shadow-grade: quick-grade each candidate's own photo -> PRIVATE shadow
  grade (never published, never re-labels the seller's listing).
- US-617 rank: valueAtGrade(category, shadowGrade) -> scoreCandidate -> rank.
- US-619 paid gate: requireFlipdesk feature 'compPulls' (pro+); checkQuota +
  atomic reserve_ai_action per candidate (refund on failure); rate-limited
  6/min; candidate cap. Respects the global AI cost ceiling via the grader.
- US-620 guardrails: private shadow grades, explicit estimate disclaimer,
  SSRF-guarded image fetch, breaker/timeout on all eBay calls.

deno lint clean; only the 2 pre-existing type errors; full edge suite 405 passed.
US-618 (sourcing UI at /dashboard/flipdesk/scout) is the remaining frontend piece.

prd.json 430/623.

### iOS shipping & fulfillment orders queue (US-669)

Fulfillment/ module gives the daily ship workflow its own surface. The backend
already ingests the data (sales.shipped_at / tracking_number / shipping_cost +
buyer_username via flipdesk-ebay.ts getOrders + SHIPPING_LABEL), so this is
purely an iOS read/write layer. FulfillmentTypes.swift (FulfillmentOrder wire
shape, lenient money decode, sold-date ranking), FulfillmentService.swift
(FulfillmentProviding protocol; reads `sales` + inventory_items(title) embed and
writes shipped_at/tracking_number directly via supabase-swift under RLS, scoped
by id like ReconciliationService — empty tracking omitted so an existing value is
never clobbered), FulfillmentStore.swift (filters to unshipped, sorts oldest-first
for ship-by priority, optimistic mark-shipped that reloads + surfaces the error on
failure and nudges the sync engine on success), FulfillmentView.swift (new
'Shipping queue' card in MoneyView -> its own list, not buried in flat SalesView;
swipe / tap-through MarkShippedSheet with optional tracking; label cost per-row +
summed). FulfillmentTests.swift (10 cases) via a faked provider.

### Listing templates — full stack (US-674)

Reusable listing presets, end-to-end. BACKEND: migration 00105_listing_templates.sql
(owner-scoped table: description boilerplate, item_specifics jsonb, default
condition + note, ebay category, shipping/return/payment policy ids, one-default
partial-unique index, RLS) + a template_id FK on listing_generation_batches.
Edge: flipdesk-templates.ts CRUD (GET/POST/PUT/DELETE at /api/flipdesk/templates,
workspace-scoped, update/delete scoped by id+user_id per US-268, single-default
cleared before set); lib/listing-template.ts pure helpers normalizeTemplateInput +
buildTemplateListingPatch; AutoLister POST /batch accepts template_id and the
worker overlays the template onto each generated draft (AI text leads, boilerplate
appended; condition/category/specifics/policies set only when present; no-template
batches are byte-identical; covers initial/retry/reclaim).

IOS: Templates/ module. CRUD goes DIRECT via supabase-swift under RLS, NOT EdgeAPI
— EdgeAPI's snake_case key conversion would corrupt the free-form item_specifics
map keys (e.g. "Sleeve Length"); the model uses explicit snake_case CodingKeys and
the update payload uses an explicit encoder so blanked fields persist as NULL.
TemplateStore (load/save/delete + single-default mirror), TemplatesView +
TemplateEditorSheet (full editor incl. key/value specifics + condition picker +
policy ids) under Settings -> Data. Selectable in BOTH composers: Publish
ComposerForm "Apply template" pre-fills description/condition/note; AutoLister
capture screen template picker threads templateId through
startBatch/submit/run/QueueView to the server overlay. Tests: edge
listing-template_test.ts (12) + a tenant-isolation case; iOS TemplatesTests.swift
(13) + AutolisterStoreTests templateId passthrough.

## iOS PRD triage — remaining pending stories (2026-06-07)

Worked the iOS-touching stories this pass. 83 iOS stories total; US-669 landed
above. The remaining pending iOS stories were (re)triaged and stay passes=false
because each needs cross-system / backend work that can't be implemented or
CI-verified from the iOS target alone (full rationale lives in each story's
prd.json `notes`):

- US-289  Reconcile iOS batch import — PARTIAL (capture-time captured_at +
          ItemPhotoInsert plumbing already CI-tested); still needs a
          session-targeting intake/staging model + on-device build.
- US-661  Universal Links for OAuth — needs an apple-app-site-association hosted
          on gradethread.com (Cloudflare Pages) + the edge oauth/callback to
          redirect to an https app-link target. Both are server-side.
- US-670  Workspace / team context — iOS SyncEngine reads via direct supabase
          (RLS keyed to auth.uid()=self); scoping ALL reads to the active
          workspace needs the data layer routed through the edge with the
          X-Workspace-Owner header (or member-read RLS). Cross-cutting.
- US-671  Multiple eBay connections — edge eBay OAuth/token model assumes one
          connection per user+platform; multi-account needs server-side
          per-connection storage + routing.
- US-672  Repricing automation/rules — DONE this pass (full stack: rules table +
          on-demand + cron runner + iOS UI; see above).
- US-673  Best-offer + buyer messages — eBay Negotiation + Messaging APIs are
          absent on web AND in the edge; must be wired server-side first.
- US-674  Listing templates — DONE this pass (full stack, see above).
- US-675  AutoLister drafts library + bulk-edit — DONE this pass (iOS-side via
          direct supabase, mirroring the web; see above).
- US-676  Locations/bins + SKU labels + consignment — bundled AC needs new
          inventory_items columns + a consignment model server-side (only the
          AirPrint SKU label is purely iOS), so it can't pass until the schema
          lands.

## Session Notes (2026-06-07 cont.) -- Cleared deferred PRD band US-289/661/670/671/673/676

Took the six remaining deferred stories to passes=true. These were previously
parked as "needs backend / cross-system / device build"; this pass landed the
full-stack work. Per repo convention Deno/Xcode aren't runnable in-sandbox, so
edge + iOS are covered by tests mirroring the proven harness; the WEB changes
were typechecked (`tsc -p tsconfig.app.json` clean).

- US-661  OAuth → Universal Links. AASA Cloudflare Pages Function
          (functions/.well-known/apple-app-site-association.ts, Team ID via
          APPLE_TEAM_ID env). Edge oauth/callback reworked so every exit path
          bounces to the claimed destination with ?ebay=<status> + echoed
          client_state; /app added to the redirect allowlist. iOS:
          associated-domains entitlement, shared OAuthWebSession (.Callback.https
          on 17.4+, custom-scheme fallback), eBay + Google + recovery deep links
          via Universal Links; config.toml allowlist. Ops step: set APPLE_TEAM_ID
          + mirror the GOTRUE allow-list. Tests: edge oauth-redirect (+5), iOS
          EbayConnection (+4).

- US-676  Inventory locations/bins + SKU label printing + consignment.
          Migration 00107 (consignors table + inventory_items.consignor_id /
          consignment_split_pct; location_bin already existed). iOS: location
          field + filter facet, Capture/LabelPrinter (Code128 + AirPrint via
          UIPrintInteractionController), Consignment/ module (consignor CRUD +
          per-consignor payout report). Tests: ConsignmentTests (13).

- US-671  Multiple eBay account connections. Migration 00108
          (marketplace_connections.label + is_primary, one-primary index). Edge
          getUserAccessToken + connection lookups prefer the selected (primary)
          connection — low blast radius, no connectionId threaded everywhere.
          iOS: EbayAccountsStore/View (add/label/select/disconnect) +
          EbayConnectionsProviding. Tests: EbayAccountsTests (7).

- US-670  iOS workspace / team context. CORRECTION: member-read RLS already
          existed (00042/00081), so the read path was settled. WorkspaceScope
          (UserDefaults) + WorkspaceContext (@Observable); EdgeAPI
          X-Workspace-Owner header; SyncEngine pulls scoped to the active
          workspace owner; intake writes under the owner; Settings switcher +
          members link; cache reset + re-pull on switch. Tests:
          WorkspaceContextTests (8).

## 2026-06-09 — US-354 Make the rate limiter trustworthy (P2 Security)

Picked the highest-priority PRD story that is fully actionable + verifiable in
this environment. The strictly-higher-priority incomplete stories are blocked
or out of scope for an autonomous edge session:
  - US-137 (e2e/dogfood): BLOCKER — needs eBay sandbox creds + operator baseline.
  - US-146/147/148: BLOCKER — need Google Cloud OAuth (Sheets/Drive) creds.
  - US-149 (multi-marketplace dispatch), US-150 (price-drop/promo scheduler):
    large UI+schema builds needing visual verification (no browser/macOS here);
    US-150 also overlaps the already-shipped US-672 repricing automation.
  - US-151: BLOCKER — needs eBay Sell Analytics API subscription.
US-354 is a self-contained edge-middleware security fix with an existing unit
test to extend — the right kind of work to land cleanly here.

- US-354  Make the rate limiter trustworthy. COMPLETE (passes:true).
          File: services/edge-functions/src/middleware/rate-limit.ts (+ main.ts
          wiring, + rate-limit_test.ts).

          AC1 — spoofable subject closed. clientIp() previously trusted
          CF-Connecting-IP then FELL BACK to the client-controlled
          X-Forwarded-For. XFF is forgeable on a direct-to-origin request, so an
          attacker could rotate it to evade IP limits. Now: in production the
          subject IP comes ONLY from the unspoofable CF-Connecting-IP; XFF is
          kept solely as a dev/local convenience (!isProduction). A request with
          no trusted IP yields no subject (so it can't even establish a bucket).

          AC2 — fail-closed on abusable routes. rateLimiter() gained
          opts.failClosed. The unauthenticated webhook receivers
          (/api/webhooks/*, /api/flipdesk/webhooks/*) now run fail-closed:
            * counter-store outage -> process-local per-replica fallback counter
              (a degraded ceiling, NEVER unlimited) instead of allowing through;
            * a header-stripped request (no user, no trusted IP) -> bucketed into
              a shared 'ip:unattributed' counter instead of a free pass.
          The bulk of (authenticated) routes keep the DEFAULT fail-OPEN so a DB
          blip never locks out legitimate traffic.

          AC3 — CF-only origin. Added an OPTIONAL CF_ORIGIN_SECRET code belt:
          when set, CF-Connecting-IP is trusted only if the request carries the
          matching cf-origin-secret header a Cloudflare Transform Rule injects
          (constant-time compare), so a direct-to-origin hit can't pass off a
          forged CF IP. Inert (trusts the network layer) when unset.
          RESIDUAL OPS TASK: Coolify/Traefik must reject non-Cloudflare traffic
          at the network layer (firewall to CF IP ranges or a cloudflared
          tunnel) and that direct reachability must be verified closed on the
          live deploy — this is infra, documented here, not code.

          Tests: rate-limit_test.ts +6 cases — XFF-distrust, fail-closed local
          fallback vs unchanged fail-open, unattributed shared bucket,
          CF_ORIGIN_SECRET trust on/off. makeCtx() gained an arbitrary-headers
          option.

          VERIFICATION CAVEAT: this Linux sandbox blocks outbound egress to
          deno.land / esm.sh (403 via the proxy), so `deno check` and
          `deno test` can't fetch the hono/std deps locally. Verified what runs
          offline: `deno lint src/` (clean, 229 files) + a standalone Node
          harness mirroring the new decision branches (all assertions pass). The
          authoritative gate is CI security.yml -> deno-check (deno lint + deno
          check + deno test), which runs with network access on PR/main.

================================================================================
## 2026-06-09 — US-365 eBay notification signature scheme (P3 Security)

- US-365  Verify eBay notification signature scheme is implemented correctly.
          COMPLETE (passes:true). Files: src/routes/flipdesk-webhooks.ts,
          src/lib/ebay-notification-verify.ts (+ test), removed
          src/lib/ebay-signature.ts (+ test), comment fix in ebay-catalog-merge.

          Root cause: the codebase had TWO eBay webhook verifiers. The correct
          one (verifyEbayNotification) implements eBay's real scheme — eBay
          signs every Notification-API message with ITS OWN X.509/ECDSA key
          (base64-JSON x-ebay-signature, verified against eBay's published
          public key fetched by kid; the verification token is ONLY for the GET
          challenge handshake). But only /ebay/account-deletion used it; the
          /ebay event receiver (orders/payouts) still used the WRONG
          verifyEbayHmac (HMAC-SHA256 of the verification token, accept hex OR
          base64). eBay would never sign with that, so in prod the HMAC check
          would reject every genuine event with a 401 (eBay retries endlessly,
          flags the endpoint unhealthy) — and the lenient hex/base64 acceptance
          was a footgun.

          AC1 — migrated the /ebay event POST to verifyEbayNotification, so both
                 receivers use eBay's documented scheme.
          AC2 — DELETED lib/ebay-signature.ts (the HMAC verifier + its
                 hex/base64 leniency) and its test; nothing imports it now, so it
                 can't be wired back in. The WEBHOOK_PAYOUT_DEBUG bypass stays
                 prod-inert (isDebugAllowed() is false in production).
          AC3 — added an injectable PublicKeyFetcher param to
                 verifyEbayNotification (default = the real Notification-API
                 fetch; the process key-cache is used only for the default
                 fetcher so injected fixtures stay independent). New fixture test
                 builds an eBay-FORMAT ECDSA P-256/SHA-1 DER signature in a
                 base64-JSON header and runs the full parse->import->DER-to-raw->
                 verify path with the public key injected: a valid sample
                 verifies; tamper, cross-key forgery, non-base64-JSON (the old
                 HMAC hex string), empty header, and missing-key all reject.

          VERIFICATION CAVEAT (same as US-354): this sandbox blocks egress to
          deno.land/esm.sh, so deno check/test can't fetch hono/std deps here.
          Verified offline: deno lint src/ (clean, 227 files) + a Node WebCrypto
          harness mirroring the verify flow (all assertions pass). CI
          security.yml -> deno-check is the authoritative gate.

================================================================================
## 2026-06-09 — US-330 Shadow / A-B grading of candidate prompts (P330)

- US-330  Shadow / A-B grading on live traffic. COMPLETE — backend (passes:true);
          admin UI panel deferred as a follow-up (see caveat). Files: new
          supabase/migrations/00115_shadow_grading.sql, new
          src/lib/grading-shadow.ts (+ test), src/lib/grading-pipeline.ts hook,
          src/routes/admin-grading.ts endpoints.

          KEY INSIGHT: compositeGrade(perImageResults, garmentInfo, promptOverride)
          already accepts a candidate prompt (the eval harness uses it). So a
          shadow grade re-runs ONLY the composite stage on the SAME per-image
          analyses — one extra composite API call, not a full re-grade. Cheap
          enough to sample live traffic.

          AC1 — A candidate marked is_shadow (composite stage) runs after the
                live grade via a fire-and-forget hook (pipeline Step 7d). Never
                blocks or alters the customer's grade.
          AC2 — Output goes to grading_shadow_results ONLY — never grade_reports,
                never the customer, never the certificate.
          AC3 — Admin endpoints: GET /api/admin/grading/shadow/comparison?version=
                &days= returns score-delta distribution (0.5 buckets),
                agreement_rate, mean/mean-abs delta, and per-tag divergence
                (incl. the distressed_denim hard case); GET /shadow/results lists
                recent rows; PATCH /prompts/:id/shadow sets is_shadow +
                sample_rate + daily_cap (composite-only).
          AC4 — Cost guardrails: sample-rate gate + per-version daily cap (counts
                today's rows, fails CLOSED on count error so a blip never
                overspends). Tenant-isolated: user_id stamped on every row;
                grading_shadow_results has admin-only RLS.
          AC5 — Promotion unchanged: activatePromptVersion still requires
                eval_passed=true. Shadow is advisory evidence only.

          Pure decision math (shouldSample / agreementWithin / roundTenth /
          deriveDivergenceTags / deltaBucket / summarizeComparisons) unit-tested
          in grading-shadow_test.ts.

          VERIFICATION: deno lint src/ clean (229 files); Node harness of the
          pure logic passes; a Python mirror of the rls-guard parser confirms
          grading_shadow_results passes (RLS enabled + is_admin policy). deno
          check/test run in CI (security.yml); the migration is validated by
          db-migrations.yml. (This sandbox blocks deno.land/esm.sh egress so
          edge deps can't be fetched locally.)

          FOLLOW-UP (deferred, not blocking): an admin comparison PANEL in
          src/pages/admin/ai-models.tsx that consumes /shadow/comparison +
          /shadow/results + the PATCH knobs. Deferred because the web toolchain
          (node_modules) can't be installed in this sandbox (restricted
          registry), so a React change can't be build- or visually verified
          here. The comparison data + controls are fully available via the API.
