## 2026-06-09 — US-414 Global Claude concurrency + cost ceiling (P1)

- US-414  Global Claude concurrency + cost ceiling. COMPLETE (passes:true).
          Files: new src/lib/ai-limiter.ts (+ test), src/lib/ai-config.ts
          (central wrap), src/lib/ai-grading.ts (comments only — calls now
          bounded centrally).

          Audit finding: grading-pipeline ran Promise.all over every image with
          NO global cap — a burst of concurrent grades/autolister batches could
          spike simultaneous Anthropic calls (rate-limit trips + cost spikes).

          lib/ai-limiter.ts:
            * Semaphore — process-wide FIFO counting semaphore
              (AI_MAX_CONCURRENCY, default 8), .run() releases on throw.
            * reserveGlobalDailyBudget — GLOBAL daily volume ceiling
              (AI_GLOBAL_DAILY_CALL_CEILING, default 50k; 0 disables) via the
              shared rate_limit_counters RPC (distributed across replicas);
              fails OPEN on a counter-store error so a DB blip never halts
              grading; throws AiCeilingError when exceeded.
            * runAiCall(fn) = ceiling -> semaphore -> withRetry(fn) with
              attempts = AI_MAX_RETRIES+1.

          AC1 (process-wide, ALL requests): wired in ONE place —
          getAnthropicClient() wraps messages.create, so every non-streaming
          Anthropic call in the whole edge service is bounded (grading,
          autolister, extract, reconcile, content). No per-call-site wiring to
          forget. Streaming (messages.stream / create({stream:true})) is
          bypassed so SSE content flows aren't broken/retried.
          AC3 (retry.ts on vision calls): retry.ts is the SINGLE retry layer for
          wrapped calls — the SDK's own per-call retry is disabled (maxRetries:0
          injected) so a 429 isn't retried twice.
          AC2 (per-account + global ceilings): GLOBAL daily ceiling added here;
          over-limit throws -> grading rethrows -> per-grade charge reversed.
          PER-ACCOUNT spend was already bounded — atomic monthly
          reserve_ai_action quota (US-387) for FlipDesk AI actions + per-grade
          billing/credits (US-207) for grading.

          DESIGN NOTE: chose a single central wrap in getAnthropicClient over
          wrapping ~16 call sites — DRY, future-proof (new callers bounded
          automatically), and avoids nested-runAiCall deadlocks. The override
          casts create's return to a Promise (not the SDK's APIPromise); safe
          because every call site awaits the result and none use APIPromise-only
          methods (.withResponse()). Streaming guard prevents wrapping a stream.

          VERIFICATION: deno lint src/ clean (231 files); Node harnesses for the
          Semaphore (concurrency bound/FIFO/release-on-throw), the ceiling
          decision (throws/disabled/fail-open), and the getAnthropicClient
          override (streaming bypass + maxRetries:0 injection + method
          shadowing) all pass. deno check/test run in CI (security.yml); this
          sandbox blocks deno.land/esm.sh egress so deps can't be fetched here.

================================================================================
## 2026-06-09 — US-456 eBay sync concurrency guard + stuck-run reaper (P1)

- US-456  Concurrency guard + stuck-run reaper for the eBay sync. COMPLETE
          (passes:true). Files: new supabase/migrations/00116_sync_run_concurrency.sql,
          new src/lib/sync-run-lock.ts (+ test), src/routes/flipdesk-ebay.ts,
          src/main.ts (cron mount).

          The 'running'-row plumbing already existed (startSyncRun/recordSyncRun
          with runId) but: (a) /listings/pull had NO guard against overlapping
          pulls, and (b) a throw AFTER the offer fetch (or a killed container)
          left the row stuck in 'running' forever — the UI's "syncing…" never
          cleared.

          AC1 — race-free lock. Migration 00116 adds a PARTIAL UNIQUE INDEX on
                flipdesk_sync_runs(user_id, marketplace) WHERE status='running'
                (after reaping any pre-existing running rows so the index can be
                built). claimSyncRun() inserts a 'running' row; a unique
                violation => already_running. /listings/pull claims before firing
                and returns 409 {alreadyRunning:true} on a concurrent pull.
          AC2 — finalize on every path. success/partial via recordSyncRun(runId);
                early fetch-failure via recordSyncRun(failed,runId); any other
                throw via the handler .catch -> failSyncRun(runId), a conditional
                UPDATE WHERE status='running' (idempotent, never clobbers a
                finalized run). doListingsPull no longer self-claims (startSyncRun
                removed) — the handler owns the lock lifecycle and passes runId.
          AC3 — reaper. reapStuckSyncRuns() flips runs stuck past
                SYNC_STUCK_THRESHOLD_MIN (default 15m) to 'failed'. Runs both
                opportunistically inside claimSyncRun (a crash can't lock a
                tenant out) AND on a new cron POST /api/jobs/sync-reaper
                (X-Internal-Job-Secret) so the UI unblocks with no new pull.

          Pure helpers isStuckRun()/stuckThresholdMs() unit-tested. Verified:
          deno lint src/ clean (233 files) + Node harness (stuck/threshold/
          claim-mapping). deno check/test in CI; migration validated by
          db-migrations.yml (reap-before-index ordering is safe on existing data).

================================================================================
## 2026-06-09 — US-462 Atomic reconciliation two-table writes (P2)

- US-462  Make reconciliation two-table writes atomic. COMPLETE (passes:true).
          Files: new supabase/migrations/00117_reconcile_payout_link.sql,
          src/routes/flipdesk-reconciliation.ts (/run + /match).

          /run and /match linked a payout to a sale with TWO separate writes —
          payout_imports(sale_id, reconciled) then sales(payout_reference). A
          failure of the second left a payout marked reconciled with the sale
          side never linked (a half-linked row that was only logged).

          AC1 — atomic via RPC. Migration 00117 adds SECURITY DEFINER
                reconcile_payout_link(p_user_id, p_payout_import_id, p_sale_id,
                p_payout_reference). A plpgsql function body is one transaction,
                so both writes commit together or roll back together. It
                re-verifies tenant ownership of BOTH rows (payout via user_id,
                sale via inventory_items.user_id since sales has no user_id —
                US-268) and locks both FOR UPDATE so concurrent reconcilers can't
                double-link. Both /run and /match now call it instead of two
                supabaseAdmin updates.
          AC2 — idempotent + self-repairing. Re-linking the same pair returns
                already_linked:true (re-running /run is a no-op); if a payout is
                already reconciled to this sale but the sale's payout_reference
                was never written (the old failure mode), the RPC backfills it.
                Conflicts (payout/sale linked elsewhere) return structured errors
                -> 409 (/match) or ambiguous++ (/run). service_role-only grant.

          VERIFICATION: deno lint src/ clean (233 files) + migration sanity
          (balanced $$, structure, grants). Atomicity is inherent to the plpgsql
          transaction. The RPC applies in db-migrations.yml; its tenant/atomicity
          behavior is exercised by the env-gated reconciliation cross-tenant case
          in tenant-isolation_test.ts (behavioral DB testing needs a live
          Postgres = CI, not this sandbox).

================================================================================
## 2026-06-09 — US-464 Idempotent eBay publish (P1)

- US-464  Make eBay publish idempotent. COMPLETE (passes:true). Files:
          src/lib/ebay-client.ts (publishOrAdoptOffer + export
          getPublishedListingId), src/routes/flipdesk-ebay.ts
          (publishItemForOwner step 4), src/tests/publish-idempotency_test.ts.

          The window: publishItemForOwner persisted the local listings row only
          AFTER publishOffer returned. A publish that succeeded remotely then
          crashed (or whose container died) before that write left an offer live
          on eBay with no local record — and a retry (manual re-publish or the
          publish-due cron) re-published it -> duplicate live listing.

          AC1/AC2 — new publishOrAdoptOffer(userId, offerId, ops) pre-checks
          getPublishedListingId BEFORE publishing; if the offer is already live
          it ADOPTS that listingId (skips publishOffer), so a retry reconciles to
          the existing listing instead of creating a duplicate. createOffer
          already adopted the existing OFFER (25002 path, US-528); this closes
          the publish step too.
          AC3 — publishOrAdoptOffer takes injectable I/O; publish-idempotency_test
          adds 3 cases including an explicit post-publish-crash + retry
          simulation (attempt 1 publishes + 'crashes' before persist; attempt 2
          adopts the same listingId; publishOffer called exactly once).

          Complements US-528 (publishOffer's intra-call reconciliation across its
          own retries). Verified: deno lint src/ clean (233 files) + Node harness
          of the 3 scenarios; deno check/test run in CI.

## 2026-06-11 — eBay/reconciliation batch (US-461, US-466)

- US-461  Include shipping-label cost in reconciliation matching. COMPLETE.
          Files: new services/edge-functions/src/lib/reconciliation-scoring.ts,
          new src/tests/reconciliation-net_test.ts, flipdesk-reconciliation.ts.
          The candidate-net used sale_price - platform_fees, ignoring the eBay
          shipping LABEL the seller buys (deducted from the payout) and the
          buyer-paid shipping (added) — it over-estimated free-shipping nets and
          mis-scored them. New pure saleNetEstimate(): payout_amount when enriched,
          else sale_price + shipping_collected - platform_fees - shipping_cost -
          payment_processing_fees, returning { net, estimated }. Estimated nets
          are flagged in the candidate reasons ("estimated net") and scored x0.85
          so a ground-truth enriched candidate outranks an estimate at equal
          delta. Both /queue + /auto-match selects share SALE_CANDIDATE_COLUMNS.
          6 deno unit cases (CI) + local vite-node sanity of the calc.

- US-466  Stop silent truncation of large eBay catalogs/histories. COMPLETE.
          File: services/edge-functions/src/lib/ebay-client.ts, flipdesk-ebay.ts.
          The three paginated fetches (inventory/orders/transactions) had fixed
          `i < 50/50/100` page loops that SILENTLY returned a partial set at the
          cap. Now: named ceilings raised to 50k/40k/100k rows, each loop tracks
          natural-completion vs ceiling-hit, and on a ceiling hit (with eBay's
          `total` saying more remains) pushes a structured truncationWarning()
          into the caller's sync-run errors. doListingsPull declares `errors`
          up-front and threads it into listAllOffers/listRecentOrders/
          listRecentTransactions, so a truncated sync is recorded + flips the run
          to "partial" (surfaced by the US-457 UI). deno lint clean (259 files).
          Pagination-to-ceiling itself is integration-level (CI/staging) — the
          module graph imports hono which deno.land blocks in this sandbox.

- US-459  Handle eBay returns and cancellations. COMPLETE (passes:true). Files:
          new supabase/migrations/00128_sync_run_reversals.sql,
          services/edge-functions/src/routes/flipdesk-ebay.ts, src/hooks/use-ebay.ts,
          src/pages/flipdesk/reconciliation.tsx.
          AC1 was already done (CANCELED/FULLY_REFUNDED orders mark the sales row
          cancelled/refunded and revert the item from 'sold' to 'listed'). ADDED:
          AC2 — net_profit now REVERSES revenue for cancelled/refunded sales
          (net = agg.gross - fees - shippingLabelCost, refund-netted; cost basis
          NOT subtracted since the item is retained, original revenue NOT counted;
          negative when the seller ate a label) rather than the full completed-sale
          formula. AC3 — new sales_reversed count (migration 00128) incremented per
          reversed line item, recorded by recordSyncRun and shown as a red "-N"
          (cancelled/returned) in the reconciliation sync-history. lint/tsc/eslint clean.

          `raw.title.slice(0,80)` in ai-listing.ts. Trims on word boundaries
          (never mid-word), strips dangling separators, hard-slices only a single
          over-long token as a last resort, and preserves the highest-priority
          leading keywords (brand → item type → modifiers). Logic validated
          (14/14 assertions). US-546 stays open for its remaining ACs (eBay
          search/autosuggest demand terms into the prompt; A/B title variants).

- US-473  Validate publish images (cap + reachability). COMPLETE. File:
          lib/publish-preflight.ts (dedupeAndCapImages, imageCapBlocker,
          checkImageReachability, reachabilityBlocker) + tests/publish-
          preflight_test.ts. toEbayImageUrls now delegates to the shared
          de-dup/cap helper. assemblePublishContext de-dups + caps to eBay's real
          24-image limit (the 12-cap was Trading-API-era) and pushes a fixable
          blocker when a set EXCEEDS the cap — lowest-priority shots are never
          silently dropped. publishItemForOwner HEAD-probes the image URLs
          pre-publish (headProbe + fetchWithTimeout, 5s) and converts a definitive
          404/410/403 into a 422 blocker; transient errors/timeouts are treated as
          reachable so a flaky CDN never blocks a legit publish. Image-count/URL
          issues now surface as blockers, not raw 502s (AC3).

- US-566  Harden publish pre-flight (condition + image cap). COMPLETE. New
          Taxonomy helper getItemConditionPolicies (ebay-client.ts) calls
          get_item_condition_policies and read-through caches the per-category
          conditionId allow-list in ebay_category_condition_policies (migration
          00129, same shared-cache/RLS model as ebay_category_aspects).
          assemblePublishContext validates the resolved condition against that
          allow-list via validateConditionForCategory (CONDITION_ENUM_TO_ID maps
          the Inventory-API enum → numeric conditionId); a disallowed condition
          becomes a fixable blocker naming what the category DOES accept.
          Unrestricted/unknown categories and any policy-fetch failure never block
          (best-effort). Image-cap pre-flight shared with US-473. Both surface as
          fixable blockers instead of raw eBay 25002/25019/25601.

  NOTE: edge `deno test` / `deno check` could not be run in this container (Deno
  not installed; outbound network blocked → can't fetch std/remote imports). The
  two new libs are PURE and were validated by porting the logic to Node
  (title-trim 14/14, preflight 15/15). Run `npm run verify:edge` on a Deno host
  before deploy.

## 2026-06-11 — Multi-quantity / multi-line eBay orders (US-468)

- US-468  Handle multi-quantity / multi-line eBay orders. COMPLETE. Migration
          00130 adds sales.quantity (unit count, CHECK >= 1) + sales.line_item_id
          and REPLACES the (inventory_item_id, platform_order_id) unique index
          with (inventory_item_id, platform_order_id, COALESCE(line_item_id,''))
          so distinct line items that resolve to the SAME inventory item in one
          order no longer collapse onto a single sale row (legacy null-line rows
          keep their old dedupe behavior). flipdesk_ebay_orphan_sales also gains
          a quantity column.

          New pure lib/ebay-order-lines.ts: normalizeUnitCount (eBay quantity ->
          positive int), pickSaleRowForLine (per-line dedupe selection with a
          one-time legacy-row adoption so the first post-migration re-sync doesn't
          duplicate), and perUnitPrice (display only). Unit-tested in
          tests/ebay-order-lines_test.ts (18 assertions, validated via Node port).

          doListingsPull now fetches ALL sale rows for the item+order, selects the
          right one per line item, and writes quantity + line_item_id;
          snapshotOrphanSale records quantity too.

          KEY CORRECTNESS NOTE: verified against the eBay Fulfillment API LineItem
          docs that `lineItemCost` is the EXTENDED line total (unit price ×
          quantity), NOT a per-unit price. So sale_price = lineItemCost is already
          the correct line revenue and is NEVER multiplied by quantity (doing so
          would double-count). Documented inline at both write sites to prevent a
          future "fix" from reintroducing the bug.

          (Same container caveat as the prior entry: edge deno test/check could
          not run here — Deno absent + network blocked. Pure logic validated by
          Node port; run `npm run verify:edge` + `supabase db reset` on a proper
          host before deploy.)

## 2026-06-11 — Link orphaned eBay listings to inventory (US-465)

- US-465  Let users link orphaned eBay listings to inventory. COMPLETE.
          AC2 (re-syncs don't resurrect a manual link): the two orphan-listing
          upserts in doListingsPull (REST inventory pass + Trading-API legacy
          pass) NO LONGER write match_status. Omitting it means the column
          default ('unmatched') applies only to brand-NEW orphan rows, while
          PostgREST preserves an EXISTING row's match_status + matched_item_id on
          conflict — so a manually-linked or ignored orphan is never flipped back
          to unmatched by a later sync. (matched_item_id was already omitted.)

          AC1 (link creates/updates the listings row): linking an orphan to an
          EXISTING FlipDesk item (quickLink + LinkItemDialog) previously only set
          match_status + matched_item_id on the orphan, leaving the item with no
          listings row for the live eBay listing. New shared
          upsertEbayListingRowForItem() in components/flipdesk/ebay-sku-match.tsx
          now also creates/updates the listings row (platform_listing_id,
          listing_url, listing_price, listing_status active) — mirroring what the
          create-new-item path (createItemFromListing) already did.

          AC3 (UI exposes the link action): already shipped — Create / Link /
          one-click "Link to this" / Ignore buttons on the Reconciliation
          SKU-match surface.

          NOTE: frontend couldn't be compile-verified here (node_modules absent,
          network blocked). All types were hand-checked against
          src/types/database.ts (ListingRow has every column used; ListingPlatform
          includes 'ebay'). Run `npm run verify:web` before deploy. The edge AC2
          change is pure omission of a field from an existing upsert — no schema
          change, no migration.

SESSION 2026-06-25 — open prd.json stories (iOS Launch/Production Readiness)
================================================================================
(iOS caveat as always: Swift/xcodebuild is macOS-only; code written to match
conventions, the no-ungated-print gate passes, iOS CI on a macOS runner is the
safety net. New file GradeApplication.swift auto-includes via XcodeGen folder
sources.)

- US-1208 iOS: offline UPDATE/DELETE replay must not run ahead of a pending
          CREATE (silent data loss). COMPLETE. SyncEngine.flushPending now
          computes unconfirmedCreateTargetIds() from the FULL queue (incl. stuck
          creates past maxRetries) and shouldDeferDependent() skips an UPDATE/
          DELETE whose CREATE for the same id is still queued — so the replay
          never runs `… WHERE id=?` against a row the server doesn't have (the
          PostgREST 200/0-rows case that silently dequeued + lost the edit).
          apply() now returns Bool, so a create confirmed earlier in the same
          pass removes its id from the blocked set and unblocks its dependents.
          +4 unit tests (dependent deferred while create pending / stuck create
          still blocks / unblocks once confirmed / independent edit never
          deferred) in SyncTests.

- US-1210 iOS: delta watermark must not advance past resiliently-dropped rows
          (permanent row loss). COMPLETE. paginatedFetch now returns a PagedFetch
          with per-page decoded vs dropped cursor lists (splitCursors scans the
          raw JSON and treats any row whose id isn't in the decoded set as
          dropped). safeCursor() advances the watermark only to the greatest
          decoded cursor strictly BEFORE the earliest dropped row (nil = hold), so
          an undecodable low-timestamp row keeps the cursor behind it and is
          re-fetched + re-decoded on the next delta pull instead of being skipped
          permanently. Wired for items/photos/sales/expenses/disputes; US-1003
          drop logging preserved. +3 unit tests.

- US-1211 iOS: Realtime follows the active workspace + catch-up on reconnect.
          COMPLETE. startRealtimeIfNeeded scopes the channel to
          WorkspaceScope.tenantOwnerId(selfId:) (not just self) so a member sees
          the owner workspace's live rows. RealtimeService.resubscribe(userId:)
          tears down + re-subscribes, called from .workspaceDidChange. Every
          transition INTO .subscribed (first subscribe AND every re-subscribe
          after a socket drop) kicks SyncEngine.pull() to reconcile events missed
          during the gap. .workspaceDidChange now flushPending()s FIRST (drains
          the prior workspace's queued writes — replayed scoped by their own
          payload ids — before reset/clear/re-home) so a queued mutation can't
          carry into the new workspace's first pass.

- US-1221 iOS: sync hygiene. PARTIAL (passes:false). DONE: AC3 consistent
          flush-then-pull order — sync() now flushes before pulling, matching the
          connectivity debouncer, closing the pull-then-flush resurrection/clobber
          window. AC4 replay-error classification — SyncReplayError.isTerminal
          (missingTarget / missingLocalFile / invalidStorageURL) routes to a new
          markStuck() that pins retryCount to maxRetries immediately, so terminal
          errors surface for manual attention without burning the 6 transient
          retries; notSignedIn / storageUpload stay transient. DEFERRED: AC1
          server-side delete/tombstone propagation (sales/expenses/listings/
          photos) and AC2 watermarking the listings full-pull — both need the
          latest-price derivation reshaped (delta + cached LocalListing) and
          prune-correctness device/CI-verified, not safe to do blind.

- US-1209 iOS: low-confidence (<0.75) grades not presented as certified.
          COMPLETE. GradeScale.gradeReviewConfidenceThreshold=0.75; new
          GradeApplication.stamp() holds back certificateURL + does NOT promote

## 2026-06-30 — Pre-launch audit batch (US-1418, US-1420) + CI unblock

Two HIGH pre-launch-audit stories, quality-first, plus a CI-blocking regression fix.

- US-1418 Price Suggestions error/failure state — NOW COMPLETE (passes:true).
  src/pages/price-suggestions.tsx migrated off the legacy useEffect/useState
  fetch (which ignored the { error } of all four Supabase calls and rendered a
  failed load as an empty "no suggestions" table) to useQuery + <QueryBoundary>,
  matching inventory.tsx. The queryFn THROWS on any { error } so an outage routes
  to <ErrorState> (retry = refetch, isRetrying = isFetching) instead of an empty
  table (AC2); the genuinely-empty case keeps its existing "No active inventory
  items" copy (AC3). The not-ready window (no workspace owner yet) is treated as
  loading, not empty.

- US-1420 Rate-limit + cache the public passport chain read — NOW COMPLETE
  (passes:true). main.ts adds a per-IP, fail-closed limiter on GET
  /api/passport/:slug (rateLimiter(20, 60s, "passport-read", { failClosed,
  methods:["GET"] })) consistent with the tag/claim budgets (AC1). GET-only +
  the single-segment :slug match means it never double-limits POST /claim or the
  multi-segment /tag/* and /garments/* paths (AC3). passport.ts adds a short
  per-replica in-memory TtlCache (30s TTL, bounded with eviction) that caches
  ONLY successful PII-free chain responses, so a same-slug flood is served from
  memory instead of re-running the ~6-query fan-out (AC2). Bogus-slug floods miss
  the cache and are governed by the limiter. New TtlCache unit coverage in
  tests/passport_test.ts.

- CI unblock (not a story): the latest main commit (US-1416) introduced a tsc
  error in src/pages/submission-detail.tsx — disputePhotos[i] is File|undefined
  under noUncheckedIndexedAccess. CI on main went red at e8aeb64. Added a
  `if (!photo) continue;` guard so `npx tsc -b` is clean again.

VERIFICATION (this container): frontend gate fully green — eslint (0 errors),
`npx tsc -b` clean, vitest 985 pass / 3 skipped with coverage above all floors
(stmts 75.5/72, branches 65.43/65, funcs 74.38/68, lines 77.4/72), `npm run
build` + bundle-budget guard OK. Edge: `deno lint` clean on the changed files;
the TtlCache class was type-checked and runtime-asserted standalone via deno.
`deno check`/`deno test` could NOT run here (egress policy blocks esm.sh +
deno.land/x, so hono/supabase/std don't resolve) — the new limiter line copies

## 2026-06-30 — Pre-launch audit batch 3 (US-1432, US-1436)

Two auth-error-handling stories (PR #147 already merged; this is a fresh branch
off the updated main per the merged-PR workflow). Both share a new helper.

- US-1432 Distinguish rate-limit / network errors from credential failures —
  NOW COMPLETE (passes:true). Login funneled every failure (incl. GoTrue 429 and
  offline) into the single SIGN_IN_FAILED_MESSAGE; signup collapsed every
  non-"already registered" error into one generic message. New src/lib/
  auth-error.ts exports classifyAuthFailure(err) → rate_limit (429 /
  over_request_rate_limit / over_email_send_rate_limit) | network (TypeError,
  AuthRetryableFetchError, status 0/502/503/504, fetch/network/timeout text) |
  credentials (the default, for genuine 400/401). LoginPage + SignupPage now
  surface distinct enumeration-safe messages (AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_MESSAGE /
  AUTH_NETWORK_ERROR_MESSAGE) for the first two and keep the generic credential
  message only for real auth failures. Test: auth-error.test.ts asserts each
  bucket, that rate_limit wins over a 4xx, and that unknown errors default to
  credentials (no false "you're offline").

- US-1436 Fail fast on an expired/invalid password-reset link — NOW COMPLETE
  (passes:true). readAuthError() (reads error/error_description from query AND
  hash) was extracted from auth-callback.tsx into the shared auth-error.ts (AC3
  reuse) — auth-callback now imports it, behavior unchanged. UpdatePasswordForm
  (reset-password.tsx) calls readAuthError() on mount and jumps straight to the
  expired/invalid state when the link carries an error, instead of spinning for
  the full 10s timeout; the timeout stays only as the no-session/no-error
  fallback (AC2).

## 2026-06-30 — Feature: edit & resubmit a published eBay listing from the web (US-1490)

User-requested (not from the audit backlog). The web composer already had eBay
category/specifics/condition editors + a publish flow; the missing piece was
editing an ALREADY-published listing and pushing the changes — including the
structured fields — back to eBay. (Verified via an Explore pass that capabilities
a–d already existed; only the post-publish edit/resubmit was absent.)

- EDGE: ebay-client.ts updateOfferFields() gains `categoryId` (the eBay leaf
  category lives on the offer, not the inventory item, so a category change must
  be re-asserted there). flipdesk-ebay.ts POST /listings/:id/revise gains a
  `resync_ebay_fields` flag: it widens the inventory-item re-PUT gate (which
  already rebuilds aspects from columns + auto-corrects condition against the
  category policy) and the offer-update gate (now pushing the lifted
  reviseCategoryId), and is folded into the at-least-one validation + the
  eBay-origin lock (category/condition/specifics are eBay-owned too).

- FRONTEND: useEbayReviseListing's ReviseListingPatch gains resync_ebay_fields.
  composer.tsx detects isLiveListing (listing_status=active + platform_offer_id)
  and renders a "live on eBay" banner + a "Save & resubmit to eBay" action in
  place of Publish. New saveLiveListing() updates the EXISTING active row in
  place (never inserts a draft, never flips listing_status/is_active) and mirrors
  category/aspects to the item WITHOUT regressing its 'listed' status;
  handleResubmitClick() saves then revises with { photos, resync_ebay_fields }.
  resolveListingFields() is shared by the draft + live save paths so they can't
  drift. item-canvas.tsx adds an "Edit eBay listing" overflow action for
  GradeThread-originated listed items (eBay-originated mirrors stay read-only and
  link out to eBay).

VERIFICATION (this container): frontend gate green — eslint (0 errors), tsc -b
clean, vitest 1008 pass, build + bundle-budget OK. Edge: deno lint clean on the
two changed files; deno check/test couldn't run (egress blocks esm.sh +
deno.land/x) — edge CI (security.yml deno-check) type-checks + tests it. eBay
API calls (offer category re-assert, inventory re-PUT) are exercised against the
sandbox by CI/manual QA, not in this container.

## 2026-07-01 (cont.) — US-1518 photo thumbnail tier — NOW COMPLETE (passes:true)
Normal iOS uploads never populated item_photos.thumbnail_url, so 56pt inventory
cells downloaded full ~450-500KB 1600px JPEGs (300-item scroll >100MB). Web upload
path ALREADY generates a 320w client thumbnail (photo-uploader.tsx) — the gap was
iOS + existing photos. Chose the EDGE JOB (AC1's sanctioned "or an edge job")
rather than a second client PUT: the iOS foreground uploader is deliberately ONE
PUT per photo against fragile self-hosted storage (aborts under concurrency — see
PhotoUploadService comments + memory), so adding a client thumbnail PUT is high-
risk and unverifiable on Windows. Server-side is verifiable + touches nothing
fragile. Implemented:
 - lib/thumbnail.ts: generateThumbnail() via imagescript (already a dep, used by
   defect-annotations) — decode → cap LONGEST edge to 320px → encodeJPEG(70).
   Re-encode from pixels = EXIF-free output (AC5). + thumbnailStoragePath() derives
   {owner}/{item}/thumbs/{name}.jpg (preserves owner folder for storage RLS).
 - routes/jobs-thumbnail-backfill.ts: POST /api/jobs/thumbnail-backfill (job-secret
   gated, job-lock, bounded 100/run, drain-to-zero via `remaining`). Finds
   item_photos WHERE thumbnail_url IS NULL AND photo_url != '' AND photo_type NOT
   IN (sensitive) → download (downloadItemPhoto, both buckets) → generate → upload
   to item-photos → write thumbnail_url + thumbnail_storage_path. Idempotent (AC2).
   SENSITIVE photos (tag/tag_2/certificate, private bucket, empty photo_url) SKIPPED
   so no public thumbnail for private imagery (AC3). getPublicUrl for item-photos.
   Covers BOTH backfill AND ongoing new-iOS-photo coverage. Registered in main.ts.
 - Doc: run via a Coolify scheduled task (curl the endpoint w/ X-Internal-Job-Secret,
   every ~5m; drain until remaining=0 for backfill). Documented in the handler header.
 - AC4: inventory rows/board cards already load thumbnail_url ?? photo_url (web +
   iOS InventoryRow.swift). No client change needed.
NO MIGRATION (thumbnail_url + thumbnail_storage_path columns already exist).

## 2026-07-02 (cont.) — US-1493 iOS tenant isolation: stale scope on sign-out + workspace-switch/sign-out sync race — COMPLETE (passes:true) — NO MIGRATION
Tenant-isolation class; one epoch mechanism closes the switch-mid-pull, sign-out
race, and access-revoked recovery.
 - (AC1) WorkspaceScope.clear() now runs in ContentView's .signedOut phase branch
   (so the next account on the device never inherits the previous user's
   X-Workspace-Owner → empty app / wrong tenant) AND in AuthStore.deleteAccount()
   (idempotent belt-and-suspenders vs the auth-stream race).
 - (AC2) SyncEngine gains a `scopeEpoch` bumped by `invalidateScope()`. pull()
   captures the epoch before the network fetch; on return, TWO guards
   (`pullResultApplies`) discard the payload if the epoch moved — one after
   pullRemote (skip the whole merge) and one after the merges (block the watermark
   advance + reconcile). So a workspace-A pull that lands after a switch to B never
   merges A rows into B's store and never poisons B's freshly-reset cursors.
 - (AC3) pull() while one is in flight no longer silently no-ops: it sets
   `pendingPullRequested` and the in-flight pull loops once more for the now-current
   scope (bounded — one extra pass after the last request).
 - (AC4) Sign-out captures the engine ref and bumps the epoch, so a late-returning
   pull discards instead of re-saving the old account's rows after the wipe.
 - invalidateScope() wired on .workspaceDidChange (covers both explicit switch and
   the access-revoked auto-switch, which posts that notification) and .signedOut.
 - (AC5) Tests (SyncTests): pullResultApplies same-epoch applies / changed-epoch
   discards (both directions) + invalidateScope-moves-epoch on a real in-memory
   engine (so the wiring can't silently regress). New DEBUG-only
   currentScopeEpochForTesting accessor.

no-ungated-print + no-bare-strings + no-default-shared-session OK. SourceKit "No such

## 2026-07-02 (cont.) — US-1495 iOS frozen/ghost items: dirty-flag never cleared + items never delete-reconciled — COMPLETE (passes:true) — NO MIGRATION
Two related offline-sync integrity bugs that produced the "stale data"/"my edit
disappeared" class of complaints. Files: SyncEngine.swift, SyncMergeActor.swift,
SyncTests.swift.

Bug 1 — sticky hasLocalChanges froze rows forever. The canvas sets
`hasLocalChanges=true` on an OFFLINE save (ItemCanvasView:636,1910) but only the
ONLINE path (:2185) ever cleared it. flushPending()/apply() dequeued the flushed
mutation without clearing the flag, so one offline edit froze the row: applyServerWins
(SyncMergeActor) kept the LOCAL value for every user-owned field on every subsequent
pull (web/member/AI edits never appeared on the device) and the row was exempt from
full-backfill prune (an immortal ghost if deleted server-side).
 - FIX: dirtiness is now defined by the pending queue. flushPending() tracks each
   inventory-item create/update it flushes this pass; after the loop it snapshots the
   queue (as late as possible, so an edit made DURING the flush keeps its flag) and,
   for every flushed item with NO remaining pending edit, calls the new
   SyncMergeActor.clearItemDirtyFlags(itemIds:) to clear hasLocalChanges. retryMutation()
   (manual Retry) clears the flag on the same path. New static helpers
   isInventoryItemEdit / itemIdsWithPendingEdits.

Bug 2 — inventory_items never delete-reconciled. reconcileDeletesIfDue covered
sales/expenses/listings/photos but NOT items, so an item deleted on web lingered on
iOS forever; a later iOS edit then UPDATE'd 0 rows, reported success and dequeued.
 - FIX: reconcileDeletesIfDue now fetches the surviving inventory_items id set
   (user-scoped) and passes it to SyncMergeActor.reconcileDeletes, which prunes local
   items absent from the server set. Guarded so it NEVER prunes a pending-create target
   (protectedIds) NOR a row with a genuinely-pending local edit (protectedItemIds =
   protectedIds ∪ itemIdsWithPendingEdits, plus a belt-and-suspenders !hasLocalChanges
   skip). nil id set (offline/fetch-fail) leaves items untouched — same partial-view
   guard the other tables use.
 - Tests: itemIdsWithPendingEdits kind filter; clearItemDirtyFlags unfreezes a row so
   the next pull applies the server edit (AC3) + leaves still-pending dirty rows alone;
   reconcile prunes a server-deleted item but keeps protected-create + dirty rows (AC2/4);
   nil item set leaves items untouched.
VERIFICATION (this host): brace/paren/bracket balance clean on the 3 touched files;
no-ungated-print + no-bare-strings + no-default-shared-session OK. SourceKit "No such
module" noise expected off-macOS. iOS CI (xcodebuild test on macOS) is the build +
unit-test gate. No migration.

## 2026-07-02 (cont.) — US-1496 iOS offline mutation-queue ordering + Retry safety + photo sort_order — COMPLETE (passes:true) — NO MIGRATION
Four related offline-queue integrity bugs (the impossible-to-reproduce "my edit
disappeared/reverted" reports). Files: SyncEngine.swift, PhotoUploadService.swift,
SyncTests.swift, PhotoUploadTests.swift.

(1) Same-target reorder. Two full-row updateInventoryItem snapshots A(old)→B(new) for
one item drain FIFO; if A failed transiently but B still flushed, the next pass replayed
A alone and reverted every field to the older edit. FIX: flushPending() now tracks a
blockedTargetIds set — when any mutation fails (transient OR stuck) its targetId is
blocked for the rest of the pass, so a later same-target mutation is HELD (new static
shouldHoldForBlockedTarget). B can't land ahead of A; either A succeeds then B flushes,
or A goes stuck (filtered out) and B (the newer full-row) wins — no revert, no loss.

(2) Retry bypassed the guards. retryMutation applied one snapshot directly, skipping
shouldDeferDependent + FIFO — Retry on an edit queued behind a stuck create UPDATE'd 0
rows, 'succeeded', and dequeued (silent permanent loss, the class US-1208 prevents).
FIX: retryMutation now clears the retry budget then routes through flushPending(), so the
create-deferral + same-target ordering guards apply (and the US-1495 dirty-flag clear
runs) — no out-of-order single apply.

(3) Photo replay dropped sort_order. replayUploadPhoto hardcoded sort_order 0, so a
replayed batch got nondeterministic strip order/cover and a lost-response replay upsert
clobbered a correct order back to 0. FIX: the queued uploadPhoto payload now carries
sort_order (threaded through enqueuePendingMutation ← handlePhotoLinkFailure /
handleNetworkFailure, sourced from the upload scheduling map) and the replay writes it.

(4) createListing replay used insert not upsert — a force-quit between server insert and
dequeue would replay a raw insert → PK 409 poison entry. FIX: replay switched to upsert
(dead replayInsert removed); dormant today (nothing enqueues createListing) but the
comment flags that any future enqueue MUST inject a client id to dedupe.

Tests: shouldHoldForBlockedTarget reorder scenario + nil-target never held; retry-behind-
stuck-create routes through the deferral guard; queued photo mutation carries sort_order.
VERIFICATION (this host): brace/paren/bracket balance clean on the 4 touched files;
no-ungated-print + no-bare-strings + no-default-shared-session OK. iOS CI (xcodebuild
test on macOS) is the build + unit-test gate. No migration.

Double-tap / re-entry guards on mutation flows that enqueued a bare Task with no

## 2026-07-02 (cont.) — US-1499 iOS sign-out robustness (local-first, no permanent spinner, cross-account cache purge) — COMPLETE (passes:true) — NO MIGRATION
Files: AuthStore.swift, ContentView.swift, Components/CachedThumbnail.swift.

Root cause: AuthStore.signOut() called `auth.signOut()` FIRST and only wiped local
state on success. supabase-swift propagates transport errors, so an offline sign-out
threw, the local wipe was skipped, phase stayed .signedIn, and ContentView set
signingOut=true and never reset it → a permanently-disabled Sign-out row with an eternal
ProgressView and zero feedback. Separately, the ThumbnailLoader disk URLCache was never
purged, so photo bytes persisted at rest across accounts on a shared device.

- (AC1) signOut() is now LOCAL-FIRST: clears keychain + AppleCredentialMonitor, drives
  `phase = .signedOut` (which tears the shell down to LoginView and runs the ContentView
  .signedOut cleanup) REGARDLESS of network, then does the server revoke best-effort
  (`try? await auth.signOut()`). Airplane-mode sign-out now fully signs out.
- (AC2) The Settings Sign-out button resets `signingOut` after the await (defense — with
  local-first the phase always changes and the view is torn down anyway, so the spinner
  can never stick). Sign-out is now infallible locally, so there's no failure state left
  to strand the UI; a residual error would surface on LoginView (which renders lastError).
- (AC3) ThumbnailLoader gained `purge()` (NSCache.removeAllObjects + URLCache
  .removeAllCachedResponses; retained the URLCache as a property to wipe disk bytes). It's
  called from the single ContentView `.signedOut` choke point, so EVERY sign-out path —
  explicit, account deletion, token-expiry, Apple-credential-revoke — purges the cache.
- (AC4) deleteAccount() also drives `phase = .signedOut` locally after the RPC succeeds
  (immediate UI transition + triggers the same cache purge); it still THROWS on RPC
  failure so DeleteAccountSheet keeps the user on the confirmation screen with a surfaced
  error (already wired). Account deletion is inherently server-first (can't delete
  offline), but the local teardown + UI transition are now immediate.

No unit test added: signOut/deleteAccount are coupled to the live Supabase SDK + keychain
(no clean injection seam; the existing AuthStore tests drive `apply(event:)` directly), and
the change is a local-first reordering + a cache purge. Verified by compile + code review;
existing AuthStore/auth-callback tests still exercise the surrounding surface.
VERIFICATION (this host): comment/string-aware brace balance clean on all 3 touched files;
no-ungated-print + no-bare-strings + no-default-shared-session OK. iOS CI is the build gate.
No migration.

## 2026-07-02 (cont.) — US-1500 iOS eBay category change in specifics editor now reaches eBay — COMPLETE (passes:true) — NO MIGRATION
File: Marketplaces/Listing/SpecificsEditorModel.swift.

SpecificsEditorModel.save() wrote only inventory_items.ebay_category_id + aspects and
called revise(syncPhotos:true) with no resync — it never touched

and even a later relist — stayed in the OLD category, and the aspect re-PUT validated
against the old category spec.

  detects a real category change.
- (AC1) On a category change, save() now also updates listings.platform_category_id for the
  item's GT-origin listing row(s) — `.eq(inventory_item_id).eq(listing_origin,'gradethread')`
  — covering draft AND active (so a later relist/publish uses the new category → AC4). RLS
  scopes the client update to the owner (frontend session, not service-role).
- (AC2) resyncSpecifics now forwards `resyncFields: categoryChanged` to
  EbayPublishService.revise (the `resyncFields`→`resync_ebay_fields` param already existed
  from US-1503) — so on a category change the offer-side category PUT runs and the aspect
  re-PUT validates against the NEW category spec (AC3). A non-category aspect-only edit still
  revises without the (heavier) resync, unchanged.
- A failure to mirror the category to the listing surfaces an explanatory error and returns
  false rather than a misleading success.

No unit test added: save() is coupled to the live Supabase SDK (the SpecificsEditorTests
cover only the pure helpers — missingRequired/merge/partition/storedSources). Verified by

US-1517 (memoize O(n^2) view derivations):
- NEW Components/SignatureCache.swift: @MainActor signature-gated memo cache held in @State.
  Unlike the US-967 onChange(initial:true) pattern it computes lazily on FIRST read, so there is

  build over items per rendered sale). Now one SaleJoins memo on MoneyView.titlesSignature,

  soldCount memoized in one Derived struct on a new MoneyView.rollupSignature (items ride on
  updatedAt - bumped by every local edit + sync merge; sales/expenses hash their rollup fields
  directly since they have no updatedAt; a day bucket keeps "this month"/aging fresh across
  midnight). Accessors cost one O(n) hash instead of full rollups with DateFormatters per body.

  per debounced query in .task(id:) + on table-count change (sync landing mid-search), stored in
  @State. Listing/sale hits carry their joined item (US-1187 orphan rule preserved). Old code

  seed, AnalyticsMetrics.priceBands).

  writes ONLY the changed slot's JPEG on a serial utility DispatchQueue; manifest rebuilt from
  the slot set (filenames are slot-deterministic). save()/clear() go queue-SYNC so explicit
  rewrites/discards order correctly behind pending deltas (clear can't be overtaken by a queued
  write resurrecting the draft). PhotoIntakeView autosave now calls update(old,new) - the old
  path clear()ed + synchronously rewrote EVERY staged JPEG on the MainActor per shutter press.
  flushWrites() test seam added.
- BackgroundRefreshService: both detections now run on a detached background ModelContext.

=== 2026-07-02 (session cont.): US-1541 (scale AutoLister intake to 600+ photos) ===
- NEW src/lib/upload-rate-limit.ts: SlidingWindowLimiter (paces request STARTS - reserves
  slots so N concurrent lanes share one window; 100/60s budget under the edge 120/60s cap),
  backoffDelayMs (server Retry-After preferred, else exponential 1s..30s, always +0-25%
  jitter), RateLimitedError, RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS=4. Server rateLimiter is fixed-window
  buckets, so equality-spaced schedules are safe.
- autolister.tsx: uploadStagingPhoto now converts HTTP 429 -> RateLimitedError (Retry-After
  parsed); new uploadStagingPhotoPaced wraps every upload with limiter.acquireDelayMs() +
  backoff retry loop (max 4 attempts; exhausted -> retryable error with honest copy).
  Aggregate header now reads "X of N uploaded - Y failed..." (was mislabeled done+error
  count), with an "All N uploaded." resting state.
- O(n^2) dHash freeze KILLED algorithmically: hammingHex rewritten from a per-bit BigInt
  loop (64 BigInt ops/pair -> seconds at 600 photos/180k pairs) to precomputed 2xu32 +
  SWAR popcount (~ns/pair, single-digit ms at 600); visualPairs parses each hash ONCE.
  Malformed 16-char hashes rejected by regex (parseInt would silently partial-parse).
  Idle-slicing/worker chunking deliberately NOT added - the constant dropped ~1000x, so
  there is no main-thread freeze left to slice (documented in prd notes).
- Windowed rendering: ungrouped grid mounts 105 tiles (divisible by the 3/5/7 breakpoint
  columns) and groups list 24 cards, each growing via IntersectionObserver LoadMoreSentinel
  (rootMargin 600px); per-file upload rows capped at 80, errors first, "+N more in the
  queue" footer, done rows summarized by the header. (VirtualList row-virtualizer didn't fit
  a responsive multi-column grid; windowing is the sanctioned "or equivalent".)
- localStorage persistence size-guarded: on quota error, retry with `original` (photo-edit
  undo) snapshots stripped; if still failing, warn once - staged photos remain safe on the

=== 2026-07-02 (session cont.): US-1542 (app-level background upload manager) ===
- NEW src/stores/autolister-upload-store.ts: the ENTIRE upload pipeline (dedup -> EXIF ->
  normalize -> worker-pool compress -> validate -> paced upload) lifted out of autolister.tsx
  into a Zustand store. StagedPhoto/UploadTask/StagedUploadResult/MIN_RESOLUTION + fileSig/
  sha256Hex/uploadStagingPhoto all moved; the page imports them back. In-app navigation no
  longer cancels uploads.
- Division of labor: page owns `staged` (grid/grouping/persist); store owns tasks + results.
  Page ATTACHES on mount (adopting the session; a NEW session while idle resets stale state),
  syncs staged identities for dedup (module-level non-reactive Sets: stagedSigs replaced by
  sync, pipelineSigs = inflight+succeeded claims released on failure), and CLAIMS results
  (deduped by id). While DETACHED, each finished photo is ALSO merged into the persisted
  localStorage session key (read-modify-write, deduped) so a hard reload mid-navigation
  cannot lose an uploaded photo.
- AC4 byte progress: transport switched fetch -> XHR (upload.onprogress); progress 30..100 is
  real byte fraction, throttled to >=2pt steps; keeps edgeFetch parity (edgeAuthHeaders, one
  forced-refresh 401 retry, 429 -> RateLimitedError into the US-1541 paced/backoff loop).
- AC2 global affordance: NEW components/flipdesk/upload-progress-pill.tsx in the dashboard
  sidebar (desktop + mobile sheet) - "Uploading X of N (· Y failed)" spinner or "N uploads
  failed - review", links to /dashboard/flipdesk/autolister, renders null when idle.
- AC3: module-level beforeunload guard while anything is in flight; `autolister:lostUploads`
  marker (count of not-done tasks, maintained by a store subscriber, cleared when settled);
  page mount toasts "N photos didn't finish uploading before the page closed" via
  consumeLostUploadCount (returns 0 on in-app returns where tasks still live).
- AC5: failed tasks keep their File IN THE STORE -> Retry works after navigating away and back.
- Batch summary toasts moved into the store (sonner Toaster is app-level, so a batch that
  finishes while browsing elsewhere still reports).
- ALSO FIXED (pre-existing, NOT mine): src/test/grid-image-lazy.test.ts was RED on HEAD -
  the concurrent agent's "thumbnail retry" commit wrapped the autolister grid <img> in
  StagedThumb, breaking the static needle (this is why that agent's push failed its pre-push
  hook). Guard updated: lazy-needle now targets StagedThumb's inner img (src={url}); new
  assertion walks every <StagedThumb> usage requiring itemPhotoThumb + thumbnail_url.

=== 2026-07-02 (loop): US-1528 (eBay Browse cross-reference for identification) ===
- NEW lib/identification-verify.ts, pure decision core + impure wrapper:
  * tokenizeTitle (keeps 501/lw7dvcs-style codes), scoreTitleAgreement (a title agrees
    when it contains >=60% of the style tokens; keywords = tokens recurring in >=40% of
    titles minus brand/style/color/size/stopwords, cap 8 - harvests colorways/fabric
    tech/fit descriptors for US-1529 title SEO).

    (cap 0.95); ZERO market hits for a supposedly-known product -> -0.2 (floor 0.05),

  honest character; linen wrinkles never a defect - plus the ELASTANE_CRITERIA

  label shows spandex at any percentage. dominantFiberClass picks by total pct with

=== 2026-07-02 (user request): US-1550 (AutoLister: sortable grid, bounded detection, group-every-N + range select) ===
USER BUG: a 600-photo dump auto-grouped into 2 merged singles + ONE 600-photo group,
and Ungroup appeared to scramble the original photo order.
ROOT CAUSE (two compounding, both in lib/autolister-grouping.ts):
- US-1540 filename-sequence seeding treated ANY contiguous run as one item; an
  EXIF-less export (HEIC/Live-Photo conversion strips capture time) is one 600-long
  contiguous run -> one seeded mega-cluster.
- The visual dHash pass (shared applyVisualSecondPass) unions clusters transitively
  with no bound; same-background clothing shots sit within dHash 10 of each other, so
  the union-find chained everything else together.
- "Scrambled order": US-1540 also removed the manual sort buttons and force-sorted the
  grid by provenance (time first) - with mixed/absent EXIF that order looks arbitrary.
FIX:
- seedSequenceClusters: runs longer than MAX_AUTO_GROUP_PHOTOS (12) seed per-photo
  clusters (order signal only), never one group.
- New applyBoundedVisualMerge (AutoLister-only; reconcile.tsx keeps the unbounded
  embed-based pass): closest pairs merge first; a merge is refused if it grows a group
  past MAX_AUTO_GROUP_PHOTOS or joins clusters > VISUAL_MERGE_ORDINAL_WINDOW (3) apart
  in shooting order. Time-gap clusters exempt from the cap (strong signal; the 30x20
  US-1541 fixture still groups exactly).
- autolister.tsx: persisted sort Select for the Ungrouped grid (Shooting order default /
  File name natural-compare / Date taken / Upload order = original order, recoverable
  after any Ungroup); "Group every N" chunks the grid in displayed order (undoable as
  one run, N persisted); shift-click range-selects between clicks in displayed order;
  "New group from selected" now orders by displayed order instead of click order.
TESTS: 4 new grouping regressions (40-photo mega-run capped; identical-hash 30-photo
chain capped; far-apart similar pair stays apart; nearby reshoot still merges).
65/65 vitest across grouping/group-edits/reconcile; tsc -b, eslint, full build +
prerender green. prd.json: US-1550 appended passes:true, nextId -> 1551. No migration.

=== 2026-07-03 (user bug, prod, follow-up): US-1552b (batch stall TRUE root cause: PostgREST rejects .or() on mutations) ===
The US-1552 claim logging paid off on the very next run: every claim errored with
42703 "column listing_generation_jobs.status does not exist". The column exists -
the failure is the .or() filter ON AN UPDATE: prod's older self-hosted PostgREST
rejects logical operators on mutations (update-CTE alias qualification), while the
newer local-stack PostgREST accepts them, so deno tests + verify:db never catch it.
All 44 jobs were still pending (nothing failed, no quota burned).
FIX: rewrote ALL FOUR or-on-mutation sites as sequential conditional updates
(pending-first, then stale-running):
- flipdesk-autolister.ts generation claim (the confirmed failure)
- flipdesk-autolister.ts publish-job claim (same shape; also now logs claim errors)
- flipdesk-ebay.ts scheduled-publish claim (same shape; now logs + counts skips)
- lib/user-events.ts touchLastActive (advance-only touch had been warn-failing on
  EVERY user event in prod - last_active_at was never advancing)
.or() on SELECT is unaffected and stays. Rule added to CLAUDE.md Gotchas.
VERIFIED: deno lint + check clean; full edge suite green (see below).
RECOVERY: redeploy edge -> queue page Resume picks up all pending jobs.

=== 2026-07-03 (loop): US-1557 (per-category confidence calibration, shadow-first) ===
The flat 0.75 review threshold becomes MEASURED per-category thresholds:
- NEW lib/confidence-calibration.ts (pure): every human review yields a (confidence,
  |AI overall - human-final overall|) pair; error event = off by >0.5. binReliability
  renders the curve; deriveThreshold picks the LOWEST t in [0.5, 0.9] whose shipped
  set (conf >= t, n >= 10) keeps the error rate at/under the 10% target; categories
  under N=50 pairs (documented minimum) or with hopeless data FALL BACK to the flat
  threshold - calibration can lower review load, never disable review.
- NEW weekly cron /api/jobs/confidence-calibration (job-secret + lock; CRON_REGISTRY
  + COOLIFY.md rows, Sun 13:00 UTC): mines 365d of human_reviews (latest per report;
  APPROVALS count as absError 0 - dropping them would bias every bin pessimistic),
  joins confidence + category, derives per category, persists to system_settings
  grading_confidence_calibration (thresholds + curves + sample sizes). The job NEVER
  flips enabled - preserving the operator flag across recomputes is the shadow-first
  guarantee.
- Grading path (composite confidence-policy site in ai-grading.ts): when
  setting.enabled AND the category has a real calibrated threshold, it replaces the
  flat term ONLY; defect-divergence and every confidence cap stay ORed in. While
  DISABLED (default), any decision the calibrated threshold would change emits a
  grading.calibration_shadow_delta telemetry event (AC4 - provable from data before
  it gates real reviews).
- Admin: GET /calibration-thresholds + CalibrationCard on /admin/grading
  (Enforcing/Shadow badge, per-category threshold vs flat + n + shipped error rate +
  text reliability curve); manual override + enable via the existing settings-registry
  editor (grading_confidence_calibration jsonb).
- DRIVE-BY, caught by the day-old skill discipline: admin grading.tsx had its OWN
  computeWeightedScore rounding the review-dialog preview to 0.5 - drift from the 0.1
  lockstep contract (reviews.tsx was correct). Fixed to 0.1; the grading-engine
  skill's rounding-sites reference now maps FOUR sites and records the catch.
TESTS: 6 new deno tests (bin rates; lowest-qualifying threshold; reliable category
earns LOWER-than-flat; under-sample fallback; hopeless-data fallback; resolver
clamp/fallback/case-insensitivity). FULL edge 2526/0/70; web tsc -b 0, eslint clean,
vitest 1121/1121. Held local; no migration (system_settings is row-data).

=== 2026-07-03 (loop): US-1558 (active-learning review-queue routing) ===

- NEW lib/review-info-value.ts (pure): information value = 0.4 x category novelty
  (golden-set + active-exemplar coverage; zero coverage = 1.0 decaying as labels
  accumulate) + 0.35 x calibration gap (confidence within +/-0.15 of the CATEGORY
  review threshold - wired to US-1557 thresholdForCategory with flat fallback; labels
  near the boundary refine the curve, labels far from it refine nothing) + 0.25 x

=== 2026-07-03 (loop v2): US-1523 ACs 2-4 (iOS auth hardening - AC1 stays macOS-gated) ===
- AC2 (transient vs no-session): SupabaseShared.AccessTokenResult
  (token/noSession/transient) + pure classifyTokenError - hop-bounded walk of the
  NSError underlying chain; NSURLErrorDomain anywhere = transient; unknowns lean
  noSession ON PURPOSE (the EdgeAPI server-401 + forced-refresh path is the
  authoritative expiry signal, and public endpoints must not fail on an exotic
  error misread as network). EdgeAPI's tokenProvider AND tokenRefresher are now
  throwing: a transient failure throws retryable .network - a request is NEVER
  sent unauthenticated on a blip at the 1h token boundary, and a blip during the
  one forced 401-refresh reads "connection problem, retry" instead of "session
  expired". Legacy String? shims kept for the ~30 non-Edge call sites (behavior
  unchanged). NEW AccessTokenClassificationTests (6 URLError codes, wrapped
  chains, non-network -> noSession, deep-chain termination).
- AC3 (plan-step leak): the pending flag was a DEVICE-level boolean - if A signed
  up and abandoned before confirming, the next account signing in on that device
  inherited A's one-time plan offer. markPendingEligibility(email:) now stamps
  the normalized signup email; resolvePending(userId:email:) attaches ONLY on
  match, PRESERVES pending on mismatch (A still gets their offer later on the
  family iPad), and consumes-without-attaching legacy unstamped flags. The Apple
  first-grant path skips the device-level hop entirely (markEligible(userId:) -
  the session exists at grant time). PlanSelectionStateTests rewritten - the old
  test_pendingResolvesToWhicheverUserSignsIn literally documented the bug; now
  replaced with leak-closure cases.
- AC4 (weak_password copy): verified ALREADY COVERED by US-1521 (three phrasing
  variants map to actionable requirements copy; FriendlyErrorCopyTests).

  deployment requests the scope but THIS token 403s (consented pre-grant), the
  client gets 501 code=reconnect_required with copy naming the fix, vs the
  US-1510 feature_unavailable dead-end when the deployment lacks the scope.
- iOS: reconnect_required maps onto featureUnavailable(detail:) so the server
  copy flows through; EligibleCheck.unavailable carries the detail; the
  send-offer sheet shows the reconnect instruction instead of an indefinite
  "not available yet". (The old == .unavailable comparisons became pattern
  matches; NegotiationTests updated.)
- 2 new edge tests (both body variants); full edge suite 2544/0/70. iOS static
  gates clean (no ungated prints / curly quotes); compile proof = iOS CI on push.
When the grant lands: re-add the two documented scope lines at getScopes
(US-1510 kept them ready), sellers re-consent at /oauth/start, and the runtime
flag + reconnect flow shipped here handles the transition automatically.

=== 2026-07-03 (loop v2): US-1306 (Android networking core - EdgeAPI) ===
The biggest foundation story: the 680-line iOS EdgeAPI actor ported with its
full behavioral contract, proven by 23 tests (13 of them MockWebServer
behavioral tests - a level of coverage the iOS original earned only over
months of incidents):
- Typed error model: all 11 iOS cases; discriminators BEAT status codes
  (workspace_access_revoked, email_unverified, feature_unavailable +
  reconnect_required, offer_not_open); 240-char body preview; actionable copy.
- Retry policy carried exactly (US-638/1145/1164): network + 429 retry any
  method (rejected-not-processed), 5xx only for idempotent methods (a POST 5xx
  may have applied - blind retry risks duplicate sales), <=2 attempts,
  exponential backoff w/ Retry-After hint, injectable sleeper for tests.
- ONE forced token refresh on a server 401 (US-1146); email_unverified proven
  to SKIP the futile refresh; the US-1523 transient-token contract holds (a
  blip THROWS retryable Network - never an unauthenticated request).
- Plan-gate (US-805): X-Plan-Warning parsed on ANY status; 402 body decodes
  the verbatim plan-gate.ts keys; both emit on buffered SharedFlows.
- TTL GET cache (LRU 64 entries / 4MB, oversized-refusal, clock-injectable);
  multipart image POST (no transient retry, same 401 refresh); shared 20s vs
  AI 120s OkHttp client profiles.
- DELIBERATE DEVIATION: raw OkHttp + kotlinx-serialization instead of
  Retrofit - the iOS surface is a generic path-based client used by ~40
  services; Retrofit's per-endpoint interfaces would fragment the port. The
  AC's intent (auth attach + single synchronous refresh) is implemented and
  TESTED in the send loop.
- Kotlin gotcha: while(true) in a lambda body doesn't infer Nothing - needs an
  explicit unreachable-throw tail.
VERIFIED LOCALLY: 23/23 networking tests in the results XML; three gates green.

=== 2026-07-05 (loop: non-android prd): US-1611 (Cron-fleet governance) ===
THE ASK: runtime health of the ~54-job schedule itself — the continuous DIFF
between CRON_REGISTRY (schedule) and cron_runs (reality). Missed-tick detection,
duration creep, maintenance-window suppression (no false alarms).
- NEW lib cron-fleet-governance.ts (PURE, fixture-tested) — REUSES the tested
  nextCronRun() so "expected" == what Coolify actually fires:
  - expectedTicks: enumerate a schedule's fire times in a window via nextCronRun
    (capped at 2000, surfaces `capped` — NO silent truncation).
  - detectMissedTicks: a slot [tick[i], tick[i+1]) is MISSED when no run landed
    in it and it fully elapsed. INTERVAL-AGNOSTIC (no fixed tolerance — the slot
    IS the tolerance). Maintenance windows over the slot start suppress the miss.
    Also computes consecutive_missed (a stall signal, recomputed cleanly).
  - detectDurationCreep: older-half baseline vs newer-half recent avg; creep when
    recent >1.5x baseline (>=6 samples, else inconclusive).
  - assembleCronFleetReport: per-job scorecard (skips one-offs/unrecorded);
    stalled (missed>0) / slow (creeping) lists + fleet verdict.
- TOOL get_cron_fleet_health: CRON_REGISTRY (in-code) + fetchCronRuns (existing)
  + NEW fetchMaintenanceIntervals (maintenance_windows overlapping the lookback;
  open-ended -> Infinity; .or() on a SELECT which is allowed per the CLAUDE.md
  gotcha). CHARTER + registered.
- DECISION (documented in 00367): a SMALL DEDICATED weekly agent (module J)
  rather than folding into Sentinel — Sentinel is 30m incident triage; fleet
  governance is a weekly schedule-vs-reality diff, a distinct concern.
- SEED migration 00367 (paused, L0, weekly, idempotent, self-records).
  EXPECTED_SCHEMA_VERSION 00366->00367. HELD.
- TESTS: cron-fleet-governance_test.ts (expected ticks, missed-tick no-runs/

=== 2026-07-05 (loop: non-android prd): US-1606 (Agent memory) ===
THE ASK: durable per-agent learnings assembled into context each run, deduped by
key, curated + pruned. THE CAPS ARE THE FEATURE (keep them tight). The
agent_memory table already exists (00357) — NO migration.
- NEW lib agent-memory.ts (PURE, fixture-tested):
  - selectMemoryForContext: strongest-first (weight desc, recency), stops at the
    row cap (8) OR token budget (800, ~chars/4) — always >=1 row.
  - formatMemoryBlock: SELF-DESCRIBING context block (explains what memory is +
    how to record a new one) so charters need not each repeat it (satisfies "the
    charter tells the model what memory is").
  - applyMemoryWrites: dedup-by-(kind,key) — UPDATE + bump weight (cap 10), never
    append; enforce the hard per-agent row cap (40) by evicting the weakest.
  - curateMemory: decay (0.8) + prune below the floor (0.3, a proxy for "unused
    for M runs" — a memory keeps strength only if re-written) + cap.
  - parseMemoryWrites: validate the model's {kind,key,content} array.
- KERNEL wiring (agent-kernel.ts, NON-BREAKING — memory only acts when present):
  AgentOutcome gains optional `memory`; parseAgentOutcome passes it through;
  KernelDeps gains loadMemory/persistMemory seams (prod impls + test-harness
  defaults so tests stay offline); runAgent injects the top-N memory block into
  the run context (empty -> prompt byte-identical); post-run persists the emitted
  writes (dedup + cap via the pure core).
- CURATION kernel-side (AC allows "job OR kernel-side"): chose kernel-side to
  AVOID the CRON_REGISTRY drift chain (COOLIFY.md/LAUNCH_CHECKLIST.md docs +
  drift test). agent-tick.ts runs runMemoryCuration weekly via a system_settings
  watermark (agents.memory_curation_last); pruning logged as agent.memory.pruned
  ops events. New lib agent-memory-curation.ts.
- MISSION CONTROL: admin-agents.ts GET /agents/:id/memory (read) + DELETE
  /agents/memory/:id (operator delete of a bad memory, audited).
- TESTS: agent-memory_test.ts (selection token+row caps, dedup update-not-append,
  cap eviction, malformed-ignored, curation decay/prune/cap, parse);
  agent-kernel_test.ts (memory load->inject into context + output->persist;
  no-memory -> byte-identical prompt); agent-tick_test.ts harness updated.
VERIFY: deno lint clean (10 files). deno test/check un-runnable locally (network
policy) — CI is the safety net. Validated ALL pure logic standalone in node:
selection caps, dedup+bump, cap eviction, curation decay/prune/cap reproduce
every expectation. Existing kernel/tick tests use field-level asserts (not exact
shape) so the new optional fields don't break them. NO migration.

=== 2026-07-05 (loop: non-android prd): US-1608 (Autonomy promotion) ===
THE ASK: earned L1->L2 autonomy per (agent, action-class) via approval track
record + eval gate; immediate demotion on rejection-spike/breach; hard caps
respected; history queryable. Built BEFORE its dependency US-1607 by consuming
the eval gate CONSERVATIVELY (absent eval data => not passing => never promote),
which US-1607 later populates (agents.eval_pass).
- NEW lib autonomy-promotion.ts (PURE, fixture-tested) — the EXACT §2 ladder rule:
  - computePromotionStats: per-(agent, action-class) from agent_proposals history;
    approved = approved+executed+failed (operator said yes), rejected; pending/
    expired ignored; approval_rate.
  - promotionEligibility: L1->L2 only; eligible iff >=20 approved AND >=90%
    approval AND evalPassing AND no breach in 14d AND not hard-capped. Returns
    reasons for each unmet condition.
  - shouldDemoteForRejections: >=6 recent decisions, >50% rejected -> demote.
  - Constants MIN_APPROVED=20 / MIN_APPROVAL_RATE=0.9 / BREACH_LOOKBACK 14d.
- NEW action class promote_autonomy (agent-policy.ts). Approving a promote_autonomy
  META-PROPOSAL flips agents.autonomy[target_class]=to_level (executeAutonomyPromotion
  in agent-proposals, re-reads autonomy so a concurrent grant isn't clobbered;
  emits agent.autonomy.promoted). Branch added to approveProposal alongside
  run_playbook.
- NEW pass autonomy-promotion-pass.ts (thin orchestration over the pure core):
  stats -> eligibility -> files L1->L2 meta-proposals (dedup vs pending) with
  {stats, eval_state} evidence + emits promotion_proposed; rejection-spike ->
  recordPolicyBreach (US-1586, the demotion path). Wired KERNEL-SIDE into
  agent-tick DAILY (watermark agents.autonomy_pass_last) to avoid the CRON drift
  chain. HARD CAPS reuse AUTONOMY_HARD_CAPS (trust-safety account actions never
  offered).
- HISTORY: Mission Control GET /agents/:id/autonomy-history (from ops_events:
  promotion_proposed / promoted / policy_breach, filtered by payload.agent_key).
- TESTS: autonomy-promotion_test.ts (stats, eligibility incl. every unmet

  exactly-threshold qualifies); agent-tick_test.ts harness updated.

policy) — CI is the safety net. Validated ALL pure logic standalone in node:

=== 2026-07-05 (loop: non-android prd): US-1613 (Agent-to-agent handoffs) ===
Modules E+N: findings become another agent's queued work, WITH PROVENANCE and
WITHOUT AUTHORITY. Support's ticket-cluster finding queues a Sentinel
investigation; Sentinel's vendor finding queues an Integrations Watchdog
deep-dive — cross-domain incidents connect without the operator as router.

Delivered (pure core + kernel seam, mirroring the US-1606 memory pattern):
- lib/agent-handoffs.ts — pure, fixture-tested:
    • parseHandoffIntents: validate the model's `handoffs` array ({target_agent,
      kind, payload, evidence}); payload/evidence are opaque INFORMATION.
    • routeHandoff: hop cap checked FIRST (a runaway chain stops even if accepted),
      then acceptance (target's accepts_handoffs_from). deliver → QueuedHandoff
      with the provenance chain extended by this agent; unaccepted / hop-capped →
      a downgrade admin task (the finding is never lost).
    • inheritedProvenance (deepest consumed chain), formatHandoffBlock (self-
      describing context block), selectHandoffsForContext (FIFO cap 10),
      summarizeConsumed. HOP_CAP=3.
- agent-kernel.ts: AgentOutcome.handoffs + parse pass-through; 5 KernelDeps seams
  (loadHandoffs / markHandoffsConsumed / loadHandoffPolicy / deliverHandoff /
  fileHandoffTask). Run START: consume queued handoffs → inject block, stamp
  consumed with run id, remember inherited provenance, record a `handoffs_consumed`
  transcript step + agent.handoff.consumed ops event. Run END: route each emitted
  handoff → deliver (agent.handoff.delivered) or downgrade to admin task
  (agent.handoff.downgraded). All best-effort — a handoff failure never fails the
  run; empty handoffs leave the prompt byte-identical.
- Wired examples (AC4): support-triage charter now HANDS clusters to sentinel
  (replacing the US-1595 tagged-task stopgap); sentinel charter accepts them +
  hands vendor_degradation to integrations-watchdog; integrations-watchdog charter
  accepts that deep-dive. accepts_handoffs_from seeded on both targets in 00372.
- Mission Control (AC5): consumed handoffs render via the existing run-transcript
  viewer (the `handoffs_consumed` output step) — no bespoke FE component needed —
  plus the three agent.handoff.* ops events.
- Migration 00372 (agent_handoffs table, deny-all RLS + rls-guard registration +
  the two config merges) + EXPECTED_SCHEMA_VERSION 00371→00372. Held.
- Tests: agent-handoffs_test.ts (parse, inheritance, deliver/unaccepted/hopcap
  routing, context helpers) + 3 kernel-seam tests (injected+consumed+delivered
  with extended provenance; unaccepted→task; hop-cap→task). Kernel test harness
  gained the 5 handoff seam defaults.

Design note (AC1/AC2): a handoff carries INFORMATION, never AUTHORITY — the
receiving agent still runs everything it DOES with one through its own proposal
path (kernel routes emitted proposals via applyWritePolicy exactly as before).
Provenance = the {agent, run_id} chain; hop = chain length.

      cohort ('trial_expiring_7d') server-side — marketing opt-outs excluded (reuses
      marketingOptedOutEmail), already-enrolled deduped, hard-capped 500 — and
      enrolls via the same idempotent upsert as the trial-drip tick (UNIQUE
      user_id,campaign 00274). The model names a cohort + campaign, NEVER a user.
      New drip variants route through file_task.
- agent-policy.ts: action class enroll_cohort.
- charter user-lifecycle-agent.ts (module U, weekly Mon 05:00, sonnet) + registered.
- Seed 00374 (paused, L0) + EXPECTED_SCHEMA_VERSION 00373→00374. Held.
- Tests: user-lifecycle_test.ts (stall/churn/winback/memo + PII-absence assertion)
  + 2 tool tests (stall via read tool + PII-absence on the serialized memo;
  enroll_cohort write-gated + cohort/campaign pass-through + invalid guard). fakeIO
  seams added.

The-cohort-rule (§3.U): the tool layer aggregates before the model sees anything;
all delivery still flows through the drip engine + the marketing-frequency caps —
this agent emails no one. No new HTTP route → no tenant-isolation_test.ts case; the
one write is id-scoped by a server-resolved whitelisted cohort, never model input.

Verification (honest): deno test un-runnable here (network policy 403s). Ran
`deno lint` (clean, 7 files) + a standalone Node reimplementation of the pure
logic — 15/15 passed. Scanned all new files for NUL (clean). prd-lint OK.

=== 2026-07-05 (loop: non-android prd): US-1651 (Web P2 — server-side list sort) ===
Global sort across ALL rows for the submissions (by grade) and inventory (by
status) lists — previously a client-side sort that only reordered the page in view.

Delivered:
- lib/list-sort.ts — pure, unit-tested (vitest): orderIdsByScore (nulls/ungraded
  ALWAYS last in both directions, ties broken by id for stable pagination),
  pageSlice, reorderByIds (restore order after an unordered .in() fetch).
- pages/submissions.tsx — grade sort now orders across the whole result set: fetch
  all filtered ids (id+status), resolve active grade_reports scores, order the id
  list globally (nulls last), slice the page, then materialize + reorder just the
  page's rows. count = full ordered length (pagination stays correct). The default
  created_at sort keeps its efficient single paged server-ordered query.
- pages/inventory.tsx — status sort is now server-side `.order("status")`. The
  item_status ENUM is defined (via BEFORE/AFTER ADD VALUE) in the exact
  ITEM_STATUSES lifecycle order, so Postgres enum ordering == the old client-side
  ITEM_STATUSES.indexOf() order — but global, and correct across pagination.

AC4 (PostgREST verification): the submissions fix deliberately AVOIDS embedded
foreign-table `.order()` — the version-sensitive feature CLAUDE.md warns diverges
between the local and self-hosted-prod PostgREST (here a FILTERED one-to-many embed,
the worst case). Instead it uses only bedrock supabase-js (select / .in / .order on
a real column / .range), which is prod-safe BY CONSTRUCTION — no prod-PostgREST
verification gate needed. The inventory fix orders by a real column (enum), also
bedrock. This is the documented fallback AC4 anticipates.

Verification (FULL — the web toolchain runs in this environment):
- `npx vitest run src/lib/list-sort.test.ts` → 9/9 passed.
- `npx tsc --noEmit` → clean (exit 0).
- `npx eslint` on all four touched files → clean (exit 0).
- `npm run build` → GREEN (tsc + vite + prerender, exit 0, 47 routes prerendered).

── Build health: cron-registry drift guard line-ending fix (2026-07-08) ──
- Investigated the lone edge-suite failure (cron-registry-drift_test.ts). Root
  cause: NOT a real bug — core.autocrlf=true smudges the LF-in-git COOLIFY.md /
  LAUNCH_CHECKLIST.md to CRLF in the Windows working tree; the test's marker
  regex was LF-only (`-->\n`) so it missed → "markers missing". Git stores both
  as LF, so Linux CI checks out LF and the test PASSED on CI the whole time.
- Fixed the TEST (not the docs) to be line-ending-agnostic: normalize CRLF->LF
  before matching. No doc change (git already has them correct). Edge suite now
  green locally too (was 3153/1, now 3154/0). Commit 38a6d404.
- Not a prd story; pending count unchanged (179).

── US-1749: Value Index comp-refresh scale + cost/quota guardrails (2026-07-08) ──
- Rewrote refreshConditionIndex (lib/condition-index.ts): was rebuild-EVERY-seed-
  every-run (cost scales linearly, no ceiling). Now config-driven via system_settings
  key `condition_index_refresh` (no migration; getSetting falls back to defaults):
  { enabled, maxSeedsPerRun, hotDays, coldDays, hotSampleThreshold }.

    older than their tier interval (hot = total_sample_size >= threshold → hotDays;
    long-tail → coldDays), oldest-first, capped at the per-run eBay call budget.

  • No silent truncation: budget-deferred seeds counted (deferred); post-refresh re-read
    logs any seed below MIN_INDEX_TOTAL_SAMPLE (condition-index.refresh.insufficient_samples
    with slugs). Per-run summary logged; cron_runs ledger + per-curve refreshed_at =
    heartbeat + last-refreshed observability.
  • Backoff = the existing eBay client circuit-breaker/timeout + the budget cap.

  detail (details: {...result}).

## 2026-07-09 — US-1812 (Rewards: confirm/dispute engine + grading feedback loop) — passes:true

The buyer's post-sale verdict — did the arrived item MATCH its grade? — is the
two-sided moat: one confirm/dispute event fans out to the grading accuracy loop,
the buyer's Trust Score, the SELLER's Grade Integrity, and the guarantee path.
- 00421_buyer_grade_confirmations.sql: EXTENDS grade_outcomes with buyer cols
  (buyer_user_id / buyer_purchase_id / seller_user_id / match_status /
  factor_deltas jsonb / overall_delta / dispute_reason / dispute_severity /
  prompt_version / guarantee_eligible / human_review_flagged), a UNIQUE partial
  idx on buyer_purchase_id (one verdict per purchase, upsert), a seller-scan idx,
  and a buyer-owner SELECT policy. NEW seller_grade_integrity cache (per-seller
  aggregate; seller/admin read, service-write) = the US-1912 substrate. US-1108
  triple + footer, EXPECTED_SCHEMA_VERSION=00421.
- lib/buyer-grade-confirmation.ts: PURE deriveDisputeSeverity /
  decideGuaranteeEligible / computeSellerIntegrityScore (smoothed 0–100, material
  disputes ×2, MIN_INTEGRITY_SAMPLE=5) + impure recordBuyerGradeOutcome
  orchestrator + recomputeSellerGradeIntegrity. Reuses claim-accuracy.ts
  ({defect,severity,location}→per-factor deltas). Confirmations emit a
  grade_confirmed reputation event (US-1817) + recompute Trust Score; a MATERIAL
  dispute flips grade_reports.needs_human_review (routes to the expert queue —
  never fabricates a human_reviews correction, grading-engine golden-set rule)
  and refreshes updatePromptVersionAccuracy. Material + eligible + in-window +
  over-threshold ⇒ guarantee_eligible (US-1821 claim intake picks it up).
- accuracy-tracking.ts: both grade_outcomes readers (public stats count +
  computeOutcomeFeedback) now .neq("source","buyer_arrival") so buyer verdicts
  don't pollute the seller-sale metrics.
- Route: POST /api/buyer/purchases/:id/confirm (ownership .eq id/user_id → 404;
  disputes require a reason or an issue). Tenant isolation: tenant-isolation_test

  database.ts GradeOutcomeRow buyer cols + BuyerGradeOutcomeRow projection.
